### [76] [A force model for dense granular flows and particle segregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09398)
*M. P. van Schrojenstein Lantman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文从单颗粒受力出发提出一种力模型（force model），指出稠密颗粒流（dense granular flow）中存在两个方向相反的阻力（drag force），并据此重新理解颗粒偏析（segregation）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密颗粒流在自然界与工业中普遍存在，但其颗粒偏析现象（因粒径、密度等性质差异导致颗粒分离）尚未被充分理解。本文试图从新的视角切入该问题。

Method: 基于单个颗粒上存在两个方向相反阻力的观察，构建单颗粒力模型；在单分散流（mono-disperse flow）中验证该模型，并将结果与文献中已知的颗粒标度律（scaling law）进行比较。

Result: 模型验证得到一族速度剖面（velocity profile），其中包含 Bagnold 剖面；与已有颗粒标度律的对比进一步支持模型有效性。模型表明偏析机制在稠密颗粒流中始终存在，而单分散流只是该机制不显著显现的特例。

Conclusion: 该研究为稠密颗粒流及其单个颗粒的行为提供了根本性的新思考方式，并为后续研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Dense granular flows are a common occurrence throughout nature and industry, but still exhibit phenomena that are not fully understood. One of these phenomena is the segregation of particles that constitute a dense granular flow, where particles separate due to differences in properties such as size and density. This work approaches segregation from a new perspective by developing a force model for a single particle based on the observation that there are two opposing drag forces acting on the particle. Validation of the model in mono-disperse flows reveals a family of velocity profiles, including a Bagnold profile. Further validation is done by comparing with known particle scaling laws in literature. The force model shows that the mechanism of segregation is always present in dense granular flows and that mono-disperse flows are a unique case where it is not distinctly visible. The insights in this paper prove a fundamental new way of thinking about dense granular flows and its individual particles, opening the field for new research directions.

</details>


### [77] [A Sharpened Entropy Principle for Two-Fluid Polymer Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10393)
*Dieter Bothe*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文将可压缩、非等温稀聚合物溶液（dilute polymer solution）表述为 Class-II 二元混合物（Class-II binary mixture），通过引入“平衡锚定公理（balance-anchoring axiom）”锐化熵利用，并给出熵不变的 Class-II 至 Class-I 约化，最终导出含热化学、构型应力与部分黏性应力扩散项的 Hookean 应力与温度方程；仅在单速度、不可压、等温极限下退化为 Oldroyd-B/UCM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于为可压缩、非等温稀聚合物溶液建立同时包含溶剂与聚合物各自质量、动量平衡以及混合物总能量与总熵平衡的一致混合物表述。摘要强调现有熵利用（entropy exploitation）需要被“锐化”，以明确二元耗散乘积中未闭合通量或源项的构成性因子来源。

Method: 方法上引入“平衡锚定公理”：在固定平衡代表元中，每个选定的二元耗散乘积都含有来自未闭合平衡通量或源的构成性因子。聚合物构型先由连接子分布（connector distribution）再用构型张量（conformation tensor）解析；由群体运动学（population kinematics）确定聚合物输运，并借矩（moments）得到双速度上随体速率（two-velocity upper-convected rate）。在总熵平衡内，当变形与应力分解权重匹配时，构型输运与变形功率同化学势与弹性偏应力（partial-stress）对应项相消；Gordon--Schowalter 检验独立要求给定哑铃自由能与 Kramers 应力下采用仿射上随体选择，除非引入额外可逆通道。

Result: 结果为：协调的应力—相互作用变化使局部熵通量/产生对相差一个散度，从而揭示局部机制式产生的表示依赖性（representation dependence）。熵不变的 Class-II 至 Class-I 约化选出保持母体产生的子代熵通量，并给出热化学、构型应力与部分黏性应力扩散项；被省略的二次相对惯性通量（quadratic relative-inertia flux）与相对动能存储及输运配对，属于可逆截断而非缺失熵产生。

Conclusion: 结论是所得可压缩非等温 Hookean 应力与温度方程仅在单速度、不可压与等温极限下退化为 Oldroyd-B/UCM，表明后者是该更一般框架的特例。摘要亦强调客观性（objectivity）用于验证协变性（covariance）而非选择协变性。

Abstract: A compressible, non-isothermal dilute polymer solution is formulated as a Class-II binary mixture with separate solvent and polymer mass and momentum balances and with total energy and entropy balances for the complete mixture. The entropy exploitation is sharpened by a balance-anchoring axiom introduced here: in a fixed balance representative, each selected binary dissipative product contains a constitutive factor from an unclosed balance flux or source.
  Polymer configuration is resolved first by a connector distribution and then by its conformation tensor. Population kinematics fix polymer transport and, by moments, yield a two-velocity upper-convected rate; objectivity verifies covariance rather than selecting it. Within the total entropy balance, configurational transport and deformation powers cancel their chemical-potential and elastic partial-stress counterparts when deformation and stress-decomposition weights match. A Gordon--Schowalter test independently requires the affine upper-convected choice for the stated dumbbell free energy and Kramers stress unless an additional reversible channel is supplied. Coordinated stress--interaction changes shift the local entropy flux/production pair by a divergence, exposing representation dependence of local mechanism-wise production.
  An entropy-invariant Class-II-to-Class-I reduction selects a descendant entropy flux preserving the parent production and yields thermo-chemical, configurational-stress and partial-viscous-stress diffusion terms. The omitted quadratic relative-inertia flux is paired with relative kinetic-energy storage and transport and is a reversible truncation, not missing entropy production. The resulting compressible non-isothermal Hookean stress and temperature equations reduce to Oldroyd-B/UCM only after one-velocity, incompressible and isothermal limits.

</details>


### [78] [Comprehensive molecular dynamics study of the dynamical properties of a dense binary hard-sphere mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09532)
*Sabry G. Moustafa,Andrew J. Schultz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，系统研究二元硬球流体（binary hard-sphere fluid）在宽堆积分数（packing fraction）范围内的动力学性质，并给出有限尺寸效应、超阿伦尼乌斯（super-Arrhenius）行为及动力学异质性的定量基准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密无序体系（dense disordered systems）中玻璃态动力学（glassy dynamics）的理论模型缺乏严格的基准数据，且自扩散系数（self-diffusivity）与剪切黏度（shear viscosity）的有限尺寸效应及临界堆积分数（critical packing fraction）估计存在争议。

Method: 采用二元硬球流体模型，在堆积分数 $φ≈0.357-0.582$ 范围内开展大规模分子动力学模拟；利用高效实现的 Einstein–Helfand 方法计算自扩散系数 $D$ 与剪切黏度 $η$，并将数据外推至热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）。同时分析 van Hove 自关联函数 $G_s(r,τ)$ 与四点动力学磁化率（four-point dynamic susceptibility）$χ_4(τ)$。

Result: $D$ 的有限尺寸效应按 $1/N^α$ 缩放，$α$ 随 $φ$ 由约 $1/3$ 增至 $3/4$；$η$ 的有限尺寸效应除 $φ≳0.554$（按 $1/N$ 缩放）外可忽略。外推所得 $D_∞$ 与 $η_∞$ 在稠密态呈超阿伦尼乌斯依赖，且 Stokes–Einstein 关系（Stokes–Einstein relation）失效；指数拟合无法可靠估计 $φ_0$，$η$ 与结构松弛时间 $τ_α$ 的正比假设亦不成立。$φ≳0.570$ 时 $G_s(r,τ)$ 呈空间指数衰减，衰减长度满足 $λ∼τ^ν$，$ν≈1/3$。

Conclusion: 研究结果为玻璃态动力学的计算研究提供了严格的 MD 基准数据，并为稠密无序体系理论模型的检验建立了参照；同时表明常用经验关系（如 $η∝τ_α$、Stokes–Einstein 关系）在该体系中并不适用。

Abstract: We present an extensive molecular dynamics (MD) study of the dynamical properties of a binary hard-sphere fluid over a wide range of packing fractions, $φ\approx 0.357-0.582$. The self-diffusivity, $D$, and shear viscosity, $η$, are computed using an efficient implementation of the Einstein--Helfand method. The finite-size effects in $D$ scale as $1/N^α$, with $α$ increasing from approximately $1/3$ to $3/4$ with increasing $φ$, whereas those in $η$ are negligible except for $φ\gtrsim 0.554$, where they scale as $1/N$. The data are then extrapolated to the thermodynamic limit to obtain $D_{\infty}$ and $η_{\infty}$. Both coefficients show a super-Arrhenius dependence on $φ$ for dense states, accompanied by a breakdown of the Stokes--Einstein relation. Although both $D_{\infty}(φ)$ and $η_{\infty}(φ)$ data are well described by an exponential form, we demonstrate that these fits do not provide reliable estimates of the critical packing fraction, $φ_0$, owing to the substantial extrapolation required beyond the accessible equilibrium range. We find the commonly assumed proportionality between $η$ and the structural relaxation time, $τ_α$ , to not hold for this system. For $φ\gtrsim 0.570$, the van Hove self-correlation function $G_s(r, τ)$ exhibits a spatial exponential decay at intermediate times, $τ$, signaling dynamic heterogeneity. The characteristic decay length scales as $λ\sim τ^ν$, with $ν\approx 1/3$, in contrast to the conventional square-root scaling. We also investigate temporal heterogeneity through the four-point dynamic susceptibility, $χ_4(τ)$, and its peak time, $τ_4$. These findings provide rigorous benchmark MD data for computational studies of glassy dynamics and establish a reference for testing theoretical models in dense disordered systems.

</details>


### [79] [The validity of the Stokes-Einstein relation in ionic liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09576)
*Gan Ren,Tao Duan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过粗粒化离子液体（ionic liquids, ILs）的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），系统检验了斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系（Stokes-Einstein, SE）在400-800 K温度范围内的有效性，指出常用变体公式与原式结果不一致，其失效源于强静电相互作用引起的离子关联（ion correlations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子液体在室温以上即表现出过冷液体（supercooled liquids）行为，文献中常认为SE关系在ILs中失效，但该结论多基于SE关系的某些变体（variants）得出，可靠性存疑。因此有必要系统考察原式与变体的适用性与一致性。

Method: 采用粗粒化IL模型开展分子动力学模拟，温度范围设定为400-800 K；同时检验原始SE公式及两种常用变体公式，比较其给出的结果，并进一步通过模拟验证离子关联的作用。

Result: 三种公式给出明显不同的结果，表明两种变体无法作为原始SE关系的可靠替代；这种不一致反映了斯托克斯定律（Stokes law）中的半径r随条件变化。SE关系的失效源自强静电相互作用（strong electrostatic interactions）所引入的离子关联，该论点经模拟进一步证实。

Conclusion: 在ILs中判断SE关系有效性时，基于变体公式的结论可能不可靠；SE关系失效的物理机制在于静电作用导致的离子关联，而非过冷液体类比下的简单失效。

Abstract: Ionic liquids (ILs) exhibit supercooled liquids behavior even above room temperature and the Stokes-Einstein (SE) relation is often considered to be invalid in ILs as that in supercooled liquids. However, the conclusion is usually drawn based on some variants of SE relation. In this work, we have systematically investigated the validity of the Stokes-Einstein relation in ILs by performing molecular dynamics simulations of coarse-grained IL within the temperature range of 400-800 K. Both the original SE formulation, and two commonly employed variants, were examined to assess their applicability and consistency. These three formulas yield distinctly different results, indicating that the two variants are not reliable substitutes for the original SE relation. The inconsistency suggests the fact that the r in Stokes law varies with conditions. The breakdown arises from the ion correlations introduced by the strong electrostatic interactions. This proposition is further confirmed by simulations.

</details>


### [80] [Coarse-graining to create minimalist models for dynamic, end-linked star-polymer networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09594)
*Tyla R. Holoman,C. Levi Petix,Michael P. Howard,Thomas M. Truskett*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文利用相对熵粗粒化（relative-entropy coarse-graining）方法，从高分辨率的珠子-弹簧（bead-spring）星形聚合物模拟结果出发，构建动态末端缔合四臂聚乙二醇（poly(ethylene glycol)）大分子单体的5-珠模型，并考察粗粒化精度与可迁移性对粗粒化状态点的依赖及自由度缩减与有效相互作用范围之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有针对斑块状吸引粒子（patchy attractive particles）的极简模型揭示了粒子价态（valence）如何主导胶体自组装的设计规则，但关于可逆、形成网络的星形聚合物（star polymers）的类似简单模型研究较少。因此需要为动态、末端缔合的星形大分子建立简化模型并评估其可靠性。

Method: 采用相对熵粗粒化（relative-entropy coarse-graining），并基于更精细分辨率的珠子-弹簧（bead-spring）星形聚合物模拟结果，生成动态、末端缔合四臂聚乙二醇（poly(ethylene glycol)）大分子单体的5-珠模型。

Result: 通过比较粗粒化模型与精细模型的结构关联（structural correlations）、网络连通性（network connectivity）和相行为（phase behavior），揭示了粗粒化模型的精度与可迁移性（transferability）如何依赖于所选粗粒化状态点（state point）。结果还表明，在粗粒化中减少自由度与扩展有效相互作用（effective interactions）范围之间存在内在权衡，该权衡影响模型中成对相互作用（pairwise interactions）的总数。

Conclusion: 粗粒化模型的精度与可迁移性取决于粗粒化状态点的选择；自由度缩减与有效相互作用范围扩展之间的权衡会影响成对相互作用总数，进而对模型的计算效率（computational efficiency）产生影响。

Abstract: Although minimalist models for patchy attractive particles have revealed powerful design rules for how particle valence directs colloidal assembly, less work has focused on comparably simple models of reversible, network-forming star polymers. Here, we use relative-entropy coarse-graining and simulation results from finer-resolution bead--spring star polymers to generate 5-bead models of dynamic, end-associating four-armed poly(ethylene glycol) macromers. Our results comparing structural correlations, network connectivity, and phase behavior of the coarse- and fine-grained models provide insight into how the accuracy and transferability of the coarse-grained models depend on the state point chosen for coarse-graining. The results also reveal intrinsic trade-offs between reducing degrees of freedom and expanding the range of effective interactions in coarse-graining that impact the total number of pairwise interactions in the resulting model, with implications for its computational efficiency.

</details>


### [81] [Geodesic Trapping and Escape of Active Particles on Curved Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09649)
*Yuzhu Chen,Vishal P. Patil,David Saintillan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究揭示：弯曲曲面上的活性粒子可被闭合测地线（closed geodesics）形成的几何陷阱（geometric traps）捕获，且逃逸行为取决于重取向（reorientation）是连续还是离散过程，并受高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认知中，粒子被捕获通常需要物理屏障；该工作关注无需物理屏障、仅由曲面几何导致的陷阱，并探讨连续与离散重取向机制在此类陷阱中的差异。

Method: 基于活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles）与游走-翻滚粒子（run-and-tumble particles）两类模型，在高佩克莱特数（Péclet numbers）与低佩克莱特数两种极限下，分析其从几何陷阱中逃逸的行为及平均逃逸时间（mean exit time）的渐近标度。

Result: 高佩克莱特数下，活性布朗粒子通过旋转扩散（rotational diffusion）高效逃逸，而游走-翻滚粒子则被捕获更长时间；低佩克莱特数下，二者均退化为被动扩散（passive diffusion）。高斯曲率通过聚焦或散焦邻近测地线来控制逃逸，并产生不同的平均逃逸时间渐近标度。

Conclusion: 几何陷阱的逃逸行为从根本上区分了连续与离散重取向机制，且高斯曲率对逃逸时间具有决定性调控作用。

Abstract: Active particles on curved surfaces can become trapped along closed geodesics even without physical barriers. We show that escape from these geometric traps exposes a fundamental distinction between continuous and discrete reorientation. At high Péclet numbers, active Brownian particles escape efficiently through rotational diffusion, whereas run-and-tumble particles remain trapped much longer; at low Péclet numbers, both reduce to passive diffusion. Gaussian curvature controls escape by focusing or defocusing neighboring geodesics, producing distinct asymptotic scalings of the mean exit time.

</details>


### [82] [Configurational-space separation and structure selection in three hard squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09936)
*Yuheng Yang,Meng Xiao,Duanduan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 硬粒子自组装源于纯排除体积（excluded-volume）约束，本文以二维周期盒中三个硬方板（hard squares）的最小体系为对象，揭示其构型空间中存在由密度触发的连通性分裂，并识别出分离密度 φ_sep=3/5，表明多粒子自组装特征可在少粒子极限中显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同形状硬粒子的自组装可仅由排除体积约束产生丰富结构，但结构选择与构型连通性在极少粒子极限下是否已具备可辨识特征尚不明确。本文意在用最小体系（三个硬方板）探究密度驱动的构型行为与结构选择机制。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov-chain Monte Carlo）与均匀非重叠插入采样（uniform non-overlapping insertion sampling）计算径向分布函数（radial distribution functions），并配合压力（pressure）测量；通过构造显式无重叠过渡路径（overlap-free transition pathways）与量化构型空间体积进行分析。

Result: 随堆积分数（packing fraction）增加，两种方法给出的径向分布函数在低密度一致、中间区间显著偏离、高密度再次收敛。压力测量提供强数值证据，表明偏离源于特征密度以上允许构型空间分裂为两个非连通区域，分离密度为 φ_sep=3/5；在略低于该密度处可构造显式无重叠过渡路径并量化两区域相对构型空间体积。高堆积分数下，粒子中心近似 L 形排列（L-shaped arrangement）显著优于交错排列（staggered one）。

Conclusion: 排除体积几何（excluded-volume geometry）即使在三个粒子体系中也能同时支配构型连通性与局部结构选择，L 形排列对应更大硬方板体系中四方序（tetratic ordering）与方格序（square-lattice ordering）的结构基元，说明多粒子自组装的特征可在少粒子极限中提前涌现。

Abstract: Self-assembly of hard particles with diverse shapes gives rise to a rich variety of structures through excluded-volume constraints alone. Here we show that even a minimal system of three hard squares confined in a two-dimensional periodic box exhibits nontrivial configurational behavior relevant to structure selection. As the packing fraction increases, radial distribution functions obtained from Markov-chain Monte Carlo and uniform non-overlapping insertion sampling agree at low densities, deviate markedly over an intermediate range, and converge again at higher densities. Pressure measurements provide strong numerical evidence that the discrepancy originates from the separation of the allowed configurational space into two disconnected regions above a characteristic density. We identify the separation density as $φ_{\rm sep}=3/5$, construct explicit overlap-free transition pathways connecting the two regions immediately below it, and quantify their relative configurational-space volumes. At higher packing fractions, an approximately L-shaped arrangement of the particle centers becomes strongly favored over a staggered one, revealing a structural motif characteristic of tetratic and square-lattice ordering in larger hard-square systems. These results show that excluded-volume geometry can govern both configurational connectivity and local structure selection even in a three-particle system, revealing how signatures of many-particle self-assembly can already emerge in the few-particle limit.

</details>


### [83] [Effect of Spherical Confinement on the 2-TIPS of Soft Repulsive Spherocylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10014)
*Jaydeep Mandal,Chandan Dasgupta,Prabal K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究考察球形约束下软排斥椭球（soft repulsive spherocylinders, SRS）体系的双温度诱导相分离（2-Temperature induced phase separation, 2-TIPS），并比较自由锚定（free anchoring）与切向锚定（tangential anchoring）两种条件下的相行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解球形约束与锚定方式如何影响二元混合物中的相分离行为，尤其是活性与被动子系统之间的相分离。

Method: 采用软排斥椭球（SRS）作为模型粒子，将其约束于球面，并设定自由锚定与切向锚定两种取向条件，通过调控堆积分数（packing fraction）与长径比（aspect ratio）考察相分离行为。

Result: 自由锚定下，提高堆积分数或长径比会抑制活性与被动子系统间的相分离，体系表现为稠密-稀疏相共存（dense-dilute phase co-existence），该现象可由捕获效应（trapping effect）解释并源于约束作用；切向锚定下，体系由初始各向同性态（isotropic state）分离为局部有序的稠密区与无序的稀疏区。

Conclusion: 该工作为球形约束下二元混合物的相分离提供了有益认识，揭示了锚定条件与约束对相行为的调控作用。

Abstract: We studied the 2-Temperature induced phase separation (2-TIPS) in a system of soft repulsive sphe rocylinders (SRS), confined on the surface of a sphere. We used two different anchoring conditions for the SRS on the spherical surface- free and tangential. For the free anchoring, increasing either the packing fraction or the rod aspect ratio suppresses phase separation between the active and passive subsystems, and the system corresponds to a dense-dilute phase co-existence. These phenomena can be explained via the trapping effect and are emergent due to the confinement. For the tangential anchoring, the system phase separates from its initial isotropic state into a locally ordered dense region and a disordered dilute region. Our work provides useful insights into phase separation in binary mixtures under spherical confinement.

</details>


### [84] [Topology-dependent mixing of knots in flexible polymer chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10188)
*Souradeep Sengupta,Garima Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用粗粒化布朗动力学模拟，研究柔性聚合物中两个纽结在同质（$3_1\otimes 3_1$）与异质（$3_1\otimes 5_1$）拓扑下的混合行为，发现组分拓扑显著影响多纽结的组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物轮廓上多个纽结（knot）的组织可能强烈依赖其组成纽结的拓扑结构，但这一依赖关系尚需在受控条件下加以刻画。

Method: 使用粗粒化布朗动力学模拟（coarse-grained Brownian-dynamics simulations），在受控的归一化壁间距（normalized wall separation）下，采用基于轮廓的坐标（contour-based coordinate）区分混合与去混合构型，并比较同质对（$3_1\otimes 3_1$）与异质对（$3_1\otimes 5_1$）。

Result: 在中等壁间距下，从部分重叠构型出发，$3_1\otimes 5_1$对在模拟时间窗口内比$3_1\otimes 3_1$对更频繁地处于混合构型；且$3_1\otimes 5_1$混合后形成的复合纽结区域所占聚合物轮廓显著少于两个组成纽结在去混合状态下占据的轮廓总和，而$3_1\otimes 3_1$对的相应轮廓占用减少幅度明显更小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，组成纽结的拓扑可强烈影响柔性聚合物中多纽结的组织方式；异质拓扑的组分可在复合构型中被容纳于更小部分的聚合物轮廓内。

Abstract: The organization of multiple knots along a polymer contour can depend sensitively on the topology of the constituent knots. Using coarse-grained Brownian-dynamics simulations, we investigate topology-dependent mixing of two knots in a flexible polymer at controlled normalized wall separation. We compare homogeneous ($3_1\otimes 3_1$) and heterogeneous ($3_1\otimes 5_1$) knot pairs using a contour-based coordinate that distinguishes mixed and demixed configurations. Starting from partially overlapping configurations, the $3_1\otimes 5_1$ pair is observed in mixed configurations more frequently over the simulation time window than the $3_1\otimes 3_1$ pair at intermediate wall separation. We further find that, over the intermediate range of normalized wall separation, mixing of the $3_1\otimes5_1$ pair produces a composite knotted region that occupies substantially less polymer contour than the two constituent knots collectively occupy in demixed configurations. The corresponding reduction in contour occupancy is much smaller for the $3_1\otimes3_1$ pair. This indicates that the heterogeneous constituent topologies can be accommodated within a smaller portion of the polymer contour in the composite configuration. These results establish that constituent topology can strongly influence multiple-knot organization in flexible polymers.

</details>


### [85] [Characterizing particle rearrangements in sheared highly polydisperse materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10247)
*Waad Paliwal,Eric R. Weeks*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文比较了驱动流下高多分散性材料中三种粒子尺度重排度量：非仿射运动（nonaffine motion）、最近邻连接性变化、以及 $D^2_{\min}$，并强调在高多分散体系中"邻域"定义的敏感性。研究推荐使用 radical Delaunay 三角剖分（radical Delaunay triangulation）检测连接性变化，使用尺寸感知的成对截断距离法（size-aware pairwise cutoff distance method）计算 $D^2_{\min}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多分散性材料中重排的粒子尺度度量缺乏统一定义，尤其"邻近粒子"的界定在尺寸差异极大时具有非平凡性。作者旨在评估三种常用度量在高多分散条件下的适用性与稳健性。

Method: 将三种度量（相对时间平均流的非仿射运动、最近邻连接性变化、$D^2_{\min}$）应用于已发表的二维模拟（多分散度至 $δ\approx0.50$）与颗粒流实验数据，并比较不同邻域定义对结果的影响。

Result: 连接性变化与 $D^2_{\min}$ 均依赖邻域定义。作者推荐：连接性变化用 radical Delaunay 三角剖分；$D^2_{\min}$ 用尺寸感知的成对截断距离法。改变邻域定义可逆转实验数据中 $D^2_{\min}$ 对粒径的表观依赖关系。

Conclusion: $D^2_{\min}$ 的趋势不能独立于所用邻域解释。总体而言，三种度量提供了关于高多分散体系重排的互补信息。

Abstract: We compare three particle-scale measures of rearrangement in highly polydisperse materials under driven flow: (a) nonaffine motion defined relative to the time-averaged mean flow, (b) changes in nearest-neighbor connectivity, and (c) $D^2_{\min}$, which measures nonaffine motion relative to an affine deformation fitted locally in space and time [Falk and Langer, Phys. Rev. E 57, 7192 (1998)]. We apply these measures to previously published two-dimensional simulations [Jiang, Sussman, and Weeks, Phys. Rev. E 108, 054605 (2023)] and granular-flow experiments [Illing and Weeks, Phys. Rev. E 111, 045422 (2025)] with polydispersities up to $δ\approx0.50$. Changes in connectivity and $D^2_{\min}$ both require a definition of neighboring particles, making the choice of neighborhood nontrivial in highly polydisperse systems. For detecting changes in connectivity, we recommend radical Delaunay triangulation, which provides a size-aware topological definition of neighbors. For calculating $D^2_{\min}$, we recommend a size-aware pairwise cutoff distance method. We further show that changing the neighborhood definition can reverse the apparent dependence of $D^2_{\min}$ on particle size in experimental data. Thus, trends in $D^2_{\min}$ cannot be interpreted independently of the neighborhood used to calculate it. Overall, the three measures presented provide complementary information about rearrangements in highly polydisperse systems.

</details>

### [86] [The energetics of force errors in machine-learned molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09251)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Vincent Michaud-Rioux,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出方向性残余功系数（directional residual-work coefficient），将力的方向性曲率失配与残余响应的空间分布相结合，用于刻画力误差在原子运动中的能量效应，并在锂电解质界面体系中验证其预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力误差的能量效应取决于原子运动，仅凭力匹配程度难以判断其对参考能量转移的实际影响，因此需要建立能同时反映方向性与空间分布的度量。

Method: 针对在锚点（anchor）处力匹配的保守势（conservative potentials），构造方向性残余功系数，结合方向曲率失配（directional curvature mismatch）与残余响应（residual response）的空间分布；在474原子锂电解质界面（lithium-electrolyte interface）上，于未来参考评估之前固定预测，并在24个预设终点上检验。

Result: 预测值与测量值的偏差小于预测功的4.6%；在结构与初始总动能不变、仅改变初始速度方向时，力-功排序（force-work ranking）发生反转：同一0.25 fs时刻下，一方向的最大力残差（force residual）大11.6%，但功少36.1%，该反转在第二个结构中重现。

Conclusion: 该框架将力容差（force tolerances）与参考能量转移（reference-energy transfer）联系起来，为势函数评估与自适应参考分配（adaptive reference allocation）提供物理依据。

Abstract: The energetic effect of a force error depends on atomic motion. We establish a directional residual-work coefficient combining directional curvature mismatch with the spatial distribution of the residual response. For conservative potentials force-matched at an anchor, it determines the leading signed work at the first crossing of a small force-error budget. At a 474-atom lithium-electrolyte interface, predictions fixed before future reference evaluations differ from measurements by less than 4.6% of predicted work across 24 prescribed endpoints. Changing only the initial velocity direction at fixed structure and initial total kinetic energy reverses the force-work ranking. At the same admitted time of 0.25 fs, one direction gives an 11.6% larger maximum force residual but 36.1% less work. The reversal recurs at a second structure. The framework connects force tolerances to reference-energy transfer, providing a physical basis for potential assessment and adaptive reference allocation.

</details>


### [87] [Implicit size dependence of the valence electron concentration criterion in high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09309)
*Dennis Boakye,Chuang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究基于宏观原子模型（macroscopic atom model）论证，价电子浓度（valence electron concentration, VEC）之所以能预测高熵合金（high-entropy alloys, HEAs）中 FCC 与 BCC 稳定性，是因为它实际上已隐含了原子尺寸信息；而经尺寸修正的电子计数在 265 种合金上并未改善预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VEC 是预测高熵合金中 FCC（面心立方）与 BCC（体心立方）稳定性最常用的指标，但它仅是成分平均值，不含原子尺寸信息，其有效性的来源与失效条件尚不明确。

Method: 采用宏观原子模型（macroscopic atom model）分析混合焓（mixing enthalpy）对尺寸的敏感性，并推导尺寸对电子计数的作用，将其表达为原子表面 V^{2/3} 与价电子数的协方差（covariance）除以均值；随后在 265 种已表征合金上检验尺寸修正后的计数与 VEC 的关系。

Result: 混合焓几乎对尺寸不敏感：即使成分体积相差 2 倍，变化仍小于 6%，因此尺寸只能作用于电子计数；同时体积与价电子在构建 HEA 的元素中呈强反相关（anti-correlated），使尺寸修正后的计数在 265 种合金上只是 VEC 的仿射重标度（affine rescaling），未带来预测改善。

Conclusion: VEC 本身已编码原子尺寸，这解释了它的成功，并将其失效定位于尺寸较大、电子丰富的元素；化学性质通过单一开关元素（switch element）设定焓值。

Abstract: The valence electron concentration (VEC) is the most widely used predictor of FCC against BCC stability in high-entropy alloys (HEAs), yet it is a compositional average carrying no information about atomic size. Using the macroscopic atom model, we show that the mixing enthalpy is almost size-blind, shifting by less than 6\% even when constituent volumes differ by a factor of 2, so that size can act only on the electron count. That action equals exactly the covariance of the atomic surface $V^{2/3}$ with the valence electron count, divided by its mean. This covariance is not free. Volume and valence are strongly anti-correlated across the elements used to build HEAs, so the size-corrected count is an affine rescaling of VEC over 265 characterized alloys and improves no prediction. VEC already encodes atomic size, which explains its success and locates its failure among large, electron-rich elements. Chemistry sets the enthalpy through one switch element.

</details>


### [88] [Phase stability and mechanical response of Ag-interlayered Al/Cu resistance spot-welded joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09364)
*Shuang Lin,Kyubok Lee,Jiahui Ye,Ho Kwon,Shun-Li Shang,Allison M. Beesea,Xun Liu,Jingjing Li,Zi-Kui Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作引入 Ag 中间层抑制 Al-Cu 金属间化合物，通过 CALPHAD、第一性原理与实验相结合，建立从中间层化学到析出相及其力学性质的预测性框架，使接头名义强度从 47.9 MPa 提升至 67.4 MPa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异种 Al/Cu 接头是电池包（battery-pack）组装的关键，但其力学强度受脆性 Al-Cu 金属间化合物（intermetallic compounds, IMCs，如 Al2Cu、Al4Cu9）限制。既有中间层策略多为经验性，缺乏将中间层化学与所形成相及其本征力学特性联系起来的预测框架。

Method: 引入 Ag 中间层，整合计算热力学（CALPHAD 平衡与 Scheil 模拟）、第一性原理计算（准谐振近似下的声子计算、弹性常数与 Pugh 判据）、微观结构表征（EDS、EBSD）与力学测试。CALPHAD 预测富 Al 的 Al-Ag 熔合区凝固路径，第一性原理给出有限温度熵与吉布斯自由能并以 CALPHAD 为基准校验。

Result: EDS 与 EBSD 证实 FCC Al-Ag 固溶体为主导组成相，Ag 存在时 Cu 的掺入受限。所有 Al-Ag 相（含观测到的固溶体）均超过 Pugh 阈值 1.75，而目标 Al-Cu IMCs 未超过。性能上，名义强度由 47.9 增至 67.4 MPa；纳米压痕给出熔合区折减模量 82.8 GPa（杨氏模量 82.0 GPa），对比 0 K Voigt-Reuss-Hill 计算值 71.4 GPa。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了一套可迁移的 CALPHAD、第一性原理与实验相结合的工作流（workflow），用于异种金属连接（dissimilar-metal joining）中中间层的理性选择，并为 Ag 中间层有效性提供了机制性依据。

Abstract: Dissimilar Al/Cu joints are essential to battery-pack assemblies; however, their mechanical strength is limited by brittle Al-Cu intermetallic compounds (IMCs) such as Al2Cu and Al4Cu9. Interlayer strategies to suppress these phases remain largely empirical, lacking a predictive framework linking interlayer chemistry to the phases that form and to their intrinsic mechanical character. Here an Ag interlayer is introduced and combines computational thermodynamics, first-principles calculations, microstructural characterization, and mechanical testing into a single self-consistent description of the joint. CALculation of PHAse Diagrams (CALPHAD) equilibrium and Scheil simulations predict the solidification path of the Al-rich Al-Ag fusion zone and explain why Cu incorporation is limited when Ag is present; energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy (EDS) and electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) confirm an FCC Al-Ag solid solution as the dominant constituent. First-principles phonon calculations within the quasiharmonic approximation yield finite-temperature entropy and Gibbs energy, benchmarked against CALPHAD, while elastic constants assess ductility via the Pugh criterion (i.e., the bulk/shear (B/G) modulus ratio). All Al-Ag phases, including the observed solid solution, exceed the Pugh threshold of 1.75, whereas the targeted Al-Cu IMCs do not, giving a mechanistic basis for the interlayer's effectiveness. This microstructural change translates into improved performance: nominal strength rises from 47.9 to 67.4 MPa. Nanoindentation gives a fusion-zone reduced modulus of 82.8 GPa (Young's modulus 82.0 GPa), versus a calculated 0 K Voigt-Reuss-Hill value of 71.4 GPa. The present work establishes a transferable CALPHAD, first-principles, and experiment workflow for rational interlayer selection in dissimilar-metal joining.

</details>


### [89] [High-pressure elastic properties of GeO2 polymorphs up to 120 GPa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09469)
*Gulshan Kumar,Sumit Ghosh,Sharad Babu Pillai,Rajkrishna Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）系统计算了四种 GeO2 同质多象（polymorph）的相稳定性和弹性性质的压力依赖性，并利用应变耦合的朗道自由能展开（strain-coupled Landau free-energy expansion）揭示了金红石型（rutile-type）到 CaCl2 型相变附近的弹性软化（elastic softening）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeO2 多相在高压下的相稳定性与弹性响应（尤其是相变附近的弹性反常）尚需系统阐明，研究旨在厘清各相稳定性范围及相变对弹性性质的影响。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论的理论计算，在由静态焓（static enthalpy）计算确定的各相稳定区间内以 5 GPa 间隔计算弹性常数；进一步使用经典应变耦合的朗道自由能展开描述金红石型至 CaCl2 型相变相关的弹性响应演化。

Result: 金红石型至 CaCl2 型相变符合朗道型二级相变（Landau-type second-order transition），临界压力为 14.6 GPa；随相变压力逼近，金红石型相出现弹性软化，导致体模量与剪切模量呈反常压力依赖；弹性波各向异性（elastic-wave anisotropy）在相变附近显著增大，22.5 GPa 时最大值约 122%。

Conclusion: 相变后 CaCl2 型相各向异性急剧下降，并在 CaCl2 型/alpha-PbO2 型相界处出现不连续；更高压的 alpha-PbO2 型与黄铁矿型（pyrite-type）相各向异性压力依赖性较弱，黄铁矿型相各向异性最低，高压下仅约 4-5%，与其高对称立方结构和近各向同性弹性波传播一致。

Abstract: We systematically investigated the phase stability and pressure dependence of the elastic properties of four GeO2 polymorphs: rutile-, CaCl2-, alpha-PbO2-, and pyrite-type phases using theoretical calculations based on density functional theory. The elastic constants were calculated at 5 GPa intervals within the respective stability ranges of the four phases, as determined from static enthalpy calculations. We further employed a classical strain-coupled Landau free-energy expansion to describe the pressure evolution of the elastic response associated with the rutile- to CaCl2-type transition and to elucidate the origin of the elastic softening near the transition. The rutile- to CaCl2-type phase transition is consistent with a Landau-type second-order transition, with a critical pressure of 14.6 GPa obtained from the strain-based analysis. As the transition pressure approaches, elastic softening develops in the rutile-type phase, resulting in anomalous pressure dependence of the bulk and shear modulus. The calculated elastic-wave anisotropy increases markedly near the transition, primarily due to the rapid reduction in shear-wave velocity, reaching a maximum of approximately 122% at 22.5 GPa. Following the transition, the anisotropy decreases sharply in the CaCl2-type phase and exhibits a discontinuity at the CaCl2-type/alpha-PbO2 -type phase boundary. The higher-pressure alpha-PbO2- and pyrite-type phases exhibit comparatively weak pressure dependence of anisotropy, with a small discontinuity at their respective phase transition boundaries. The pyrite-type phase has the lowest anisotropy, reaching only approximately 4-5% at high pressure, consistent with the high-symmetry cubic structure and nearly isotropic elastic-wave propagation.

</details>


### [90] [Light-Driven Ultrafast Control of Time-Reversal Symmetry in SrTiO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09497)
*In Hyeok Choi,Sergei Urazhdin,Shivasheesh Varshney,Seung Gyo Jeong,Bharat Jalan,Keith A. Nelson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用非共振、椭圆偏振的太赫兹（THz）脉冲，在立方相钛酸锶（SrTiO₃）中同时实现磁对称性降低与时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）破缺，并通过二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation, SHG）偏振测量学予以表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光驱动控制材料对称性可工程化平衡态下被禁戒的现象。尽管电磁场已被用于破缺时间反演对称性，但在非磁性绝缘氧化物（nonmagnetic insulating oxides）中于超快时间尺度上的动态调控仍基本未被探索。

Method: 采用非共振（off-resonant）、椭圆偏振的太赫兹脉冲驱动立方相 SrTiO₃，并运用二次谐波产生偏振测量学（SHG polarimetry）探测其对称性变化，分析 SHG 圆二色性（circular dichroism）与偏振图样的镜像对称破缺，并考察 SHG 响应与 THz 脉冲角动量的标度关系。

Result: SHG 偏振测量显示出涌现的 SHG 圆二色性，与立方 m3m 对称性下的三阶非线性过程不符；SHG 偏振图样呈现镜像对称破缺，表明体系瞬态降低至四方 4/mm'm' 磁对称性。此外，SHG 响应随 THz 脉冲角动量线性标度，直接证明 THz 场诱导的时间反演对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了在非磁性氧化物中实现超快对称性控制的直接途径，使超快时间尺度上涌现相（emergent phases）的动态操控成为可能。

Abstract: Light-driven control of material symmetry enables the engineering of phenomena forbidden in equilibrium. Although electromagnetic fields have been used to break time-reversal symmetry, its dynamic control on ultrafast timescales in nonmagnetic insulating oxides remains virtually unexplored. Here, we demonstrate simultaneous magnetic symmetry lowering and timereversal-symmetry breaking in cubic SrTiO3 driven by an off-resonant, elliptically polarized THz pulse. Second harmonic generation (SHG) polarimetry reveals emergent SHG circular dichroism inconsistent with third-order nonlinear processes under cubic m3m symmetry. The SHG polar patterns exhibit mirror-symmetry breaking, indicating a transient reduction to tetragonal 4/mm'm' magnetic symmetry. Furthermore, the SHG response scales linearly with the angular momentum of THz pulse, providing direct evidence for THz-field-induced timereversal-symmetry breaking. These results establish a direct pathway for ultrafast symmetry control in non-magnetic oxides, enabling dynamic manipulation of emergent phases on ultrafast timescales.

</details>


### [91] [Composition and Surface Termination Control Band Alignments in P3HT/Perovskite Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09566)
*Somayyeh Alidoust,V. Ongun Özçelik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过高通量第一性原理计算（high-throughput first-principles calculations），系统研究了820种聚(3-己基噻吩)（P3HT）变体与钙钛矿构成的异质结的能带对齐（band alignment），揭示组分与表面终止对能带排列及载流子限域（carrier confinement）的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合有机-无机钙钛矿异质结（hybrid organic-inorganic perovskite heterostructures）是能量转换与光电器件的新兴平台，但组分与表面终止（surface termination）对能带对齐的影响尚不清晰，缺乏系统认识。

Method: 采用高通量第一性原理计算，构建并分析820种由P3HT变体与常规及混合阳离子（mixed-cation）金属卤化物钙钛矿组成的异质结；开展能带边缘分析（band edge analysis）与显式界面计算（explicit interface calculations）。

Result: 能带边缘分析预测出组分依赖的能带对齐：多数基于Pb和Sn的钙钛矿表现为Type II与Type III行为，而混合阳离子CsMASnBr3与所有P3HT变体独特地形成Type-I结。显式界面计算显示，表面终止可逆转常规锡基钙钛矿的预测对齐，而混合阳离子钙钛矿无论终止方式如何均保持与P3HT的Type-I对齐；尽管电荷转移可忽略，终止依赖的能带偏移（band offsets）仍导致显著不同的载流子限域。

Conclusion: 组分与表面终止共同决定P3HT/钙钛矿界面的能带对齐与载流子限域，该研究确立了P3HT/钙钛矿界面工程的设计原则（design principles）。

Abstract: Hybrid organic-inorganic perovskite heterostructures are emerging platforms for energy conversion and optoelectronic devices, yet the effects of composition and surface terminations on band alignment remain poorly understood. Here, using high-throughput first-principles calculations, we systematically reveal the effect of composition and surface termination on the band alignment of 820 heterostructures formed between poly(3-hexylthiophene) (P3HT) variants and conventional and mixed-cation metal halide perovskites. Band edge analysis predicts concentration dependent band alignments, with most Pb and Sn based perovskites exhibiting Type II and III behavior while mixed-cation CsMASnBr3 uniquely forms Type-I junctions with all P3HT variants. Explicit interface calculations reveal that surface termination can reverse the predicted alignment in conventional tin perovskites, while the mixed cation perovskite preserves Type-I alignment with P3HT regardless of termination. Despite negligible charge transfer, termination dependent band offsets produce markedly different carrier confinement, establishing design principles for engineering P3HT/perovskite interfaces.

</details>


### [92] [An encoded asymmetric ligand for metal-selective topological assembly of two-dimensional metal-organic frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09598)
*Huimin Qi,Jinkun Guo,Xinyan Wu,Weishan Li,Tongyang Zhao,Ze-Fan Yao,Hao Chen,Ling Zhang,Bin Jiang,Yi Liu,Haoyang Zhang,Yunlong Fan,Tianyang Chen,Qingqing Ji,Jin-Hu Dou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过引入去对称化的编码型配体 BTH，实现了金属离子选择性的二维 MOF 拓扑组装，并由此产生跨越六个数量级的电导差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维金属有机框架（2D MOFs）的拓扑多样性受限于高对称配体的固定几何构型，亟需可编程的结构调控策略。

Method: 设计编码非对称配体苯并[b]三亚苯-2,3,6,7,11,12-六酚（BTH），将多位点配位场与空间位阻差异环境相结合，并分别与二价 Cu(II) 和 Zn(II) 离子配位组装。

Result: Cu-BTH-MOF 形成双模式六边形孔道拓扑，Zn-BTH-MOF 形成均一六边形孔道，经 PXRD Pawley 精修、结构模拟与孔径分布分析验证；电导率分别为 1.186 x 10-3 与 3.38 x 10-10 S cm-1，相差逾六个数量级。

Conclusion: 配体去对称化（ligand desymmetrization）可作为二维 MOF 中金属选择性拓扑多样化的可编程策略。

Abstract: Two-dimensional metal-organic frameworks (2D MOFs), with diverse topological architectures, provide a powerful platform for exploring unconventional electronic and lattice-dynamical responses. Yet their structural diversity remains fundamentally constrained by the fixed geometry of high-symmetry ligands. Here, we introduce an encoded asymmetric ligand, benzo[b]triphenylene-2,3,6,7,11,12-hexaol (BTH), for metal-selective topological assembly. By integrating multi-site coordination fields with sterically differentiated environments, BTH exhibits distinct topological programmability: different divalent metal ions direct divergent framework architectures. Specifically, coordination of BTH with divalent Cu(II) and Zn(II) ions assembles Cu-BTH-MOF with a dual-mode hexagonal pore topology and Zn-BTH-MOF with uniform hexagonal channels, respectively, as supported by PXRD Pawley refinement, structural simulations, and pore-size distribution analysis. Furthermore, this topological divergence is accompanied by a significant divergence in charge-transport properties, with Cu-BTH-MOF reaching an electrical conductivity of 1.186 x 10-3 S cm-1, more than six orders of magnitude higher than that of Zn-BTH-MOF (3.38 x 10-10 S cm-1). This work establishes ligand desymmetrization as a programmable strategy for metal-selective topological diversification in 2D MOFs.

</details>


### [93] [Heteroepitaxial Growth of PbSe on InP Substrates via Lattice-Matched III-V Buffer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09605)
*Biridiana Rodriguez,Mark Martino,Brody Yeung,Benjamin Sprenger,Leland Nordin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作证明 InP 基异质外延（heteroepitaxy）可为 PbSe 提供晶格失配约 4% 的兼容平台，通过分子束外延在 In0.53Ga0.47As 与 In0.52Al0.48As 缓冲层上生长高质量 (001) 岩盐结构 PbSe 薄膜，并获得覆盖 3.7–5.0 微米的室温光致发光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中波红外（mid-wave infrared，3–5 微米）探测器成本高昂，限制了其广泛应用。PbSe 作为廉价的窄带隙 IV-VI 族半导体虽有多晶薄膜中的大量晶界仍具高性能，但外延生长所需的合适衬底有限；已有 PbSe 异质外延多集中于 III-V、II-VI 与 IV 族衬底，其晶格匹配合金选择范围较窄，难以支撑异质结构工程（heterostructure engineering）。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）在 (001) InP 衬底上的 200 nm 厚 In0.53Ga0.47As 与 In0.52Al0.48As 缓冲层上生长 150 nm 厚 PbSe 薄膜；通过反射高能电子衍射（reflection high-energy electron diffraction，RHEED）与 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）表征结构，并在室温至 12 K 范围内测量光致发光（photoluminescence，PL）光谱。

Result: RHEED 与 XRD 显示两种缓冲层上均获得 (001) 取向岩盐（rock-salt）结构 PbSe，面外晶格常数为 6.12 埃。PL 在室温至 12 K 均可观测，峰值波长由 3.7 微米红移至 5.0 微米。相同测量条件下，In0.53Ga0.47As 与 In0.52Al0.48As 上薄膜的室温峰值 PL 强度分别约为 GaAs 衬底 PbSe 参考样品的 1.9 倍与 1.3 倍。

Conclusion: 结果确立了与 InP 工艺兼容的平台，可将窄带隙 PbSe 与广泛的 III-V 族三元、四元合金集成，例如使 In0.53Ga0.47As 同时充当短波红外吸收层与 PbSe 生长模板的结构。

Abstract: Detector cost remains a barrier to the widespread adoption of mid-wave infrared (3-5 micron) technology. PbSe, an inexpensive narrow band gap IV-VI semiconductor that has been used since the early 1940s, delivers high performance for infrared detection despite the abundance of grain boundaries in polycrystalline films. Epitaxial growth, however, could provide superior crystalline quality and interfaces, but suitable substrates remain limited for PbSe. Recent PbSe heteroepitaxy has focused primarily on III-V, II-VI and group-IV substrates that each offer a comparatively narrow range of lattice-matched alloys for heterostructure engineering. Here we show that InP-based heteroepitaxy provides access to a broader materials platform while limiting the lattice mismatch with PbSe to approximately 4%. We grow 150-nm thick PbSe films by molecular beam epitaxy on 200-nm thick In0.53Ga0.47As and In0.52Al0.48As buffers on (001) InP substrates. Reflection high-energy electron diffraction and X-ray diffraction show (001)-oriented rock-salt PbSe with an out-of-plane lattice constant of 6.12 Angstrom on both buffers. Photoluminescence (PL) is observed from room-temperature down to 12 K and the peak wavelength red-shifts from 3.7 to 5.0 microns. Under identical measurement conditions, the room-temperature peak PL intensities from films on In0.53Ga0.47As and In0.52Al0.48As are approximately 1.9x and 1.3x that of a PbSe on GaAs substrate reference, respectively. These results establish an InP-compatible platform for integrating narrow band-gap PbSe with a broad range of ternary and quaternary III-V alloys, including, for example, structures in which In0.53Ga0.47As serves as both a short-wave infrared absorber and a template for PbSe growth.

</details>


### [94] [Twist and strain identification in moiré heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09611)
*Zhen Zhan,Federico Escudero,Dong Wang,Yiwen Liu,Ambikesh Gupta,Pierre A. Pantaleón,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立了一个通用框架，可从原子或莫尔尺度分辨率的莫尔图像中提取扭转与应变构型，并借助最小弹性能和电子谱等附加约束估计最可能的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料（moiré materials）的几何与电子性质受样品扭转（twist）和应变（strain）影响极大，原因在于莫尔放大效应（moiré magnifying effect），因此准确识别扭转与应变对相关研究至关重要。

Method: 建立从莫尔图像中提取扭转与应变构型的通用框架，适用于原子尺度或莫尔尺度分辨率；引入最小弹性能（minimum elastic energy）与电子谱（electronic spectra）等附加约束与方法以估计最可能的扭转-应变构型。

Result: 仅凭莫尔波长（moiré-wavelength）信息时，存在连续的扭转-应变构型族，各构型对应莫尔图案的不同取向；最小弹性能分析显示，随应变增加，剪切构型（shear configurations）变得更为有利；并以应变三角莫尔图案（strained triangular moiré patterns）的形成与识别为例展示该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为在具有莫尔尺度分辨率的系统中识别扭转与应变构型提供了完整方法。

Abstract: The geometrical and electronic properties of moiré materials are highly sensitive to the twist and strain in the samples due to the moiré magnifying effect. Accurate identification of twist and strain in moiré materials is therefore essential. In this work, we establish a general framework to extract the twist and strain configurations from moiré images with either atomic or moiré scale resolution. With only moiré-wavelength information, we show that there is a continuous family of possible twist and strain configurations, each one accounting for different orientations of the moiré pattern. To estimate the most likely twist-strain configuration, we discuss additional constraints and methods involving the minimum elastic energy and the electronic spectra. The minimum elastic energy, in particular, reflects that shear configurations become much more favorable as the strain increases. As an example of the developed methodology, we discuss the formation and identification of strained triangular moiré patterns. Our framework provides a comprehensive approach to identify the twist and strain configurations in systems with moiré-scale resolution.

</details>


### [95] [Automated Generation of Commensurate Magnetic Structures based on Spin Space Groups and Graph Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09679)
*Yifan Wang,Boyang Deng,John Robertson,Weisheng Zhao,Stefan Blügel,Haichang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SpinGraph 是一个自动化流程，用于生成对称性相异的磁性构型（symmetry-distinct magnetic configurations），支持一般自旋空间群（spin space groups）与单/多 Q 态叠加控制，并可与 AMATIS 集成实现自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian）构建的全自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有对称性约束的磁性结构是能量有利构型的候选，枚举此类结构对识别实验观测结果及为能量拟合提供无偏、线性稳定、最优采样的参考构型至关重要。

Method: 对有限群直接枚举相容子群；对含连续或特殊纯自旋部分的自旋空间群，先枚举有限分量再结合相容的精确纯自旋约束，并将这些操作转化为实空间或傅里叶空间（Fourier space）的图约束，生成后重新评估每个结构的对称性。

Result: SpinGraph 兼容更一般的自旋空间群，可精确控制预设的单 Q 或多 Q 态叠加；将其与磁分析代码 AMATIS 集成后，对绝缘单层 CrI3 的自旋相互作用进行了完整计算。

Conclusion: SpinGraph 补全了 AMATIS 的最后环节，实现了自旋哈密顿量构建的全自动化流程，对研究相变（phase transitions）、自旋织构（spin textures）、磁振子（magnons）与自旋动力学（spin dynamics）具有关键意义。

Abstract: Magnetic structures with symmetry constraints are candidates for energetically favorable configurations. Enumerating these structures is essential for identifying experimental observations and provides unbiased, linearly stable, and optimally sampled reference configurations for energy fitting when extracting spin interactions. We present SpinGraph, an automated workflow generating symmetry-distinct magnetic configurations. SpinGraph is not only compatible with the more general spin space groups, but also allows precise control of the prescribed single- or multi-Q states superposition. For finite groups, we enumerate compatible subgroups directly. For spin space groups with a continuous or special spin-only part, the finite component is enumerated first and then combined with the compatible exact spin-only constraints. These actions are translated into graph constraints in real or Fourier space. The symmetry of every generated structure is re-evaluated after construction. Finally, we integrate SpinGraph with the magnetic analysis code AMATIS to perform a thorough calculation for the spin interactions of the insulating monolayer CrI3. SpinGraph complements the last part of AMATIS and realizes the fully automated workflow for the spin Hamiltonian construction, which is essential for studying phase transitions, spin textures, magnons, and spin dynamics.

</details>


### [96] [First-principles investigation of structural, mechanical, vibrational, thermal, electronic and optical properties of Na3Bi: a topological Dirac semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09720)
*Maisha Fahmida,Syed Shovon Mahbub Mahin,Suptajoy Barua,Ishtiaque M. Syed*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）对拓扑狄拉克半金属（topological Dirac semimetal）Na3Bi 的六方相进行了第一性原理（first-principles）研究，系统考察其结构、弹性力学、热学、电子与光学性质，并给出面向声阻尼、紫外（ultraviolet, UV）传感与光电子应用的性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑材料（topological materials）性质研究是凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）数十年来的活跃方向，而 Na3Bi 作为最早发现的拓扑狄拉克半金属之一，其力学、热学与光学行为的综合性基线数据尚待补充。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory）的第一性原理计算，对 Na3Bi 六方相（hexagonal phase）的结构、弹性力学、热学、电子结构与光学性质进行系统计算，包括弹性各向异性、硬度与可加工性指标、德拜温度（Debye temperature）、狄拉克点（Dirac point）与费米面（Fermi surface）形态，以及宽光谱范围内的反射率与折射率。

Result: 六方相 Na3Bi 在力学与动力学上均稳定，表现出中等弹性各向异性（universal anisotropy index = 0.832）。其可加工性指数为 2.386，硬度较低（2.005），与以脆性共价键合为主、伴随微弱金属性贡献的特征一致；德拜温度较低（181.39 K），表明晶格力学上偏软、原子结合较松。电子结构确认其半金属（semimetallic）行为，狄拉克点位于费米能级，费米面由点状口袋构成。光学上呈现 40–45% 的非选择性中等反射率，可见光区折射率高达 2–4.5，且为高效的紫外辐射吸收体。

Conclusion: 上述结果为六方相 Na3Bi 的力学、热学与光学行为提供了较为完整的基线，并提示其在声阻尼（acoustic damping）、紫外（UV）传感与光电子（optoelectronic）应用方面的潜在价值。

Abstract: Exploring the properties of topological materials has remained an active area of research in condensed matter physics for the past few decades. Here, we present a first-principles investigation of the structural, elasto-mechanical, thermal, electronic, and optical properties of Na3Bi, one of the earliest discovered topological Dirac semimetals, using density functional theory. The hexagonal phase of Na3Bi is found to be both mechanically and dynamically stable, exhibiting moderate elastic anisotropy with a universal anisotropy index of 0.832. The compound displays a moderate machinability index of 2.386 alongside a low hardness value of 2.005, consistent with its predominantly brittle, covalently bonded character accompanied by a slight metallic contribution. Its comparatively low Debye temperature of 181.39K points to a mechanically soft lattice with loosely bound atoms. Electronic structure calculations confirm semimetallic behavior, with well-defined Dirac points pinned at the Fermi level and a Fermi surface composed of point-like pockets. Optical analysis across a wide spectral range reveals non-selective, moderate reflectivity of 40 to 45% and, in the visible region, an unusually high refractive index reaching between 2 and 4.5. Na3Bi is additionally found to be an efficient absorber of ultraviolet radiation. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive baseline for the mechanical, thermal, and optical behavior of hexagonal Na3Bi and point to its potential relevance for acoustic damping, UV-sensing, and optoelectronic applications

</details>


### [97] [Morphology and depletion force-based large-scale self-assembly of nanocubes on surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09761)
*Yeonhee Lee,Seungsang Cha,Yuna Kwak,Nicholas Juntunen,Grant M. Rotskoff,Jwa-Min Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 MIDAS 方法，利用形貌敏感的耗尽力（depletion force）驱动金纳米立方体（AuNCs）在表面进行选择性组装，实现二维与三维纳米结构的大规模可控制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自组装纳米颗粒方法在尺寸、形状与组成上缺乏精度与可控性，且重复性差、难以规模化，需要复杂步骤与昂贵材料。因此需要一种兼具精确性与可扩展性的组装策略。

Method: 采用均一形貌诱导与耗尽力导向的表面纳米颗粒组装（MIDAS）方法，以金纳米立方体（AuNCs）为对象；通过形貌敏感的耗尽力触发形状选择性絮凝与组装，并利用表面粗糙度受控的基底诱导组装；辅以格子气模型（lattice-gas modeling）与动力学蒙特卡洛模拟（kinetic Monte Carlo simulations）。

Result: 在无需复杂配体修饰或制备步骤的条件下，实现了 AuNC 组装单层（2D AuNAMs）或三维多层（3D AuNAMs）的大规模、高特异性形成；模拟表明耗尽力是决定超晶（supercrystal）形貌的关键参数，并与实验结果相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种具普适性的纳米颗粒组装机制，并展示了 MIDAS 策略在二维与三维纳米结构可扩展制备与图案化方面的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Self-assembling nanoparticles is a highly efficient and facile way to form functional nano-, micro- and macrostructures. However, currently available methods lack precision and controllability in size, shape and composition, suffer from poor reproducibility and scalability, and require complex steps and expensive materials. Here, we present the uniform morphology-induced and depletion force-directed nanoparticle assembly on surface (MIDAS) method with gold nanocubes (AuNCs). Using this approach, morphology-sensitive depletion forces trigger shape-selective flocculation and assembly of the AuNCs with uniform size and shape. Importantly, the surface roughness-controlled substrate drives the large-scale formation of AuNC-assembled monolayers (2D AuNAMs) or three-dimensional AuNC-assembled multilayers (3D AuNAMs) in a highly specific manner without any complex ligand modification or preparation steps. Lattice-gas modeling and kinetic Monte Carlo simulations show the depletion force is the key parameter determining supercrystal morphology and corroborate the experimental findings. This work establishes a generalizable nanoparticle assembly mechanism and demonstrates the broad applicability of the MIDAS strategy for scalable fabrication and patterning of 2D and 3D nanoparticle architectures.

</details>


### [98] [Facile hBN-hBN Interfacial Overlap Engineering for Enhanced Quantum Emitter Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09771)
*Nhat Minh Nguyen,Trung Vuong Doan,Md Shakhawath Hossain,Akila Elangasinghe,Duc Anh Ngo,Ha Ngoc Duy Huynh,Thi Ngoc Anh Mai,Yongliang Chen,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael G. Ruppert,Chaohao Chen,Xiaoxue Xu,Toan Dinh,Toan Trong Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简易、低成本的双层全干法转移（double-layer all-dry transfer）方法，通过在 hBN 薄片间构建重叠区域，实现高密度、高质量量子发射体（quantum emitters）的确定性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料（two-dimensional materials）尤其是 hBN 中的量子发射体在量子技术中颇具前景，但在预定位置实现高密度发射体并保持光学质量仍是难题。

Method: 采用双层全干法转移（all-dry transfer）工艺，在 hBN 薄片之间确定性构建重叠区域，从而在预定义的覆盖区域内形成发射体。

Result: 重叠区域的发射体密度相比未覆盖区域最高提升 15 倍；发射体保持优异光学性能，包括高信号背景比（signal-to-background ratio）与信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）、大德拜-瓦勒因子（Debye-Waller factor）、高亮度及强光谱稳定性；文中还讨论了可能的缺陷构型（defect configurations）。

Conclusion: 该可扩展策略可在目标区域优先形成量子发射体，密度高于等离子体辐照（plasma irradiation）等简单处理，且避免复杂先进制造工艺，为将高质量量子发射体集成到可扩展量子光子平台（quantum photonic platforms）提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum emitters in two-dimensional materials, particularly hBN, are promising platforms for quantum technologies. However, achieving high-density emitters at predetermined locations while preserving optical quality remains challenging. Here, we introduce a facile, cost-effective double-layer all-dry transfer approach to deterministically create overlap regions between hBN flakes. These pre-defined capped regions exhibit a significantly enhanced emitter density, with up to a 15-fold increase compared to uncapped areas. Importantly, this method does not compromise emitter quality: emitters within overlap regions demonstrate excellent optical performance, including high signal-to-background and signal-to-noise ratios, large Debye-Waller factors, high brightness, and strong spectral stability. Possible defect configurations are also discussed to contextualize the observed emission characteristics. This scalable strategy enables preferential formation of quantum emitters in targeted regions, achieving higher densities than simple treatments such as plasma irradiation while avoiding the complexity of advanced fabrication techniques. The approach provides a practical pathway for integrating high-quality quantum emitters into scalable quantum photonic platforms.

</details>


### [99] [uFlowCSP: Crystal Structure Prediction using Mean flow generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09799)
*Sourin Dey,Dipannoy Das Gupta,Lai Wei,Sadman Sadeed Omee,Jianjun Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: uFlowCSP 提出基于 MeanFlow 的晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）模型，学习平均而非瞬时概率流速度，在 1-5 次网络评估内生成完整结构，实现 5x-58x 推理加速并保持或超越现有生成模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 CSP 模型（如 CDVAE、DiffCSP、FlowMM、CrystalFlow）虽能直接学习稳定晶体分布，但其扩散与流匹配（flow-matching）推理需对每个候选结构进行数十至数千次串行网络评估，计算代价高。

Method: 采用基于 MeanFlow 的框架学习平均概率流速度；使用化学与对称性感知的 Transformer（Transformer），结合规范原子排序（canonical atom ordering）、全局组分（global composition）与逐 token 化学嵌入（per-token chemistry embeddings）；训练时引入粗粒度晶系 token（crystal-system token），推理时仅依赖化学式。

Result: 在 MP-20（每目标 20 候选）上，单步即匹配 CrystalFlow（78.38% vs. 78.34%）且评估次数减少 100 倍、墙钟时间约低 10 倍；五步达 83.64%，超过 CrystalFlow（78.34%，2000 次评估）与 DiffCSP（77.93%，约 20000 次）。生成 10000 个结构仅需 0.39-1.31 分钟（CrystalFlow 6.5 分钟，DiffCSP 76.1 分钟）。CSPBench 能量排序前五判据下，五步 uFlowCSP 结构/空间群/共识匹配率为 72%/72%/65%，而 CrystalFlow 在 100 步为 78%/73%/68%、在 5 步降至 49%/32%/31%。

Conclusion: uFlowCSP 通过学习平均概率流速度，以极少评估次数兼顾精度与效率，其优势体现在单位网络评估的准确率提升，而非仅峰值精度。

Abstract: Crystal structure prediction (CSP) is fundamental to computational materials discovery. Generative models including CDVAE, DiffCSP, FlowMM, and CrystalFlow learn stable-crystal distributions directly, but diffusion and flow-matching inference requires tens to thousands of sequential network evaluations per candidate.
  We introduce uFlowCSP, a MeanFlow-based CSP model that learns the average, rather than instantaneous, probability-flow velocity. It generates a complete structure in one to five evaluations, delivering 5x-58x faster inference with equal or better performance. A chemistry- and symmetry-aware Transformer uses canonical atom ordering, global composition, and per-token chemistry embeddings. A coarse crystal-system token is used only during training; it provides additive gains, particularly improving space-group agreement despite being absent at inference, which remains formula-only.
  On MP-20 with 20 candidates per target, one step matches CrystalFlow (78.38% vs. 78.34%) with 100x fewer evaluations and about 10x lower wall-clock time. Five steps reach 83.64%, exceeding CrystalFlow (78.34% at 2,000 evaluations) and DiffCSP (77.93% at about 20,000), while using 20x fewer evaluations. uFlowCSP generates 10,000 structures in 0.39-1.31 minutes, versus 6.5 for CrystalFlow and 76.1 for DiffCSP. Under CSPBench's energy-ranked top-five structure-and-space-group criterion, five-step uFlowCSP reaches 72%/72%/65% structure, space-group, and consensus match rates. CrystalFlow reaches 78%/73%/68% at 100 steps but falls to 49%/32%/31% at five. Thus, uFlowCSP improves accuracy per network evaluation, not merely peak accuracy.

</details>


### [100] [Dimensional Control of Excitonic Interactions in Exfoliated 2D Molecular Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09923)
*Jonghyun Son,Seonghyun Koo,Daniel Yim,Sangjin Han,Dong-Hwan Yang,Gi-Yeop Kim,Kihyun Lee,Jieun Yeon,Hye Soo Kim,Eunbeen Jeon,Minji Ko,Minhee Choe,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Hee Cheul Choi,Kwanpyo Kim,Si-Young Choi,Seogjoo J. Jang,Hyungjun Kim,Sunmin Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作实现了并四苯（tetracene）等四种分子晶体的机械剥离，获得保持晶序的单层、少层与多层薄片，并借此在同一结构组织下揭示厚度对激子（exciton）能景的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料（2D materials）可通过降低维度、改变介电屏蔽（dielectric screening）与层依赖结构重构来调控激发态性质，但此类效应在分子晶体（molecular crystals）中难以实现，原因在于难以在原子尺度控制厚度同时保持晶序。

Method: 采用机械剥离（mechanical exfoliation）将并四苯及另外三种分子个体制备为单层、少层或多层薄片并保持晶序；结合厚度依赖光谱、电子衍射（electron diffraction）与基于激子模型（exciton model）的分析，关联分子堆积、分子间耦合与介电屏蔽的变化。

Result: 随厚度减小，并四苯的Davydov分裂（Davydov splitting）减弱、Stokes位移（Stokes shift）增大，并出现更离域激子的特征；衍射与模型分析将这些趋势归因于分子堆积、分子间耦合与介电屏蔽的改变。

Conclusion: 分子激子的关键特征可通过层数系统调控，从而将维度调控能力从无机二维材料拓展至分子晶体。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) materials provide unique opportunities to tailor excited-state properties through reduced dimensionality, altered dielectric screening and layer-dependent structural reconstruction. While such effects have been widely explored in norganic systems, their realization in molecular crystals has been limited by the difficulty of controlling thickness at the atomic scale while preserving crystalline order. Here we show that tetracene and three other molecular crystals can be mechanically exfoliated into mono-, few- or multilayer flakes, while retaining crystalline order. This capability enables new studies of molecular crystals across a well defined thickness range within the same structural organization. Thickness-dependent spectra of these samples reveal how out-of-plane confinement modifies the excited-state energy landscape of tetracene: With decreasing thickness, the Davydov splitting diminishes, the Stokes shift increases, and signatures of more delocalized excitons emerge. Electron diffraction and exciton model-based analyses correlate these trends to changes in molecular packing, intermolecular coupling and dielectric screening. Our results also demonstrate that key features of molecular excitons can be systematically tuned by layer number, extending dimensional control from inorganic 2D materials to molecular crystals.

</details>


### [101] [Strong Impact of Halide Ordering on Structural Phase Transitions in Mixed Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09956)
*Felix Uddén,Erik Fransson,Julia Wiktor,Benjamin M. Gallant,Dominik J. Kubicki,Paul Erhart*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials, MLIPs）结合混合蒙特卡洛-分子动力学（Monte Carlo-molecular dynamics）模拟，系统研究了三类混合卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskites）的相行为，揭示卤化物有序化（halide ordering）是决定其结构相稳定性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合卤化物钙钛矿在光伏、发光二极管与光电探测器等领域应用广泛，理解其热力学相行为（thermodynamic phase behavior）对指导组分设计与提升器件稳定性至关重要，但相关原子尺度机制尚不清晰。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）参考数据训练机器学习原子间势，针对 CsxRb1-xPbBr3yI3-3y、CsxRb1-xPbBr3yCl3-3y 与 CsxRb1-xPbCl3yI3-3y 体系开展大规模混合蒙特卡洛-分子动力学模拟，同时采样构型与振动自由度。

Result: 三种二元卤化物体系均存在混溶间隙（miscibility gap），其范围与卤素离子尺寸失配相关：Br-Cl 与 Br-I 的间隙在低温闭合，而 Cl-I 间隙延伸至室温以上。在混溶间隙以上温度（200 K 至 500 K）体系倾向形成层状卤化物有序，卤素优先占据八面体的顶点或赤道位。CsPbBr3yI3-3y 中该有序化出现在器件相关温区，并使相变温度相对随机混合结构偏移达 100 K；实验观测到的正交-四方相界非线性组分依赖（先线性下降后平台）被归因于卤化物有序化。A 位引入 Rb 会削弱卤化物有序化、消除非线性行为，并缩小 Br-I 与 Br-Cl 体系的混溶间隙。

Conclusion: 卤化物有序化被确立为混合卤化物钙钛矿结构相稳定性的关键决定因素，为组分设计与相稳定性调控提供了原子尺度依据。

Abstract: Mixed halide perovskites are highly versatile semiconductors with applications in photovoltaics, light-emitting diodes, and photodetectors. Understanding their thermodynamic phase behavior is central to guiding compositional design and improving device stability. Here, we train machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) on density functional theory reference data for CsxRb1-xPbBr3yI3-3y, CsxRb1-xPbBr3yCl3-3y, and CsxRb1-xPbCl3yI3-3y halide perovskites, enabling large-scale hybrid Monte Carlo-molecular dynamics simulations that sample both configurational and vibrational degrees of freedom. All three binary halide systems exhibit a miscibility gap, the extent of which correlates with halide ion size mismatch. The gaps in Br-Cl and Br-I close at low temperatures, while the Cl-I gap extends above room temperature. At temperatures above the miscibility gap (200 K to 500 K), all systems show a tendency toward layered halide ordering, with halide species preferentially occupying apical or equatorial octahedral sites. In CsPbBr3yI3-3y, this ordering occurs in a device-relevant temperature regime and is linked to the structural phase transitions, shifting transition temperatures by up to 100 K relative to randomly mixed structures. We attribute the strongly non-linear composition dependence of the orthorhombic-tetragonal phase boundary observed experimentally (a linear decrease followed by a plateau) to halide ordering. Introducing Rb on the A-site weakens halide ordering and eliminates the non-linear behavior, while narrowing the miscibility gap in both the Br-I and Br-Cl systems. These results establish halide ordering as a key determinant of structural phase stability in mixed-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [102] [Observation of Magnetic-Anisotropy Crossover and High-Temperature Skyrmions in the Dirac Magnet Fe3Ge with a Distorted Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10081)
*Yalei Huang,Xiaowei Lv,Bin Li,Chunqiang Xu,Dhanarajagopal Alltrin,Xiaoxuan Ma,Wanting Yang,Wei Zhou,Xiangzhuo Xing,Wen-Chin Lin,Raman Sankar,Michael Smidman,Shixun Cao,Dong Qian,Renchao Che,Xiaofeng Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告在狄拉克笼目磁体（Dirac kagome magnet）Fe3Ge中观测到稳定存在的斯格明子（skyrmions），其居里温度（Curie temperature）约650 K，斯格明子可在375–650 K的宽温区内存在，并伴随非平庸能带拓扑导致的本征反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）与拓扑霍尔效应（topological Hall effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兼具高温稳定斯格明子与非平庸电子能带结构的拓扑材料（topological materials）在基础研究和应用前景上均具吸引力，因此成为研究热点。本文旨在报道此类候选材料Fe3Ge中的相关观测。

Method: 采用磁化测量（magnetization measurements）表征磁相变与磁各向异性，测量本征反常霍尔电导率与拓扑霍尔效应，并使用洛伦兹透射电子显微镜（Lorentz transmission electron microscopy）观测介观斯格明子。

Result: Fe3Ge居里温度约650 K；室温下本征反常霍尔电导率约380 Ω-1cm-1，源于非平庸能带拓扑；约375 K发生自旋重取向转变（spin reorientation transition），表现为从易面到易轴磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的交叉；重取向温度以下观测到大的拓扑霍尔效应，源于微观非共面自旋结构；斯格明子在易轴各向异性区间稳定，存在于375–650 K的极宽温区，远超多数已报道的斯格明子宿主材料。

Conclusion: Fe3Ge被视为探索多种拓扑性质的潜在平台，在高温自旋电子学（spintronics）应用方面具有潜力，可望用于下一代信息存储与逻辑计算器件。

Abstract: Topological materials that simultaneously host robust high-temperature skyrmions and nontrivial electronic band structures have attracted tremendous interest owing to their distinctive advantages for both fundamental research and prospective technological applications. Here, we report the observation of robust skyrmions in the Dirac kagome magnet Fe3Ge, which exhibits a high Curie temperature of ~ 650 K. At room temperature, Fe3Ge shows a large intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity of ~ 380 Ω-1cm-1, originating from its nontrivial electronic band topology. Systematic magnetization measurements reveal a spin reorientation transition at ~ 375 K, indicating a crossover from easy-plane to easy-axis magnetic anisotropy. Below the spin reorientation temperature, a large topological Hall effect is observed, arising from microscopic noncoplanar spin structures. Lorentz transmission electron microscopy shows that mesoscopic skyrmions are stabilized in the easy-axis magnetic anisotropy regime and persist over an exceptionally wide temperature window of 375-650 K, far exceeding that of most previously reported skyrmion-hosting materials. These results establish Fe3Ge as a promising platform for exploring diverse topological properties, with strong potential for advancing future high-temperature spintronic applications, ranging from next-generation information storage to logic computing devices.

</details>


### [103] [pyeCE: A Python Implementation of the Embedded Cluster Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10190)
*Yann L. Müller,Claire A. Paetsch,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: pyeCE 是一个开源 Python 库，实现嵌入式团簇展开（embedded cluster expansion, eCE），通过机器学习将多种化学物种映射到较少的有效物种，从而突破传统团簇展开在高熵合金等多组分体系中的维度限制，并提供完整的建模与有限温度模拟工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统团簇展开（cluster expansion）虽能从零温第一性原理计算预测合金的有限温度热力学性质，但当化学物种超过三到四种时其形式化处理变得不可行，导致高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）的此类研究长期难以开展。因此需要一种能限制团簇函数数量增长的新建模范式。

Method: pyeCE 实现嵌入式团簇展开（eCE），利用机器学习将众多化学物种映射到较小的有效物种集合；提供包含可学习逐子晶格化学嵌入（learnable per-sublattice chemical embedding）的对称适配描述符构建、神经网络能量模型、基于 ladder 的训练、不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification），以及通过蒙特卡洛采样（Monte Carlo sampling）进行的有限温度模拟。该库基于 PyTorch 与 pymatgen 构建，支持多子晶格多物种体系并可在图形处理单元（graphics processing units）上运行。

Result: 在两个材料体系上进行了验证：其一为覆盖 9 组分难熔合金（refractory alloy）全成分空间的单一模型，可解析短程有序（short-range order）与有序—无序（order–disorder）行为，并支持快速筛选具有强 Cr 团簇（Cr clustering）倾向的成分，该特征与连续抗腐蚀氧化皮（oxide scale）的形成相关；其二为 Mo–Nb–W 合金中氢溶解模型，可重现氢吸收对成分的依赖并解析氢占据的间隙环境（interstitial environments）。

Conclusion: pyeCE 通过嵌入式团簇展开有效缓解了多组分体系中团簇函数数量的增长问题，使高熵合金的有限温度热力学建模成为可能。其模块化设计使其可扩展至合金热力学之外的问题，包括动力学（kinetics）、缺陷能量学（defect energetics），以及化学有序与磁性和振动自由度的耦合。

Abstract: The cluster expansion is a widely used approach for predicting the finite-temperature thermodynamics of alloys from zero-kelvin first-principles calculations, but its conventional formulation becomes intractable for materials with more than three or four chemical species. High-entropy alloys have therefore remained largely out of reach. We present pyeCE, an open-source Python library that implements the embedded cluster expansion (eCE), in which machine learning maps many chemical species onto a smaller set of effective species and thereby limits the growth in the number of cluster functions. pyeCE provides the complete modeling workflow, including the construction of symmetry-adapted descriptors with a learnable per-sublattice chemical embedding, a neural-network energy model, ladder-based training, uncertainty quantification, and finite-temperature simulations through Monte Carlo sampling. Built on the PyTorch and pymatgen libraries, it supports systems with multiple species on multiple sublattices and runs on graphics processing units. We demonstrate the package on two material systems. In the first, a single model spanning the full composition space of a 9-component refractory alloy resolves short-range order and order--disorder behavior. This model enables rapid screening for compositions with strong Cr clustering, a feature linked to the formation of a continuous, corrosion-resistant oxide scale. In the second, a model of hydrogen dissolution in a Mo--Nb--W alloy reproduces the composition dependence of hydrogen uptake and resolves the interstitial environments that hydrogen occupies. The modular design of pyeCE allows it to be extended to problems beyond alloy thermodynamics, including kinetics, defect energetics, and the coupling of chemical order to magnetic and vibrational degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Impedance Additivity: A Systematic Nonlinear Perspective on Memristor Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10194)
*Luiz A. Meneghetti,Leonardo K. Castelano,Antônio Sérgio dos Santos,Soumen Pradhan,Fabian Hartmann,Ovidiu Lipan,Sven Höfling,Victor Lopez-Richard*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究检验了含记忆电阻（memristive device）的交流电路中叠加原理（superposition principle）与阻抗可加性（impedance additivity）的有效性，通过数值方法分析了串联电阻、电容或电感时的稳态响应，并提出"表观忆阻器（apparent memristor）"概念以评估等效电路（equivalent-circuit）框架的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻抗串联是可加性是线性电路理论（linear circuit theory）的基石，但其对具有记忆特性的非线性系统是否成立仍鲜有探讨，本文旨在厘清该适用性的边界。

Method: 采用状态依赖的忆阻器模型（state-dependent memristive model），在正弦激励下数值分析稳态电流响应，并表征谐波谱（harmonic spectra）、波特图（Bode diagrams）与奈奎斯特图（Nyquist plots）；同时引入"表观忆阻器"概念，从复合阻抗中提取有效参数。

Result: 在部分参数区间内，基频谐波可被表观等效电路准确再现，但其有效参数与孤立忆阻器显著不同，显示出与无源元件耦合所诱导的重整化（renormalization）；在电容与电感耦合系统中，存在表观电路描述完全失效的参数区域，复合阻抗无法表示为独立阻抗之和。

Conclusion: 研究确立了记忆型电子系统中等效电路模型的使用边界，并为具有记忆特性的非线性器件的阻抗谱（impedance spectroscopy）解读提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We investigate the validity of the superposition principle and impedance additivity in AC circuits containing a memristive device connected in series with a resistor, capacitor, or inductor. While the series association of impedances is a cornerstone of linear circuit theory, its applicability to memory-bearing nonlinear systems remains largely unexplored. Using a state-dependent memristive model, we numerically analyze the stationary current response under sinusoidal excitation and characterize the resulting harmonic spectra, Bode diagrams, and Nyquist plots. To assess whether the fundamental response can still be interpreted through an equivalent-circuit framework, we introduce the concept of an apparent memristor, whose effective parameters are extracted directly from the composite impedance. We show that, although the fundamental harmonic can be accurately reproduced by an apparent equivalent circuit over selected parameter ranges, the effective parameters differ substantially from those of the isolated memristor, revealing a renormalization induced by the coupling to the passive element. More importantly, we identify parameter regimes in which the apparent-circuit description breaks down altogether, particularly for capacitor and inductor-coupled systems, demonstrating that the composite impedance cannot generally be expressed as the sum of independent impedances. These results establish boundaries for the use of equivalent-circuit models in memory-enabled electronic systems and provide practical guidelines for the interpretation of impedance spectroscopy in nonlinear devices exhibiting memory.

</details>


### [105] [Structural and Electronic Properties of Bulk $β$(2H)-GaSe from First-Principles DFT Calculations with van der Waals Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10301)
*Julián A. Aros-González,Camilo A. Huertas-Archila,Miguel J. Espitia-Rico*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文采用 DFT-D2 色散校正方法系统研究 β(2H)-GaSe 块体晶体结构，验证其作为单层剥离能量基准的可靠性，并给出电子结构（direct gap, 1.12 eV）的定性描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块体 GaSe 晶体是模拟孤立单层的必要能量参考；需在一个一致的三维框架下刻画层间弱范德华耦合，以支撑后续二维极限研究。

Method: 使用密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT），采用 GGA-PBE 泛函并附加半经验 Grimme DFT-D2 色散校正以描述层间 van der Waals 耦合，对中心对称 β(2H)-GaSe 相进行结构优化与电子结构计算。

Result: 面内晶格参数与实验符合良好（约 1.3% 以内）；面外参数 c 相对实验被高估（该色散方案的已知局限）。电子结构显示非磁性、直接带隙（1.12 eV），带隙数值受半局域泛函低估影响，但带拓扑定性反映了块体材料内禀的二维载流子限域。

Conclusion: 该优化的三维框架可为量化剥离过程提供一致的能量基准，并为二维极限的未来理论研究（如面向自旋电子学应用的表面功能化）提供可靠起点。

Abstract: Gallium selenide (GaSe) is a layered III-VI semiconductor whose bulk crystal serves as the essential energetic reference for modeling the isolated monolayer. We present a systematic density-functional-theory (DFT) study of the centrosymmetric $β$(2H)-GaSe polymorph, employing the GGA-PBE functional supplemented with a semiempirical Grimme DFT-D2 dispersion correction to accurately capture the weak van der Waals interlayer coupling. The optimized in-plane lattice parameter agrees well with experimental values (within about 1.3%), while the out-of-plane parameter c is overestimated by the DFT-D2 correction relative to experiment, a known limitation of this dispersion scheme when the pairwise $C_6$ coefficients are not specifically fitted for the compound class under study. Electronic structure calculations confirm a non-magnetic, direct-gap profile (1.12 eV). Although this magnitude reflects the well-known underestimation of semilocal functionals, the qualitative band topology accurately captures the intrinsic two-dimensional carrier confinement within the bulk material. Consequently, this optimized three-dimensional framework provides a consistent energetic baseline for quantifying exfoliation processes, offering a reliable starting point for future theoretical explorations of the two-dimensional limit, such as surface functionalization for potential spintronic applications.

</details>


### [106] [Constrained Optimization of Higher-Order Cluster-Expansion Hamiltonians for Alloys Using Simulated Bifurcation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10310)
*Kazuhide Ichikawa,Satoru Ohuchi,Tomoyasu Yokoyama,Takuma Saito,Yoshiki Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文将二元合金的立方与四次团簇展开哈密顿量表述为带惩罚项的多元无约束二值优化（PUBO）问题，并采用基于模拟分岔（simulated bifurcation）的求解器 SQBM+ 直接处理高阶多项式目标，从而避免显式二次重构。在 Au-Cu 模型体系上验证表明，该方法可稳定获得低能可行构型并复现 CuAu、Cu3Au 有序趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定基态与低能原子构型是合金设计的核心问题。团簇展开（cluster-expansion, CE）方法将固定晶格上的构型能量表示为有效哈密顿量，但对二元合金而言，高阶 CE 模型会变成多项式伊辛哈密顿量（polynomial Ising Hamiltonians），直接优化此类高阶目标存在困难，传统做法需借助辅助变量进行二次重构。

Method: 以 Au-Cu 为模型二元合金，将立方与四次团簇展开哈密顿量在定成分约束下表述为惩罚增广的多元无约束二值优化（polynomial unconstrained binary optimization, PUBO）问题，并使用可直接处理高阶多项式二值目标的模拟分岔求解器 SQBM+ 进行优化，无需构建含辅助变量的显式二次重构。成分约束通过二次惩罚项施加，其权重由 CE 目标连续松弛的导数系数估计得到。

Result: 针对最多 2048 个原子的体系进行基准计算显示，SQBM+ 能稳健获得立方 CE 模型的低能可行构型，并对多数四次情形仍然有效。由优化构型构建的形成能凸包（convex hull）复现了 CuAu 与 Cu3Au 的有序化趋势，并在非化学计量比成分处揭示出有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）。

Conclusion: 结果证明模拟分岔（simulated bifurcation）是求解带约束高阶 CE 优化、进而开展合金构型搜索的一条实用路径，可直接处理高阶多项式目标而无需显式二次重构。

Abstract: Identifying ground-state and low-energy atomic configurations is a central problem in alloy design. The cluster-expansion (CE) method represents configurational energetics on a fixed lattice as an effective Hamiltonian; for binary alloys, higher-order CE models become polynomial Ising Hamiltonians. Using Au-Cu as a model binary alloy, we formulate cubic and quartic cluster-expansion Hamiltonians as penalty-augmented polynomial unconstrained binary optimization (PUBO) problems under fixed-composition constraints. We optimize these PUBO problems using SQBM+, a simulated-bifurcation-based solver that can treat higher-order polynomial binary objectives directly. This direct PUBO treatment avoids the need to construct an explicit quadratic reformulation with auxiliary variables. Composition constraints are imposed through quadratic penalty terms, whose weights are estimated from derivative coefficients of the continuous relaxation of the CE objective. Benchmark calculations for systems up to 2048 atoms show that SQBM+ robustly obtains low-energy feasible configurations for cubic CE models and remains effective for many quartic instances. Formation-energy convex hulls constructed from the optimized configurations recover the CuAu and Cu3Au ordering trends and reveal finite-size effects at off-stoichiometric compositions. These results demonstrate simulated bifurcation as a practical route to constrained higher-order CE optimization for alloy configuration search.

</details>


### [107] [Spectroscopic signatures of mode-selective electron-phonon coupling in transient reflectivity change on charge-density-wave TiSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10348)
*Yu Mizukoshi,Muneaki Hase*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在液氦温度下对 TiSe$_2$ 的电荷密度波（charge-density wave, CDW）态进行时间与光谱分辨的泵浦-探测（pump-probe）光谱测量，发现非振荡分量在约 1.5 eV 处出现符号反转，振荡分量在光学声子与 CDW 衍生模式间表现出不同的光谱依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究 TiSe$_2$ 电荷密度波态中的光诱导响应及其与能带结构、声子模式的关联，利用时间与光谱分辨手段区分不同物理过程的贡献。

Method: 采用时间分辨与光谱分辨的泵浦-探测光谱（time- and spectrally resolved pump-probe spectroscopy）技术，在液氦温度下测量 TiSe$_2$ 的 CDW 态信号，并分别分析其非振荡分量与振荡分量的光谱依赖。

Result: 非振荡分量在约 1.5 eV 附近出现符号反转，被解释为源自 Se $p$ 基价带到 Ti $d$ 衍生导带之间带间跃迁（interband transition）的光学跃迁能量红移；振荡分量在光学声子（optical phonons）与 CDW 衍生模式之间呈现明显不同的光谱依赖。

Conclusion: 光谱依赖的差异提示 TiSe$_2$ 中存在模式选择性耦合（mode-selective coupling），即不同声子模式与电子体系的耦合方式有所不同。

Abstract: We report on time- and spectrally resolved pump-probe spectroscopy measurement on the charge-density wave (CDW) state of TiSe$_2$ at helium temperatures. The non-oscillatory component of the spectrally resolved signals exhibit a sign reversal near 1.5 eV, which was interpreted as a redshift of the optical transition energy originated from the interband transition between the Se $p$-based valence band to the Ti $d$-derived conduction band. Furthermore, the oscillatory components exhibit distinct spectral dependences between the optical phonons and the CDW-derived modes, suggesting the mode-selective coupling.

</details>


### [108] [Overcoming Transport Layer Bottlenecks to Quantify Ionic Parameters from Transient Ion Current Measurements of Perovskite Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10438)
*Shudi Jiao,Miguel Torre Cachafeiro,Huagui Lai,Fuxiang Ji,Tristan Sachsenweger Ballantyne,Sharun Parayil Shaji,Matthias Diethelm,Fan Fu,Wei E. I. Sha,Wolfgang Tress*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出，钙钛矿太阳能电池（perovskite solar cells, PSCs）中由电压阶跃诱导的瞬态离子电流（transient ion current, TIC，亦称 BACE）所测离子密度，其上限由传输层（transport layer, TL）电容决定，而非钙钛矿本征离子性质；并提出基于平均离子位移的修正及外推方法以获取真实离子电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移-扩散（drift-diffusion）模拟预测 TIC 得到的离子密度在移动离子屏蔽电场后应饱和，但实验报道的数值常高出该极限数个数量级，其物理来源在传输层性质层面尚未被完整解释。

Method: 将传输层电容识别为根本瓶颈，开展系统性实验：p-i-n 结构中改变 C60 电子传输层厚度，n-i-p 结构中改变 Spiro-OMeTAD 空穴传输层的掺杂浓度，并结合漂移-扩散模拟考察离子向传输层渗透或正向偏压下的初始积累。

Result: TIC 可量化的最大离子密度受限于可在钙钛矿/传输层界面累积的电荷量，因此 TIC 往往更依赖传输层性质而非钙钛矿的离子性质；实验在两种器件架构中均证实了这一依赖关系。

Conclusion: 提出基于平均离子位移（average ionic displacement）的修正，并通过对传输层厚度趋势外推至无传输层情形以提取吸收层的真实离子电导率（ionic conductivity），以及依所设离子模型给出的密度与迁移率；同时讨论了离子渗透、正向偏压初始积累提升电容上限，以及被捕获载流子缓慢释放对 TIC 信号的虚高影响，为 PSC 离子性质定量提供实用考量。

Abstract: In perovskite solar cells (PSCs), voltage step-induced transient ion current (TIC) measurements, commonly referred to as bias-assisted charge extraction (BACE), are frequently used to quantify ion density. Drift-diffusion simulations predict that the ion density computed from TIC saturates once mobile ions screen the electric field in the perovskite. However, experimental studies often report ion densities orders of magnitude above this limit, whose physical origin remains incompletely explained in terms of transport layer (TL) properties. In this work, the capacitance of the TLs is identified to be the fundamental bottleneck: the maximum quantifiable ion density is limited to the charge that can accumulate at the perovskite/TL interfaces, so that TIC most often depends more strongly on TL properties than on the ionic properties of the perovskite. Experiments with systematically varied C$_{\rm 60}$ electron-TL thickness (p-i-n) and Spiro-OMeTAD hole-TL doping (n-i-p) confirm this dependence across architectures. To overcome this limitation, the importance of a correction based on the average ionic displacement is discussed, and it is shown how extrapolating the TL-thickness trend towards the TL-free situation yields the actual ionic conductivity of the absorber, alongside density and mobility, depending on the assumed ionic model. Simulations are also examined in which ion penetration into the TLs or initial accumulation under forward bias raise the capacitive limit and extend TIC sensitivity to higher ion densities. The slow release of trapped carriers is also discussed as a potential source of current which can inflate the TIC signal. Overall, the presented analysis provides important practical considerations for interpreting TIC and quantifying ionic properties in PSCs.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Hamiltonian fingerprints of a collective mode across the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09223)
*Yogeshwar Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作追踪了吸引型 Hubbard 双层中一种层奇（layer-odd）集体响应，从弱耦合杂化锚点进入配对区，揭示其从单粒子层间杂化向集体配对超交换（pair superexchange）的渡越（crossover）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
