Motivation: 集体激发（collective excitations）可在其微观自由度发生深刻变化时依然存续，但其驱动机制的演化方式尚需刻画。研究旨在追踪同一集体响应跨越费米子到复合玻色子（fermion-to-composite-boson）渡越时的行为及其微观驱动变化。

Method: 以精确的弱耦合杂化锚点为起点，对相同隧穿耦合层建立精确恒等式，确定相对相位高斯核零点在 2t_h 且与层内跃迁无关；采用无符号问题的行列式量子蒙特卡洛（sign-free determinant quantum Monte Carlo）获取多体谱尺度；并用高斯响应理论（Gaussian response theory）给出连续极点解释。

Result: 无符号行列式量子蒙特卡洛在无需解析延拓（analytic continuation）的情况下揭示了相互作用驱动的多体谱尺度软化；高斯响应理论显示响应性质从层密度向相对配对相位转变。在配对区，特征响应尺度对层内跃迁的分数敏感度增强，并呈现介于两者之间、部分趋向 1/U 律的相互作用依赖。

Conclusion: 结果展示了集体激发可被跨费米子到复合玻色子渡越地追踪，同时约束了其微观驱动机制的变化。

Abstract: Collective excitations can survive profound changes in the microscopic degrees of freedom that generate them. We track a layer-odd collective response from an exact weak-coupling hybridisation anchor into the paired regime of an attractive Hubbard bilayer and find that its Hamiltonian sensitivities evolve in a manner consistent with a crossover from single-particle interlayer hybridisation towards collective pair superexchange. At weak coupling, an exact identity for identical tunnel-coupled layers fixes a relative-phase Gaussian kernel zero at $2t_h$, independent of the intralayer hopping. Sign-free determinant quantum Monte Carlo reveals an interaction-driven softening of the many-body spectral scale without analytic continuation, while Gaussian response theory supplies the continuous pole interpretation and shows a shift from layer-density to relative-pair-phase character. In the paired regime, the characteristic response scale acquires a growing fractional sensitivity to the intralayer hopping and an interaction dependence partway towards the $1/U$ law. These results show how a collective excitation can be tracked across a fermion-to-composite-boson crossover while constraining changes in its microscopic drive.

</details>


### [110] [Two-magnon response from light scattering in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09287)
*Shuyi Li,Lexu Zhao,Chunjing Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）及其 1/S 量子修正，计算二维 d 波交变磁体（altermagnet）在不同偏振通道下的双磁子（two-magnon）光散射强度，揭示有限动量双磁子谱中的交变磁特征指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁（altermagnetism）虽结合了完全补偿磁矩与动量依赖的自旋劈裂（spin splitting），但其光谱指纹尚未确立。本文旨在寻找可用于识别该类磁序的双磁子激发特征。

Method: 在二维 d 波交变磁体中计算有限动量双磁子光散射强度，涵盖不同偏振通道；采用线性自旋波理论并纳入领先的 1/S 量子修正，比较交变磁体与传统反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）的谱响应。

Result: 交变磁体的磁子劈裂（magnon splitting）使双磁子连续谱上边界及相关谱特征在 X=(π,0) 处向高能移动，移动量与交换各向异性 |δJ₂| 成线性关系，而布里渊区中心响应不变；磁子-磁子相互作用虽强烈把谱权向低能重分布，但高能动量选择性重构的能量尺度仍然稳健，并产生线性自旋波理论中不存在的低能双峰结构。在 K=(π/2,π/2) 处，相互作用双磁子共振在常规反铁磁相中二重简并，有限交变磁交换各向异性解除该简并，产生间距与 |δJ₂| 成线性的双峰。

Conclusion: 上述两类特征所依赖的特征能量尺度源自双磁子（two-magnon）扇区本身，而非特定散射算符。结果表明有限动量双磁子谱（two-magnon spectroscopy）有望用于识别绝缘磁体中的交变磁序，并推动对候选交变磁材料的动量分辨共振非弹性 X 射线散射（momentum-resolved resonant inelastic x-ray scattering）研究。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a recently established class of magnetic order that combines fully compensated moments with momentum-dependent spin splitting, yet identifying its spectroscopic fingerprints remains an open challenge. In this work, we investigate finite-momentum two-magnon excitations in a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet by calculating the two-magnon light scattering intensity in different polarization channels. Using linear spin-wave theory and further incorporating the leading $1/S$ quantum corrections, we demonstrate that the characteristic magnon splitting of altermagnets shifts the upper edge of the two-magnon continuum and the corresponding spectral features to higher energy at $\boldsymbol{X}=(π,0)$ relative to the conventional antiferromagnet, by an amount linear in the exchange anisotropy $|δJ_2|$, while leaving the response at the Brillouin-zone center unchanged. Although magnon-magnon interactions strongly redistribute spectral weight toward lower energies, the energy scale associated with the high-energy momentum-selective reconstruction remains robust. The interactions additionally generate a pronounced low-energy two-peak structure that has no counterpart within linear spin-wave theory. At $\boldsymbol{K}=(π/2,π/2)$, we show that the interacting two-magnon resonance is twofold degenerate in the conventional antiferromagnetic phase, while a finite altermagnetic exchange anisotropy lifts this degeneracy, producing two peaks whose separation is linear in $|δJ_2|$. The characteristic energy scales underlying both features are intrinsic to the two-magnon sector rather than to a specific scattering operator. These findings highlight the potential of finite-momentum two-magnon spectroscopy for identifying altermagnetic order in insulating magnets and motivate momentum-resolved resonant inelastic x-ray scattering studies of candidate altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [111] [Finite-Temperature Flat-Band Ferromagnetism in the Kagome Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09289)
*Alon Strugatsky,Roser Valení,Jonas B. Profe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以单轨道 Kagome-Hubbard 模型（single-orbital Kagome-Hubbard model）在平带填充下的动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）研究，揭示平带铁磁性（flat-band ferromagnetism）及一种非常规准有序相（quasi-ordered phase），后者在零温下仍存在持续局域自旋涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome 金属（Kagome metals）兼具拓扑、电子关联与晶格动力学，近期在费米能级附近具平带（flat band）的材料中发现了多种关联电子相。然而，其微观起源尚不清楚，因现实描述需在同等地步上考虑多轨道与竞争相互作用。为将关联效应与材料细节分离并提炼平带区的核心物理，作者转向最小化的单轨道模型。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）研究处于平带填充（flat-band fillings）的单轨道 Kagome-Hubbard 模型（single-orbital Kagome-Hubbard model），并与精确解及平均场基态结果进行对照。

Result: 发现强烈的平带铁磁性（flat-band ferromagnetism）特征，与精确解和平均场基态结果一致；并揭示一种非常规准有序相（quasi-ordered phase）：部分填充、被钉扎于费米能级的自旋极化平带导致持续至零温的局域自旋涨落，与传统铁磁体（ferromagnet）的经典行为形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 上述反常涨落是平带简并（flat-band degeneracy）的内在结果，该工作为理解平带 Kagome 体系（flat-band Kagome systems）中的关联效应建立了最小框架。

Abstract: Kagome metals exhibit a rich interplay of topology, electronic correlations, and lattice dynamics. Recent discoveries of Kagome materials with a flat band near the Fermi level have revealed a variety of correlated electronic phases. However, elucidating their microscopic origin remains challenging, as realistic descriptions require accounting for multiple orbitals and competing interactions on an equal footing. To disentangle correlation effects from material-specific details and identify the essential physics of the flat-band regime, we study the single-orbital Kagome-Hubbard model at flat-band fillings using dynamical mean-field theory. We find strong signatures of flat-band ferromagnetism, consistent with exact and mean-field ground-state results. Moreover, we uncover an unconventional quasi-ordered phase in which a partially filled spin-polarized flat band pinned at the Fermi level gives rise to persistent local spin fluctuations down to zero temperature, in striking contrast to the classical behavior expected for a conventional ferromagnet. Our results demonstrate that these anomalous fluctuations are an intrinsic consequence of the flat-band degeneracy and establish a minimal framework for understanding correlation effects in flat-band Kagome systems.

</details>


### [112] [Tuning Charge Density Wave transitions through lattice strain in NbSe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09375)
*Antoine Gallo Frantz,Aleksander Sinchenko,Luc Ortega,Pavel Grigoriev,Vladislav Kochev,Pierre Godard,Pierre Olivier Renault,Dominique Thiaudiere,David Le Bolloch,Vincent Jacques*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）与输运测量（transport measurements）结合，考察准一维电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）体系NbSe3在沿链轴b及垂直方向施加拉伸应力（tensile stress）时晶格结构与CDW性质的演化，发现晶格呈现异常泊松系数与强各向异性杨氏模量，两个CDW临界温度的移动与晶格参数高度相关且行为不同，提示能带曲率变化主导输运特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDW态是结构与电子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）形成的复合态，其周期性晶格畸变与电子调制易受晶格应变（lattice strain）调控；NbSe3作为典型CDW体系具备两个CDW转变（145K与59K），其波矢分别沿链轴b及与b倾斜，因而适合研究应力对CDW的调控机制。

Method: 对NbSe3施加沿链轴b方向及垂直于链轴方向的拉伸应力（tensile stress），结合X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）测定晶格结构演化与输运测量（transport measurements）测定电阻行为，从而关联晶格参数变化与CDW临界温度及电学响应。

Result: 晶格结构表现出异常的泊松系数（Poissons coefficients）和强各向异性杨氏模量（Youngs moduli）；电阻演化显示两个CDW临界温度均发生移动，且与晶格参数强相关；两个CDW转变在应力下呈现不同行为。

Conclusion: 研究表明两个CDW转变在拉伸应力下具有差异化响应，输运特征的变化可归因于能带曲率（band curvature）的改变，凸显晶格应变对CDW态的调控作用。

Abstract: The Charge Density Wave (CDW) state is a perfect example of a combined structural and electronic state, both characterized by a periodic lattice distortion and an electronic modulation of condensed electrons, resulting from electron-phonon coupling. They are thus prone to be tuned by lattice strain. NbSe3 is a prototypical example of CDW states, with a chain-like structure displaying two CDWs at 145K and 59K, with wavevectors along and inclined with respect to the chain axis b. Here, we report on the evolution of the lattice structure and CDW properties in the quasi-one-dimensional charge-density-wave system NbSe3 under tensile stresses applied along and perpendicular to the chains axis b by a combination of X-ray diffraction and transport measurements. We find that the lattice structure show exotic Poissons coefficients and strongly anisotropic Youngs moduli while the evolution of the electrical resistance demonstrates shifts of both charge-density-wave critical temperatures that are strongly correlated with the lattice parameters. The two CDW transitions display different behaviours under applied stresses, and suggest that a modification of the band curvature leads to the observed transport signatures.

</details>


### [113] [Emergent Electronic Bond-Order Wave in a Quasi-One-Dimensional Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09383)
*Honghao Wang,Tristan R. Cao,Pedro Schlottmann,Gang Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在准一维材料 Ba9Rh8O24 中发现了电子键序波（BOW），其通过不等价 Rh-Rh 键的身份互换（而非传统 Peierls 二聚化）克服一维涨落并建立长程有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维体系中强涨落通常抑制对称破缺，集体有序难以稳定。该研究试图探索是否存在可突破此限制的电子有序机制，并将结构键作为活跃的电子自由度加以利用。

Method: 采用单晶 X 射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）追踪 Rh-Rh 键随温度的变化，并结合热容测量与介电响应表征，以及非线性 I-V 特性与迟滞/阻变开关的电学测试。

Result: 在 TA = 180 K 处观察到不等价 Rh-Rh 键的反转，即短键与长键互换身份；该转变伴随热容异常、介电响应重构及冷却时介电损耗的强烈抑制。BOW 表现出强非线性、频率依赖的 I-V 特性、顺时针迟滞与非易失阻变开关行为。

Conclusion: 该体系呈现从动态键液体到刚性但可电学重构的键序波的转变，确立以键为中心的电子有序（bond-centered electronic order）作为一维量子物质的新组织原则。

Abstract: Collective order is notoriously difficult to stabilize in one dimension, where strong fluctuations suppress symmetry-breaking states. Here we report the emergence of an electronic bond-order wave (BOW) in the quasi-one-dimensional (1D) material Ba9Rh8O24, in which structural bonds become active electronic degrees of freedom that overcome 1D fluctuations to establish long-range order. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction uncovers a striking inversion of inequivalent Rh-Rh bonds across a transition TA = 180 K, where short and long bonds interchange their identities rather than undergoing conventional Peierls dimerization. This bond inversion coincides with a heat-capacity anomaly and a profound reorganization of the dielectric response with strong suppression of dielectric loss upon cooling. The BOW exhibits strongly nonlinear, frequency-dependent I-V characteristics, clockwise hysteresis, and nonvolatile memristive switching. These findings uncover an unprecedented transformation from a dynamic bond liquid to a rigid yet electrically reconfigurable BOW, establishing bond-centered electronic order as a new organizing principle for 1D quantum matter.

</details>


### [114] [Anomalous Hall Response Induced by Correlated Disorder in the Breathing Kagome Lattice Mn$_{3}$Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09699)
*Tsung-Han Yang,Seng Huat Lee,Hengxin Tan,Yuanpeng Zhang,Benjamin A. Frandsen,Václav Petříček,Huibo Cao,Daniel Olds,Matthew G. Tucker,Jiaqiang Yan,Binghai Yan,Zhiqiang Mao,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 近化学计量比 Mn₃Sn 在反三角相中呈现有限的反常霍尔电导 σ_yx 及增强的 σ_zx、σ_yz，尽管其平均六方结构与共面磁序本应禁止该分量；总散射与磁对分布函数分析揭示关联正交畸变与非共面 Mn 磁矩，第一性原理计算表明这种晶格-自旋耦合畸变激活了平均对称性禁戒的 σ_yx，而该效应在低温锥形相中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认识认为宏观输运张量受材料平均晶体学与磁对称性约束，Mn₃₊δX 中 σ_yx 因平均六方结构与共面反三角磁序而被禁止；本文旨在检验近乎化学计量比样品是否突破该平均对称性限制。

Method: 采用输运测量（σ_yx、σ_zx、σ_yz 随温度的演化）、总散射（total scattering）与磁对分布函数（magnetic pair distribution function）分析，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究晶格-自旋耦合畸变对对称性的影响。

Result: 近化学计量比 Mn₃Sn 在 T_N1 ≈ 440 K 以下的反三角相中表现出有限 σ_yx（具大磁滞）及增强的 σ_zx、σ_yz；而在 T_N2 ≈ 280 K 以下的振幅调制锥形相中所有 AHE 分量消失。结构分析显示关联正交畸变与非共面 Mn 磁矩，计算证实其激活了平均对称性禁戒的 σ_yx。

Conclusion: 关联无序（correlated disorder）可作为主动的对称性破缺自由度，使拓扑输运（topological transport）得以出现，这种输运无法仅由布拉格平均结构（Bragg-average structure）预测；其消失于 T_N2 以下说明该机制需与可支持贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的长程反三角反铁磁序协同作用。

Abstract: Macroscopic transport tensors are generally constrained by the average crystallographic and magnetic symmetries of a material. In the kagome antiferromagnetic Weyl semimetals Mn$_{3+δ}X$ ($X=$~Sn or Ge), previous studies showed that the anomalous Hall conductivity $σ_{yx}$ is forbidden by the average \hexsg{} structure and coplanar inverse-triangular magnetic order. Here we report that nearly stoichiometric Mn$_3$Sn nevertheless exhibits a finite $σ_{yx}$ with large hysteresis, together with enhanced $σ_{zx}$ and $σ_{yz}$, in the inverse-triangular phase below $T_{\mathrm{N1}}\approx 440~\mathrm{K}$, whereas all AHE components vanish in the amplitude-modulated conical phase below $T_{\mathrm{N2}}\approx 280~\mathrm{K}$. Total scattering and magnetic pair distribution function analysis reveal correlated orthorhombic distortions and noncoplanar Mn moments. First-principles calculations show that this coupled lattice-spin distortion activates the average symmetry forbidden $σ_{yx}$ within the inverse-triangular phase. Its disappearance below $T_{\mathrm{N2}}$ indicates that the correlated disorder must cooperate with a long-range inverse-triangular antiferromagnetic order capable of supporting Berry curvature. Our results establish correlated disorder as an active symmetry-breaking degree of freedom that enables topological transport inaccessible from the Bragg-average structure alone.

</details>


### [115] [Orbital-selective electronic modes induced by doping and originating from distinct spin excitations: an essential feature of orbital-selective Mott transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09741)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过向Kanamori-Hubbard模型中轨道简并的自旋-1反铁磁绝缘体（spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulator）进行掺杂，揭示轨道选择性Mott转变（orbital-selective Mott transition, OSMT）中带隙内电子模式（electronic modes）的轨道选择性涌现行为，并指出这些模式是OSMT的本质特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道选择性Mott转变（OSMT）的本质仍不明确，论文旨在阐明其内禀特征。

Method: 在Kanamori-Hubbard模型框架内，对轨道简并的自旋-1反铁磁绝缘体进行掺杂，分析带隙内电子模式的涌现方式与色散关系。

Result: 掺杂后电子模式以轨道选择性方式出现在带隙中，并呈现动量移动的自旋模式色散关系（momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations）；源自常规自旋激发（含低能模式）的电子模式出现在掺杂轨道，而源自轨道间自旋激发的模式出现在未掺杂轨道。

Conclusion: 这些涌现模式构成轨道选择性Mott转变（OSMT）的必要特征。

Abstract: The nature of the orbital-selective Mott transition (OSMT) remains elusive. This study shows that, by doping an orbitally degenerate spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulator in the Kanamori-Hubbard model, electronic modes emerge in the band gap in an orbital-selective manner, exhibiting momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. Electronic modes originating from conventional spin excitations, including the low-energy one, appear in the doped orbital, while those from inter-orbital spin excitations appear in the undoped orbital. These emergent modes constitute an essential feature of the OSMT.

</details>


### [116] [Semi-Dirac States and Quantum Linear Magnetoresistance in Helimagnetic Pnictide MnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09781)
*Prasanta Chowdhury,Jyotirmoy Sau,Sanat Kumar Adhikari,Sourav Chowdhury,Peter Bencok,Matthias Gutmann,Souvik Chatterjee,Saurav Giri,Manoranjan Kumar,Subham Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文对高质量磷化锰（MnP）单晶进行了系统的磁输运实验与第一性原理计算，揭示其相依赖的磁阻与霍尔效应，并以量子极限下的半狄拉克（semi-Dirac）能带解释了低温大线性正磁阻（LPMR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑与磁性材料中的大线性正磁阻（LPMR）机制尚存争议，尤其在非共线自旋体系中自旋相关散射使电荷输运分析复杂化。螺旋磁性二元磷族化合物 MnP 具有多个场诱导磁相变，为研究复杂磁性与电子拓扑的相互作用提供了理想平台。

Method: 结合实验与理论手段：对高质量 MnP 单晶开展霍尔测量与磁输运测量（温度低至 4 K、磁场至 15 T），并进行第一性原理计算以获取能带结构。

Result: 霍尔测量显示高温下由斜散射（skew scattering）主导的反常霍尔效应，并在非共线 fan（FAN）相与低温 screw（SCR）相中出现有限拓扑霍尔效应。低温下观测到可达约 800%（4 K、15 T）的非饱和 LPMR，在场极化铁磁相（FM2）中呈现显著线性场依赖。第一性原理计算揭示 Y 点处存在强各向异性的半狄拉克型能带，其从 SCR 到 FAN、FM2 态逐步靠近费米面。

Conclusion: 由此产生的小费米口袋可在实验可及磁场下进入极端量子极限（quantum-limit）区域，从而在 Abrikosov 量子磁阻理论框架下为观测到的 LPMR 提供微观解释。

Abstract: Large linear positive magnetoresistance (LPMR) in topological and magnetic materials remains a subject of intense debate, particularly in noncollinear spin systems where spin-dependent scattering complicates charge transport. Manganese phosphide (MnP), a helimagnetic binary pnictide with multiple field-induced magnetic transitions, provides a useful platform to investigate the interplay between complex magnetism and electronic topology. Here, we present a comprehensive experimental and theoretical investigation of phase-dependent magnetotransport in high-quality MnP single crystals. Hall measurements reveal an anomalous Hall effect dominated by skew scattering at high temperatures and a finite topological Hall effect in the noncollinear fan (FAN) and low-temperature screw (SCR) phases. At low temperatures, we observe a large, non-saturating LPMR reaching nearly 800 percent at 4 K and 15 T, with a pronounced linear field dependence in the field-polarized ferromagnetic (FM2) state. First-principles calculations reveal a strongly anisotropic semi-Dirac-like band at the Y point that progressively approaches the Fermi level from the SCR to FAN and FM2 states. Our analysis indicates that the resulting small Fermi pocket can access the extreme quantum-limit regime at experimentally accessible fields, providing a microscopic framework for the observed LPMR within Abrikosov's quantum magnetoresistance theory.

</details>


### [117] [SIM-GRAPH: A universal guide to symmetric interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09996)
*R. C. Verstraten,C. Morais Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出 SIM-GRAPH 方法，利用有限量子系统中的离散对称性（如反射、旋转对称性）投影相互作用到对称子集，从而减少有效格点数并实现相对精确对角化的指数级加速，并给出两种扩展以权衡计算成本与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在物理学中处于核心地位，从离散格点上的傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）到标准模型（standard model）的基本粒子分类皆是如此。有限量子系统（finite quantum systems）中的对称性常为离散的，如反射与旋转对称性；作者希望利用这些对称性高效计算相互作用量子系统的基态可观测量。

Method: 提出 SIM-GRAPH 方法（Symmetric Ising Models - Graph Reduction And Projected Hamiltonians，即对称 Ising 模型—图约化与投影哈密顿量）。该方法将所有相互作用投影到系统的一个对称子集上，从而有效减少格点数目；并引入两种扩展以拓宽方法的适用范围，同时允许在计算成本与精度之间进行可控权衡。

Result: 通过将相互作用投影到对称子集并有效减少格点数，该方法相对标准精确对角化（exact diagonalization）提供了指数级加速（exponential speedup）。两种扩展可拓宽适用性并实现计算成本与精度之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: SIM-GRAPH 可借助离散对称性高效计算相互作用量子系统的基态可观测量，在保持可控精度的同时显著降低计算成本，并通过扩展提升方法的通用性。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a central role throughout physics, from Fourier analysis on discrete lattices to the classification of elementary particles in the standard model. In finite quantum systems, symmetries are often discrete, such as reflection and rotational symmetries. Here, we present the SIM-GRAPH method (Symmetric Ising Models - Graph Reduction And Projected Hamiltonians), which uses such symmetries to efficiently calculate ground-state observables of interacting quantum systems. By projecting all interactions onto a symmetric subset of the system, the method effectively reduces the number of sites, thus providing an exponential speedup over standard exact diagonalization. Furthermore, we introduce two extensions that broaden the applicability of the method and allow for a controlled trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Non-equilibrium dissipative stabilization of s- and d-wave superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10062)
*Aleksey Lunkin,Yury Holubeu,Denis Golež,Zala Lenarčič*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在耦合两个热浴的BCS超导体中，弱耦合且破坏细致平衡的非平衡稳态可产生非热配对能隙，即使一个热浴温度远高于平衡临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在超导平衡态之外稳定超导电性，属于非平衡态工程的核心目标之一。

Method: 将BCS超导体耦合至两个热浴，其中一浴温度可远高于平衡Tc；通过弱耦合热浴破坏细致平衡条件（detailed balance condition），形成非平衡稳态，并用带有Bogoliubov准粒子化学势的广义Gibbs系综（generalized Gibbs ensemble）描述该稳态。

Result: 在该稳态下出现非热配对能隙（nonthermal pairing gap）；该效应在s波与d波超导体中均为强非微扰（non-perturbative）效应；文中还讨论了其在腔基实验（cavity-based experiments）中的可能实现。

Conclusion: 弱耦合且破坏细致平衡的双热浴环境可稳定超越平衡极限的超导态，为利用非平衡条件调控超导提供可行路径。

Abstract: Stabilizing superconductivity beyond its equilibrium regime has been one of the quests of nonequilibrium state engineering. We report the presence of nonthermal pairing gap in BCS superconductors coupled to two thermal baths, where one of the baths can be at a temperature well above the equilibrium $T_c$. Superconducting state is enabled by a highly non-equilibrium steady state stabilized by weak coupling to baths that break detailed balance condition. The steady-state is well described by a generalized Gibbs ensemble parametrized with chemical potentials for associated Bogoliubov quasiparticle. A strong, non-perturbative effect is reported for both $s$-wave and $d$-wave superconductors, and its possible realization in cavity-based experiments is discussed.

</details>


### [119] [Fast, Accurate, and Scalable Fermionic Neural Networks via Translation Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10186)
*David D. Dai,Yen-Ting Lin,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过将神经网络量子态（neural quantum state, NQS）构造为哈密顿量对称性的精确本征态，TorFormer 在二维电子气中同时提升训练效率与变分能量精度，并可无监督描述费米液体与维格纳晶体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 NQS 的训练速度与最终变分能量是量子多体数值方法的核心挑战。作者假设：将波函数设计为哈密顿量对称性（symmetry）的精确本征态（exact eigenstate）可同时改善收敛效率与精度。

Method: 针对二维电子气（2D electron gas）设计 TorFormer——一种总动量（total momentum）精确本征态的神经网络波函数，并以 NQS 变分方法进行训练。

Result: TorFormer 无需监督即可描述费米液体（Fermi liquid）与维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal），并在大体系尺寸下显著优于基于 Psiformer 的参考方法。在 N=91、r_s=30.0 与 40.0 时，仅训练 8K 步即对比此前需 100K 步的最佳 NQS；r_s=40.0 处除平凡的 Madelung 部分外总能量改进 0.12%，约为 Slater-Jastrow-backflow 扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）能量降幅的 9.8 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明神经网络量子态能够精确且高效地求解大规模问题，将对称性内嵌于网络结构是提升 NQS 性能的有效途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that designing a neural quantum state to be an exact eigenstate of the Hamiltonian's symmetries significantly improves both training speed and final variational energy. For the 2D electron gas, we design TorFormer, a neural network wavefunction which is an exact eigenstate of the total momentum. TorFormer describes both the Fermi liquid and Wigner crystal with no supervision and significantly outperforms Psiformer-based references up to large system sizes. For $r_s = 30.0$ and $40.0$ at $N=91$, we compare TorFormer trained for $8\mathrm{K}$ steps against the previous best NQS, which required $100\mathrm{K}$ training steps. Our improvement to the total energy at $r_s = 40.0$, excluding the trivial Madelung part, is $0.12\%$---enormous compared to the tiny differences separating phases. Relative to Slater-Jastrow-backflow diffusion Monte Carlo, TorFormer's energy decrease is roughly $9.8$ times that of the previous best NQS. Our work demonstrates that neural quantum states can both accurately and efficiently solve large-scale problems.

</details>


### [120] [Gauge mean-field theories of the underscreened Kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10443)
*Ewan Scott,Michal Kwasigroch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过引入单一控制参数 $N$ 与无偏变分拟设（variational ansatz），统一了自旋-1欠屏蔽近藤晶格（underscreened Kondo lattice）中已有的多种平均场解耦方案，并将其与 Read-Newns 理论及 Nozières 强耦合理论建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1欠屏蔽近藤晶格中磁序与近藤杂化（Kondo hybridization）可能共存，此前已有多种平均场解耦方案，但彼此关系不清、缺乏统一描述。

Method: 采用单一控制参数 $N$ 与无偏变分拟设，统一并连接既有解耦方案与 Read-Newns 理论（其中涨落以 $1/N$ 展开且量级较小），并计算大-$N$ 极限附近的修正项。

Result: 通过发现重费米子金属情形下呈对数衰减的残余铁磁洪德相互作用（residual ferromagnetic Hund interaction），与 Nozières 强耦合理论衔接；绘制了以 $N$ 与近藤耦合为变量的基态相图（ground state phase diagram），并揭示既有平均场理论在共存相的杂化强度与总磁化上存在关键差异。

Conclusion: 在该统一变分理论框架下，既有解耦方案对应于两种做法：或从一开始取 $N=2$，或先做 $1/N$ 展开再外推至 $N=2$；作者最后总结了该理论向 $S>1$ 的推广。

Abstract: Various mean-field decoupling schemes have been introduced thus far to study the important and challenging problem of the spin-$1$ underscreened Kondo lattice where magnetic order can coexist with Kondo hybridization. We use a single control parameter $N$ and an unbiased variational ansatz to unify and connect the previously proposed decouplings to standard Read-Newns theory, where fluctuations are small in $1/N$. We compute the corrections around the large-$N$ limit. In particular, we make contact with Nozières strong-coupling theory by finding the residual ferromagnetic Hund interaction that decays logarithmically in the case of a heavy-fermion metal. We map out the ground state phase diagram as a function of $N$ and the Kondo coupling. We find crucial differences between the previously proposed mean-field theories in the strength of the hybridization and total magnetization of the coexistent phase. We show that, within our unifying variational theory, the previously proposed decouplings correspond to either taking $N=2$ from the start, or performing a $1/N$ expansion and then extrapolating to $N=2$. Finally, we summarize the generalization of our theory to $S>1$.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Signature of Short-Range Order in Static Response of the Three-Dimensional Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10006)
*Muhammed H. Güneş,Yubo Yang,Vitaly Gorelov,Miguel A. Morales,Matteo Gatti,Lucia Reining,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 使用扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）方法，在强耦合区域首次给出三维电子气静态局域场因子（static local field factor）$G(q)$ 的精确基态基准，并揭示其在中等波矢处随相互作用增强而增长的显著结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维电子气（three-dimensional electron gas）是凝聚态物理与量子化学的基础模型，其交换关联能（exchange-correlation energy）是材料从头算（ab initio）计算的起点；然而依赖波矢的响应，即静态局域场因子 $G(q)$，在强耦合区域三十年来缺乏准确的基态基准。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）方法，计算整个液相（liquid phase）范围内的 $G(q)$ 与静态密度-密度响应函数（static density-density response function），并给出适用于全液相区的参数化（parametrization）。

Result: 在中等波矢处发现 $G(q)$ 存在显著结构，该结构在金属密度下已可观测，并随相互作用强度增加而增强；通过证明该结构是重现静态结构因子（static structure factor）所必需，将其识别为短程有序（short-range order）的特征指纹。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数化在整个液相区有效，并预测在粒子-空穴连续谱（particle-hole continuum）内部存在一支低能共振模式（low-energy resonant mode）。

Abstract: The three-dimensional electron gas is a fundamental model in condensed matter physics and quantum chemistry, and the exchange-correlation energy derived from it serves as the starting point of \textit{ab initio} computations of materials. However, the wave-vector-dependent response, and hence the static local field factor $G(q)$, has remained without accurate ground-state benchmark in the strongly coupled regime for three decades. Using diffusion Monte Carlo, we calculate $G(q)$ and the static density-density response function across the liquid phase and find a pronounced structure in $G(q)$ at intermediate wave vectors, already visible at metallic densities and growing with increasing interaction strength. We identify it as a fingerprint of short-range order by showing that it is required to reproduce the static structure factor. Our parametrization, valid in the entire liquid phase, predicts a low-energy resonant mode inside the particle-hole continuum.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Image recognition based on optical spike processing with exciton-polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09939)
*Olgierd Jeziorski,Jakub Rogala,Krzysztof Tyszka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作数值演示了利用激子-极化激元凝聚体（exciton-polariton condensate）的瞬态响应进行图像识别，将下采样 MNIST 图像编码为光脉冲序列并采样凝聚体动力学，由线性分类器（linear classifier）处理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子-极化激元凝聚体兼具超快动力学（ultrafast dynamics）与强光学非线性（optical nonlinearities），在光学神经形态计算（optical neuromorphic computing）中具有潜力，因此需要验证其瞬态响应能否作为有用特征用于识别任务。

Method: 在下采样 MNIST 图像上展开数值实验：将图像编码为光脉冲序列，采样由此产生的凝聚体动力学，并引入线性分类器对凝聚体特征进行分类。

Result: 十分类任务中，凝聚体特征准确率为 92.2%，原始像素线性分类为 91.4%，二者结合提升至 93.2%；在数字 7 与 9 的二分类任务中，组合模型达 97.3%，高于线性基线（95.5%）与前馈神经网络（feed-forward neural-network）基线（96.6%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明极化激元动力学（polariton dynamics）可提取有用的非线性特征，用于超快光学信息处理（ultrafast optical information processing）。

Abstract: Exciton-polariton condensates combine ultrafast dynamics with strong optical nonlinearities, making them promising for optical neuromorphic computing. Here, we numerically demonstrate image recognition using the transient response of an exciton-polariton condensate. Downscaled MNIST images are encoded as optical pulse sequences, and the resulting condensate dynamics are sampled and processed by a linear classifier. For ten-class classification, condensate features achieve 92.2% accuracy, compared with 91.4% for raw-pixel linear classification. Combining both feature sets increases the accuracy to 93.2%. For the challenging binary classification of digits 7 and 9, the combined model reaches 97.3%, exceeding both the linear (95.5%) and feed-forward neural-network (96.6%) baselines. These results demonstrate that polariton dynamics can extract useful nonlinear features for ultrafast optical information processing.

</details>

### [123] [From Closed to Relative Higher-Genus Gromov-Witten Invariants via Resurgent Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09256)
*Murad Alim,Noah Tischler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用复兴（resurgence）方法研究解析后的配分函数，聚焦于消解锥流形（resolved conifold），揭示其强耦合渐近展开的枚举几何含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亏格 Gromov-Witten 不变量（Gromov-Witten invariants）的组织生成函数仅是形式参数 λ 的渐近级数，需借助复兴（resurgence）引入解析函数；本文旨在理解这些解析函数强耦合展开的枚举意义。

Method: 针对消解锥流形（resolved conifold），对复兴所得的解析函数在强耦合下按 1/λ 幂次进行渐近展开，并识别其各项的几何解释。

Result: 该强耦合渐近展开同时包含对偶变量（dual variables）下的闭曲线计数贡献，以及由相对 Gromov-Witten 不变量（relative Gromov-Witten invariants）控制的贡献，后者自然在对数几何（logarithmic geometry）框架中解释。

Conclusion: 强耦合展开兼具对偶闭曲线计数与相对 Gromov-Witten 不变量两种成分，并在对数几何中获得自然刻画。

Abstract: Higher genus Gromov-Witten invariants of Calabi-Yau threefolds are encoded in a generating function which is an asymptotic series in a formal parameter $λ$. Using resurgence, analytic functions in this formal parameter were uncovered. In this paper we focus on the resolved conifold and study the enumerative meaning of the strong-coupling asymptotic expansion, in powers of $ 1/λ$, of the resurgent analytic functions. We show that this expansion contains both a closed curve-counting contribution in dual variables and a contribution governed by relative Gromov-Witten invariants, naturally interpreted in logarithmic geometry.

</details>

### [124] [Open Inextensible Filaments in Planar Stokes Flow: Well-Posedness, Endpoint Asymptotics, and Straightening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10480)
*Han Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究平面 Stokes 流体（Stokes fluid）中带自由端点的不可伸长开丝（inextensible open filament），在近乎临界初值下证明局部适定性、端点 $d^{3/2}$ 型渐近展开、以及小初值与有限能量初值下的全局存在性与指数收敛到直线形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不可伸长开丝在黏性流体中的动力学，关注临界正则性框架与自由端点奇异性，以及全局解与有限时间破裂（breakdown）的判别条件。

Method: 将系统约化为三阶非局部曲率方程（third-order nonlocal curvature equation）与张力椭圆方程的耦合；在满足弧弦条件（arc-chord condition）的支撑型 Sobolev 空间 $\widetilde H^s$（$-1/2<s\le0$）中证明局部适定性；用 Wiener–Hopf 分解（Wiener–Hopf factorization）推导自由端点展开；通过能量恒等式与弯曲能量-弧弦常数几何估计证明全局结果。

Result: 对近乎临界初值得到局部适定性；正时刻具有改进的 Sobolev 正则性，且在每个自由端点获得 $d^{3/2}$ 型展开（$d$ 为到端点的距离）；对充分小初值及满足 $E(0)<\pi^2/4$ 的有限能量初值，证明全局存在并指数收敛到直线丝；一般地，有限时间破裂必伴随弧弦条件失效，全局解或指数收敛到直线丝，或弧弦常数沿趋于无穷的时间序列趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了不可伸长开丝在平面 Stokes 流中的临界适定性与端点奇异结构，并给出能量阈值 $E(0)<\pi^2/4$ 下全局指数弛豫到直线的判据，以及破裂与弧弦条件损失之间的必然联系。

Abstract: We study an inextensible open filament with free ends in a planar Stokes fluid. The system reduces to a third-order nonlocal curvature equation coupled to an elliptic equation for the tension. We prove local well-posedness for nearly critical initial data in supported Sobolev spaces $\widetilde H^s$, $-1/2<s\le0$, satisfying the arc-chord condition. For positive times, we prove improved Sobolev regularity and derive a $d^{3/2}$-type expansion at each free end using Wiener--Hopf factorization, where $d$ denotes the distance to that endpoint. We further prove global existence and exponential convergence to a straight filament for sufficiently small initial data and for finite-energy initial data satisfying $E(0)<π^2/4$. The finite-energy result follows from an energy identity and a geometric estimate relating the bending energy to the arc-chord constant. More generally, any finite-time breakdown must be accompanied by loss of the arc-chord condition, while every global solution either converges exponentially to a straight filament or has arc-chord constants tending to zero along a sequence of times tending to infinity.

</details>

### [125] [A Thermodynamically Consistent Model for Multicomponent Vesicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10168)
*Wangbo Luo,Zhonghua Qiao,Yanxiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个热力学一致（thermodynamically consistent）的多组分膜（multicomponent membranes）扩散界面模型（diffuse-interface model），并构造了两种稳定数值格式，验证了离散能量耗散律（discrete energy dissipation law）及蛋白分离与膜变形现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为多组分膜建立热力学一致（thermodynamically consistent）的扩散界面（diffuse-interface）描述，并为其耦合梯度流（gradient flow）系统提供稳定且具能量稳定性的数值求解方案。

Method: 基于耦合自由能泛函（coupled free energy functional）推导模型，泛函包含蛋白依赖的弯曲弹性（protein-dependent bending elasticity）、扩散表面张力（diffuse surface tension）、体积惩罚项（volume penalty）及膜相关 Ohta--Kawasaki 能量；应用 Onsager 变分原理（Onsager variational principle）导出耦合 L^2 梯度流系统及能量耗散律；构造稳定交替 ETD1 格式与稳定交替 ETDRK2 格式（交替 Strang-ETDRK2，alternating Strang-ETDRK2）。

Result: 在数值解的一定正则性假设下，证明了两种格式的离散能量耗散（discrete energy dissipation）；二维与三维数值实验验证了离散能量耗散律，并重现了模型产生的蛋白分离（protein segregation）与膜变形（membrane deformation）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了热力学一致的多组分膜扩散界面模型及其具离散能量耗散的数值格式；据作者所知，所提出的交替 Strang-ETDRK2 格式此前尚未针对耦合相场（phase field）系统被开发与分析。

Abstract: We develop a thermodynamically consistent diffuse-interface model for multicomponent membranes. The proposed model is derived from a coupled free energy functional that incorporates protein-dependent bending elasticity, diffuse surface tension, a volume penalty, and a membrane-associated Ohta--Kawasaki energy. Applying the Onsager variational principle, we derive a coupled $L^2$ gradient flow system and its energy dissipation law. We then construct a stabilized alternating ETD1 scheme and a stabilized alternating ETDRK2 scheme with Strang-type composition (alternating Strang-ETDRK2). To the best of our knowledge, the proposed alternating Strang-ETDRK2 scheme has not previously been developed and analyzed for coupled phase field systems. We further prove the discrete energy dissipation for both schemes under some regularity assumptions on the numerical solutions. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions validate the discrete energy dissipation law, and present the protein segregation and membrane deformation produced by the proposed model.

</details>

### [126] [Milky Way Structure from Double White Dwarf Gravitational-Wave Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09955)
*Shao-Dong Zhao,Xue-Hao Zhang,Qun-Ying Xie,Yu-Xiao Liu,Soumya D. Mohanty*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出 Galena，一个层次贝叶斯（hierarchical Bayesian）流程，通过非齐次泊松过程（inhomogeneous Poisson-process）似然从双白矮星（double white dwarf）目录中反演银河系结构参数，并利用 Fisher 信息矩阵解析传播位置误差使推断可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫赫兹（millihertz）引力波天区将由约 10^4–10^7 个已分辨的银河系双白矮星主导，其三维空间分布携带银河系结构参数信息；因此需要一种能够从双白矮星目录中稳健反演这些参数的方法。

Method: 采用层次贝叶斯流程，基于非齐次泊松过程似然；每个源的 3×3 银河系位置协方差由波形 Fisher 信息矩阵（Fisher information matrix）计算，并经 3×5 雅可比（Jacobian）解析传播；由此得到的测量误差卷积被分解为预先计算的稀疏权重矩阵，使嵌套采样（nested-sampling）推断变得可处理。另用粒子群优化（particle-swarm optimisation）独立验证同一似然。

Result: 应用于 GBSIEVER 报告的 LISA Data Challenge 源（质量筛选后 N=2151），Galena 反演得盘标长 R_d=2175^{+51}_{-52} pc、标高 z_d=282^{+7}_{-7} pc（与典型薄盘一致），以及核球比例 A=0.187^{+0.011}_{-0.012}、核球标度半径 R_b=773^{+26}_{-25} pc；核球比例较此前精确定位拟合更接近文献值 0.25，统计精度约 6%。

Conclusion: 独立粒子群优化在千分之几精度内复现值，支持改进归因于 Fisher 矩阵误差传播而非采样器；搜索阶段天体物理先验改善了单源距离估计，但基本不改变层次推断结果。

Abstract: The millihertz gravitational-wave sky will be dominated by $\sim10^{4}$--$10^{7}$ resolved double white dwarf binaries in the Milky Way, whose three-dimensional spatial distribution traces the Galaxy's structural parameters. We present \textsc{Galena}, a hierarchical Bayesian pipeline that recovers these parameters from a double white dwarf catalogue through an inhomogeneous Poisson-process likelihood. Each source's $3\times3$ Galactic position covariance is computed from the waveform Fisher information matrix and propagated analytically through a $3\times5$ Jacobian; the resulting measurement-error convolution is factored into a pre-computed sparse weight matrix, rendering the nested-sampling inference tractable. Applied to GBSIEVER-reported LISA Data Challenge sources ($N=2151$ after quality cuts), \textsc{Galena} recovers the disk scale length $R_d=2175^{+51}_{-52}$~pc and scale height $z_d=282^{+7}_{-7}$~pc, consistent with a canonical thin disk, together with the bulge fraction $A=0.187^{+0.011}_{-0.012}$ and bulge scale radius $R_b=773^{+26}_{-25}$~pc; the bulge fraction, now recovered much closer to the literature value of $0.25$ than in our earlier exact-position fit, is measured at $\sim6\%$ statistical precision. An independent particle-swarm optimisation of the same likelihood reproduces these values to within a fraction of a percent, supporting the attribution of the improvement to the Fisher-matrix error propagation rather than to the sampler. The search-stage astrophysical prior improves per-source distance estimates but leaves the hierarchical inference essentially unchanged.

</details>

### [127] [Distinguishability Transitions from Global Quantum Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09296)
*Catherine McCarthy,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究贝叶斯分类器（Bayesian classifier）利用少量测量结果区分多个随机量子态的能力，发现以 x = m / ln k 为控制变量存在可区分性相变，并给出阈值 x_c，结果可扩展至反集中起始附近的低深度电路、含噪电路及相位随机态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间演化（time evolution）产生的量子态在局部上无特征：局部测量（local measurement）对所有态返回相同的随机结果，难以区分。然而计算基（computational basis）下的全局快照（global snapshot）却出人意料地有效，作者由此探究分类器在该情形下的判别能力边界。

Method: 采用贝叶斯分类器（Bayesian classifier），在少量测量次数（shot）下对多个随机量子态进行区分，并将可区分性刻画为受变量 x = m / ln k 控制的相变（phase transition），进而推导阈值 x_c；方法进一步推广到反集中（anti-concentration）起始附近的低深度电路（low-depth circuit）、含噪电路（noisy circuit）以及相位随机态（phase-random state）。

Result: 在区分 k 个候选态、使用 m 次测量时，分类器的区分能力随 x = m / ln k 出现相变，并确定了阈值 x_c。该相变行为同样适用于反集中起始附近的低深度电路、含噪电路与相位随机态。

Conclusion: 尽管局部测量无法区分通用演化产生的量子态，基于计算基全局快照的贝叶斯分类可在少量测量下实现区分，且其有效性由 x = m / ln k 的相变阈值 x_c 所支配，并具一定的模型与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quantum states generated by generic time evolution are locally featureless: local measurements return random outcomes that are identical for all states. However, global snapshots in the computational basis are surprisingly effective at distinguishing between quantum states. We explore the ability of Bayesian classifiers to discriminate between many random quantum states using a small number of measurement outcomes. We identify a phase transition in the ability of the classifier to distinguish between $k$ candidate states with $m$ shots controlled by the variable $x = m / \ln k$, and derive the threshold value $x_c$. We extend our results to low-depth circuits near the onset of anti-concentration, as well as to noisy circuits and phase-random states.

</details>


### [128] [Transient and universal regimes in quantum reaction-transport kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09305)
*Hossein Hosseinabadi,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 量子二元湮灭 $A+A \to \emptyset$ 在低维下涨落奇异修正显著，使渐进动力学偏离通常的反应/输运限制分类：一维由量子Zeno尺度主导呈 $n\sim t^{-1/2}$ 的输运限制行为，$D=2$ 出现对数修正，$D>2$ 恢复平均场标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子反应-输运系统的弛豫通常按反应与输运的相对时间尺度分类为反应限制或输运限制，但该框架是否在低维成立尚未明确检验。

Method: 以量子二元湮灭 $A+A \to \emptyset$ 为模型，分析低于上临界维度 $D_c=2$ 时反应过程的奇异涨落修正（singular fluctuation corrections）及其对渐进动力学的影响。

Result: $D<2$ 时涨落主导渐进动力学；一维使平均场弛豫仅呈瞬态并驱动至 $n\sim t^{-1/2}$ 的输运限制区，由量子Zeno尺度支配；$D=2$ 出现对数修正；$D>2$ 渐进恢复平均场标度。在临界维度及以下，平均场行为可在参数上很长的交叉时间内保持。同一涨落还产生有效弹性碰撞，在 $D>1$ 时重新分配动量分布并比损失更快，使系统趋近准稳态热态。

Conclusion: 低维下反应-输运系统的弛豫分类失效，涨落主导渐进动力学并改变临界维度行为；涨落诱导的有效弹性碰撞可驱动系统趋向准稳态热态。

Abstract: Quantum reaction-transport systems consist of coherently propagating particles that irreversibly react upon encounter. Their relaxation is commonly classified as reaction-limited or transport-limited, depending on the relative timescales of reaction and particle transport. We show that this expectation fails in low dimensions by studying the quantum binary annihilation, $A+A \to \emptyset$, where reaction processes acquire singular fluctuation corrections below the upper critical dimension $D_c=2$. Consequently, fluctuations dominate the asymptotic kinetics for $D<2$. In one dimension, they render mean-field relaxation transient and drive the system toward a transport-limited regime with $n\sim t^{-1/2}$, governed by a quantum-Zeno scale even for arbitrarily weak loss. At $D=2$, the kinetics acquires logarithmic corrections, whereas above two dimensions mean-field scaling is asymptotically restored. At and below the upper critical dimension, mean-field behavior can nevertheless persist over parametrically long crossover times before the asymptotic fluctuation-dominated regime emerges. We also show that the same fluctuations generate effective elastic collisions despite the absence of microscopic coherent interactions. In $D>1$, these collisions redistribute momentum populations and are parametrically faster than losses in the transport-limited regime, allowing the system to approach a quasi-stationary thermal state.

</details>


### [129] [Exact asymptotic equivalence between precisions of record-times and integrated currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09479)
*Alberto Garilli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在连续时间马尔可夫跳跃过程（continuous-time Markov jump processes）中，建立了积分流（integrated currents）涨落与电流记录事件（current record events）时间统计之间的精确等价关系，证明积分流的Fano因子恰等于记录间等待时间（inter-record waiting times）的变异系数平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于电流涨落与记录时间关系的结果仅适用于单环网络（unicyclic networks），缺乏对任意有限状态图（finite-state graphs）的一般性刻画，且电流精度（current precision）的结构性来源尚不清晰。

Method: 采用纯概率论框架（purely probabilistic framework）。通过论证记录间时间因每次记录事件将系统重置至固定状态而诱导出更新结构（renewal structure），并借助由Narayana数（Narayana numbers）支配的组合组织，精确表达其生成函数（generating function）。

Result: 在最小假设下，积分流的Fano因子与记录间等待时间的变异系数平方精确相等；该等价关系将单环网络的既有结果推广至任意有限状态图，并给出以记录时间可观测量（record-time observables）表示的电流涨落统一表述。

Conclusion: 电流精度（current precision）的结构性起源可由类首达事件统计（first-passage-like event statistics）加以阐明，从而为电流涨落提供统一的记录时间观测视角。

Abstract: We establish an exact equivalence between the fluctuations of integrated currents and the temporal statistics of current record events in continuous-time Markov jump processes. Specifically, we show that, under minimal assumptions, the Fano factor of an integrated current coincides exactly with the squared coefficient of variation of the inter-record waiting times. This result is obtained by showing that inter-record times induce a renewal structure, since each record event resets the system to a fixed state, and that their generating function can be expressed exactly in terms of a combinatorial organization governed by Narayana numbers. The equivalence extends previous results obtained for unicyclic networks to arbitrary finite-state graphs and provides a unified representation of current fluctuations in terms of record-time observables. The results are derived within a purely probabilistic framework and clarify the structural origin of current precision in terms of first-passage-like event statistics.

</details>


### [130] [Gap-controlled thermalization in a SSH model version of the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09498)
*José A. Aké,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在一维二聚化（dimerized）FPUT链中，带隙（band gap）对长时热化（thermalization）的影响并非单调：当二聚化强度达到其最大值一半时出现尖锐的隔离阈值，源于首个umklapp过程（两支带边界声学声子融合为带心光学声子）的开启；该阈值以下声-声-光三波散射在约1e4个振荡周期内激活光学支，阈值以上两支保持动力学隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非谐晶格（anharmonic lattice）中，驱动非线性模式混合的共振结构会被带隙重塑，但谱隙（spectral gap）究竟阻碍还是促进长时热化仍不清楚，本文即针对该开放问题展开。

Method: 研究对象为二聚化Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou链（经典SSH模型的类比，弹簧常数交替变化），在α型（三次）非线性下用量子化手段考察声学-光学带隙对弛豫的控制；通过长时辛（symplectic）数值模拟追踪模式能量与谱熵（spectral entropy）。

Result: 发现当二聚化强度达到最大值一半时存在尖锐隔离阈值，由首个umplapp过程（两支带边界声学声子融合为带心光学声子）的开启所决定；阈值以下三波声-声-光散射在约1e4个振荡周期的时间尺度上激活光学支，阈值以上两支保持动力学隔离。边界条件（boundary condition）会重塑该图像，对特定模式激发产生长寿命的黏滞态（sticky state）。

Conclusion: 结果表明声子带隙（phononic gap）可作为非线性能量输运的可调、动量选择性滤波器（momentum-selective filter），为在二聚化或拓扑带隙非线性晶格中控制热化提供了一般性路径。

Abstract: In classical anharmonic lattices, the resonance structure driving nonlinear mode mixing is reshaped by band gaps. However, it remains unclear whether a spectral gap hinders or promotes long-time thermalization. Here we study a dimerized Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain - a classical SSH model analogue with alternating spring constants - and quantify how the acoustic-optical gap controls relaxation under alpha-type (cubic) nonlinearity. Tracking modal energies and spectral entropy via long-time symplectic simulations, we find a sharp isolation threshold when the dimerization strength reaches half its maximum value, set by the onset of the first umklapp process that allows two zone-boundary acoustic phonons to fuse into a zone-center optical phonon; below this threshold, three-wave acoustic-acoustic-optical scattering activates the optical branch on timescales of order 1e4 oscillation periods, while above it the bands remain dynamically isolated. We further show that boundary conditions reshape this picture, producing long-lived sticky states for specific mode excitations. These results establish the phononic gap as a tunable, momentum-selective filter for nonlinear energy transport, suggesting a general route to controlling thermalization in dimerized or topologically gapped nonlinear lattices.

</details>


### [131] [Topological instability and reentrant crystallization in active solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09500)
*Zory Davoyan,Aditya Jha,Anton Souslov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究将二维缺陷介导熔化（defect-mediated melting）的KTHNY理论（Kosterlitz-Thouless-Halperin-Nelson-Young theory）拓展至兼具非互易（odd）弹性与Cosserat（微极）耦合的活性固体（active solids），发现超越微扰的非互易阈值处存在“拓扑不稳定性（topological instability）”，并进一步发现高活性下的重入晶化（reentrant crystallization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KTHNY理论描述二维平衡固体的熔化，但活性固体同时具备非互易弹性和微极耦合，其熔化机制是否仍遵循缺陷解绑（defect unbinding）尚不明确。本文旨在检验并拓展该理论框架以涵盖此类非平衡情形。

Method: 采用场论（field theory）与数值模拟（simulations）相结合的方法，针对非互易微扰较小的情形分析熔化温度的偏移，并以缺陷对逸度（defect-pair fugacity）刻画非互易阈值处的零温转变。

Result: 在非互易微扰较小时，熔化仍通过缺陷解绑进行，但熔化温度因活性而移动；当非互易达到阈值时，出现质变的新型熔化机制，即拓扑不稳定性，其特征是任意温度下缺陷对（defect pairs）的增生。在更高活性下，观察到重入晶化，准长程有序（quasi-long-range order）在平衡晶体本应熔化的温度下依然存在。

Conclusion: 活性固体只是拓扑不稳定性的一个实现；作者提出由核心能驱动的缺陷增生（core-energy-driven defect proliferation）可能是二维熔化中一条通用且尚未被探索的路径。

Abstract: We extend the KTHNY theory of defect-mediated melting in two dimensions to the case of active solids exhibiting both non-reciprocal (odd) elasticity and Cosserat (micropolar) coupling. For perturbatively small values of non-reciprocity, melting still proceeds via defect unbinding, but the melting temperature shifts due to activity. However, at a threshold value of non-reciprocity, we discover a qualitatively distinct mechanism for 2D melting, which we call topological instability. This melting occurs through the proliferation of defect pairs at any temperature. We use a combination of field theory and simulations to characterize this zero-temperature transition in terms of the defect-pair fugacity. Surprisingly, for higher activity values, we find reentrant crystallization, where the quasi-long-range order survives even at temperatures for which the equilibrium crystal would melt. Although active solids present one realization of this topological instability, we envision core-energy-driven defect proliferation as a generic and unexplored route for two-dimensional melting.

</details>


### [132] [The Arithmetic of Spectra: Factorization, Statistics, and Symmetric Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10391)
*A. Chaudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究无相互作用量子系统单粒子谱（single-particle spectrum）的因式分解，及其对多粒子统计的影响。作者用“谱字母表（spectral alphabet）”表示单粒子配分函数，将张量分解转化为字母表的乘法分解，并借助对称函数与λ-环（λ-ring）恒等式提升为规范的玻色与费米配分函数。工作为规范玻色/费米配分函数提供了统一的组合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注单粒子谱结构如何决定多粒子统计性质这一问题，特别是谱的张量分解（tensor factorization）与乘积谱的出现机制。其动因在于将谱分解与多粒子（玻色/费米）统计之间的联系置于统一的组合语言下处理。

Method: 将单粒子配分函数表示为谱字母表，使张量分解等价于字母表的乘法分解；再利用标准对称函数（symmetric-function）与λ-环恒等式，把该分解提升为规范的玻色（Bose）与费米（Fermi）配分函数。方法还用于考察乘积谱的来源、反对称化在奇数个因子间的分配，以及与固定谱相容的张量分解。

Result: 论文展示了乘积谱如何由不同分解方式产生，说明反对称化（antisymmetrization）可被分配于奇数个因子之上，并刻画了哪些张量分解与固定谱相容。

Conclusion: 该研究围绕谱因式分解（spectral factorization）展开，为规范玻色与费米配分函数提供了一个统一的组合框架（consolidated combinatorial framework）。

Abstract: We study factorizations of the single-particle spectrum of non-interacting quantum systems and their consequences for many-particle statistics. Representing the single-particle partition function by a spectral alphabet, tensor factorizations become multiplicative factorizations of the alphabet, which can be lifted to canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions using standard symmetric-function and $λ$-ring identities. We show how product spectra arise from different factorizations, how antisymmetrization can be assigned across an odd number of factors, and which tensor factorizations are compatible with a fixed spectrum. The paper therefore studies spectral factorization and provides a consolidated combinatorial framework for canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions.

</details>


### [133] [The Role of Concentration in Determining NaCl Nucleation Mechanism: A Story of Pathways Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09518)
*Porhouy Minh,Sapna Sarupria*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作利用无偏路径采样（unbiased path sampling，$\infty$RETIS）研究 12、13、14 mol/kg 浓度下 NaCl 的成核过程，识别出随过饱和度连续变化的竞争性反应路径。结果表明，无定形聚集与结晶的解耦程度随浓度升高而增强，但各路径共存于同一宽反应通道内，无需引入不同的非经典机制即可解释机制多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水溶液中 NaCl 成核的微观细节仍是化学物理（chemical physics）的开放问题，其中无定形离子聚集（amorphous ion aggregation）的作用程度以及两步式（two-step-like）行为出现的过饱和度区间长期存在争论。作者旨在不预设机制性假设的前提下，厘清不同浓度下的成核路径。

Method: 在 12、13、14 mol/kg（$S = 3.2, 3.5, 3.8$）条件下，采用 $\infty$RETIS 无偏路径采样（unbiased path sampling）研究 NaCl 成核。分析手段包括反应坐标分析（reaction coordinate analysis）、联合与条件概率分布、扩散张量分析（diffusion tensor analysis）以及条件自由能景观（conditional free energy landscapes）。

Result: 所有浓度均由同一反应坐标主导，说明晶核生长与结构有序化是跨越势垒的核心；但反应轨迹的性质随过饱和度变化。低浓度下，成核主要通过团簇尺寸与有序度的紧密耦合增长实现，符合一步式（one-step-like）机制；随浓度升高，无定形聚集与结晶逐渐解耦，先发生显著无定形生长再结晶的路径概率上升。

Conclusion: 这些路径共存于单一宽反应通道，而非经由不同的亚稳中间态（metastable intermediate states），从而调和了此前关于 NaCl 成核机制相互矛盾的报道。成核可被视为一组竞争性反应路径的系综（ensemble），其相对概率随过饱和度连续变化；机制多样性可在经典成核（classical nucleation）框架内涌现，无需诉诸不同的非经典描述。

Abstract: Deciphering the microscopic details of crystal nucleation remains a key open problem in chemical physics. For aqueous sodium chloride (NaCl), the extent to which nucleation involves amorphous ion aggregation, and the supersaturation regime in which two-step-like behavior emerges, remains actively debated. In this work, we examine NaCl nucleation at 12, 13, and 14 mol/kg ($S = 3.2, 3.5, 3.8$) using unbiased path sampling with $\infty$RETIS. Through a combination of reaction coordinate analysis, joint and conditional probability distributions, diffusion tensor analysis, and conditional free energy landscapes, we identify nucleation pathways without a priori mechanistic assumptions. While all concentrations are governed by the same reaction coordinate, indicating that nucleus growth and structural ordering are central to crossing the barrier, the nature of reactive trajectories changes with supersaturation. At lower concentrations, nucleation mainly occurs via tightly coupled increases in cluster size and ordering, compatible with a one-step-like mechanism. As concentration increases, amorphous aggregation becomes increasingly decoupled from crystallization, and pathways involving substantial amorphous growth prior to crystallization become progressively more probable. Importantly, these pathways coexist within a single broad reaction channel rather than proceeding through distinct metastable intermediate states. Together, these results reconcile earlier, contradictory reports on NaCl nucleation mechanisms and support a view of nucleation as an ensemble of competing reactive pathways whose relative probabilities vary continuously with supersaturation. More broadly, our findings illustrate that substantial mechanistic diversity can emerge within a classical nucleation framework, without requiring distinct non-classical descriptions.

</details>


### [134] [Protected domains and the cost of cooperation on weighted networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10463)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究考察有限接触结构（finite contact structure）如何维持利他性继承性状的传播：在捐赠博弈（donation game）框架下，合作被青睐当且仅当合作者在互补实验中比背叛者更常占据全局。弱选择（weak-selection）阈值显示四环为 b/c=3、五点路径为 7、而六个及以上固定点路径均为 1，且趋近阈值 1+ε 需要接触对比度至少约 656ε⁻² 与 ε⁻¹ 量级的中性吸收时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有代价的遗传性状之所以能扩散，源于其后代营造了有利的局部环境。作者追问：哪些有限接触结构能够支撑这种"集体竞争者（collective competitor）"，以及这种优势需要付出何种代价。

Method: 个体进行捐赠博弈（donation game），以代价 c 提供收益 b；互惠加权接触（reciprocal weighted contacts）决定平均收益与被模仿对象，而收益来源按全局规则选择。合作被定义为在统一引入条件下比背叛者在互补实验中更常占优。通过精确涨落响应（fluctuation response）与快构型消去（elimination of fast configurations），识别受保护域（protected domains）的形成与竞争为控制过程。

Result: 弱选择阈值下确界（infima）为：四环（four-cycle）b/c=3，五点路径（five-site path）为 7，而所有至少六点的固定路径为 1。六点路径上全局界迫使近壁垒设计落入该域层级；接触比之间的多项式关系控制无约束优化并导致最终的精确反射对称（reflection symmetry）。达到阈值 1+ε 需最小接触对比度 656ε⁻²[1+O(ε)] 与 ε⁻¹ 量级的中性吸收时间，并确定了二者的权衡与设计容差；有限选择消耗相同余量。在受控小选择窗口内，当对比度超出其精确最小值 ε⁻¹ 量级时，最大固定优势（fixation advantage）为 ε^{5/2} 量级。

Conclusion: 改善入侵阈值可能要求增大接触异质性（contact heterogeneity）、精度与观测时间；换言之，入侵阈值越接近 1，所付出的接触对比度、精度与时间成本越高。

Abstract: A costly inherited trait can spread when its descendants create a favorable local environment. We ask which finite contact structures sustain such a collective competitor and what their advantage costs. Individuals play a donation game, conferring benefit $b$ at cost $c$. Reciprocal weighted contacts determine averaged payoffs and copying recipients, while sources are chosen globally according to payoff. Cooperation is favored when a uniformly introduced cooperator takes over more often than a defector in the complementary experiment. Exact fluctuation response and elimination of fast configurations identify the formation and competition of protected domains as the controlling processes. The sharp weak-selection threshold infima are $b/c=3$ on the four-cycle, $7$ on the five-site path, and $1$ on every fixed path with at least six sites. On the six-site path, a global bound forces near-barrier designs into this domain hierarchy. Polynomial relations among the contact ratios then control unrestricted optimization and imply eventual exact reflection symmetry. Reaching threshold $1+ε$ requires minimum contact contrast $656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)]$ and neutral absorption time of order $ε^{-1}$. We determine their tradeoff and the associated design tolerance. Finite selection consumes the same margin. Within the controlled small-selection window, the maximal fixation advantage is of order $ε^{5/2}$ when the contrast exceeds its exact minimum by order $ε^{-1}$. Thus an improving invasion threshold can require increasing contact heterogeneity, precision and observation time.

</details>


### [135] [Energy Transport Structure and Fluctuation Theorem in Nonreciprocal Harmonic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09593)
*Ziwei Cheng,Geng Li,Zhanchun Tu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对一维谐振链（harmonic chain）中非互易相互作用（nonreciprocal interactions），通过格林函数方法（Green-function approach）揭示了一个精确的输运结构。研究发现热流方向由温度偏置与有效方向不对称性的竞争决定，可实现冷到热输运，并建立了统一的能量与涨落框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用打破了正向与反向响应之间的对称性，从根本上改变了能量输运，但其精确输运结构尚不明确。本文旨在为具有不对称最近邻耦合（asymmetric nearest-neighbor couplings）的一维谐振链建立统一的能量与涨落描述框架。

Method: 采用格林函数方法（Green-function approach）分析一维谐振链的输运性质；在涨落层面，将两个热库（reservoirs）的热交换刻画为相关但可区分的随机流（stochastic currents），并研究其联合标度累积生成函数（joint scaled cumulant generating function）。

Result: 热流方向并非仅由温度偏置（temperature bias）决定，而是由它与有效方向不对称性（effective directional asymmetry）的竞争决定，该竞争可反转热流方向，实现冷到热输运（cold-to-hot transport）。非互易性引入了与相互作用反对称部分（antisymmetric sector）相关的额外功率通道（power channel），导致包含两个热库热流和一个非互易功率流的广义稳态能量平衡（generalized steady-state energy balance）。此外，两个热库的热交换构成相关但不同的随机流，其联合标度累积生成函数满足精确的 Gallavotti-Cohen 对称性（Gallavotti-Cohen symmetry）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了非互易热输运（nonreciprocal heat transport）的统一能量与涨落框架，表明方向性相互作用（directional interactions）可作为主动资源，用于控制非平衡能量流（nonequilibrium energy flows）。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions fundamentally alter energy transport by breaking the symmetry between forward and backward responses. Here, we uncover an exact transport structure for a one-dimensional harmonic chain with asymmetric nearest-neighbor couplings. Using a Green-function approach, we demonstrate that the direction of heat transport is determined not solely by the temperature bias, but by its competition with an effective directional asymmetry. This competition can reverse the direction of heat flow, enabling cold-to-hot transport. We further show that nonreciprocity introduces an additional power channel associated with the antisymmetric sector of the interaction, leading to a generalized steady-state energy balance involving two reservoir heat currents and a nonreciprocal power current. At the fluctuation level, the heat exchanges with the two reservoirs constitute correlated yet distinct stochastic currents, whose joint scaled cumulant generating function obeys an exact Gallavotti-Cohen symmetry. Together, these results establish a unified energetic and fluctuation framework for nonreciprocal heat transport and demonstrate that directional interactions can serve as an active resource for controlling nonequilibrium energy flows.

</details>


### [136] [Renewal process's guide to fractional Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09620)
*Hong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文借助碰撞更新过程（collision renewal process）推导玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation），并证明指数型等待时间对应经典纳维-斯托克斯方程（Navier-Stokes equations）、幂律型等待时间对应分数阶纳维-斯托克斯方程（fractional Navier-Stokes equations），从而给出求解这两类方程的随机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳维-斯托克斯方程仍未解决，是流体力学中的核心难题，也是千禧年难题（Millennium problems）之一。受 Hilbert 1900 年提出的思路启发，作者试图通过建立微观动力学与宏观连续方程之间的联系来解决该问题，其关键桥梁为玻尔兹曼方程的推导与概率论。

Method: 采用具有任意分布等待时间（arbitrarily distributed waiting times）的碰撞更新过程，推导描述粒子速度与位移概率随时间演化的玻尔兹曼方程。在此基础上，分别针对指数型碰撞等待时间与幂律型等待时间，论证其与经典及分数阶纳维-斯托克斯方程的等价性。

Result: 证明了具有指数型碰撞等待时间的更新过程等价于经典纳维-斯托克斯方程；具有幂律型等待时间的更新过程等价于分数阶纳维-斯托克斯方程。

Conclusion: 由于碰撞更新过程属随机过程，便于通过轨迹的随机模拟（stochastic simulations of trajectories）获得相应解，因此该工作实际上提供了一种求解经典与分数阶纳维-斯托克斯方程的随机方法。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes equations, which remain unsolved, are crucial equations in fluid mechanics. Discovering the solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations is one of the challenging Millennium problems. In 1900, Hilbert proposed a potential approach to tackle this problem by establishing the relationship between microscopic dynamics and the macroscopic continuum equations. The key bridge is the derivation of Boltzmann equation and the theory of probability. In this paper, we shall use the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times to derive the Boltzmann equation for the time evolution of the probability of the velocity and the displacement of the particle, based on which we prove that the renewal process with exponential collision waiting time is equivalent to the classical Navier-Stokes equations, and that with power-law waiting time is equivalent to the fractional Navier-Stokes equations. Since the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times is a random process and is easy to perform the stochastic simulations of trajectories to obtain the corresponding solution, we actually find a stochastic approach to solve the classical and fractional Navier-Stokes equations.

</details>


### [137] [Intermittent continuous-time random walks under renewal reset mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09738)
*Guohua Li,Hong Zhang,Yuexiong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出并研究了随机重置下的间歇性连续时间随机行走（intermittent continuous-time random walks, CTRWs），以跳跃与重置等待时间中的较小者作为更新间隔，推导了更新重置与马尔可夫重置下的控制方程，证明了非平衡稳态的存在性，并计算了均方位移（mean square displacement, MSD）与平均首次到达时间（mean first-arrival time, MFAT）以量化搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置（stochastic resetting）作为复杂无序环境中实用高效的搜索策略长期受到关注。文章基于跳跃与重置之间的竞争机制，旨在构建并分析间歇性CTRW模型，以刻画兼具跳跃与返回行为的搜索过程。

Method: 以跳跃与重置等待时间中较小者作为更新时刻（renewal time），两者等待时间可服从任意分布，且每次更新后重新独立抽取新的跳跃与重置等待时间。文章研究了更新重置机制下的控制方程与Montroll-Weiss方程，并考察间歇性CTRW的马尔可夫重置。针对指数与幂律型跳跃、重置等待时间分布，分析非平衡稳态的存在性，并计算指数与高斯跳跃长度下的MSD（单调性与渐近稳定性）以及首次到达时间。

Result: 在更新重置机制下，当跳跃与重置等待时间服从任意指数分布与幂律分布时，存在非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium stationary states）。对于指数跳跃与重置等待时间分布（waiting time distributions, WTDs）、幂律跳跃与指数重置WTDs、以及指数跳跃与幂律重置WTDs，间歇性CTRW对任意固定位置均给出有限的MFAT；而当跳跃与重置WTDs均为幂律时，MFAT发散。

Conclusion: 基于竞争机制的间歇性CTRW模型可应用于多种物理场景，例如动物觅食失败后返回巢穴的觅食策略，以及智能机器人在长时间运行后返回能量补给点的工作规划。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting as a practical and efficient search strategy in complex and disordered environments has long been a topic of interest to researchers. Based on the competition between jumping and resetting, this article proposes and investigates intermittent continuous-time random walks (CTRWs) under stochastic resetting, using the smaller waiting time for jump and reset as the renewal time, where the waiting times for both jump and reset can have arbitrary distributions. After each renewal event, the system will proceed with new waiting times for jump and reset regardless of their previous histories. We study the governing equation and Montroll-Weiss equation with renewal resetting, as well as the Markovian resetting for intermittent CTRWs. We prove the existence of non-equilibrium stationary states within the renewal reset mechanism when the jump and reset waiting times follow any exponential and power law distributions. For exponential and Gaussian distributed jump lengths, we examine the mean square displacements (MSDs) of particles to determine their monotonicity and asymptotic stability. Moreover, we calculate the first-arrival time to quantify search efficiency, and validate the intermittent CTRWs under renewal resetting lead to a finite mean first-arrival time (MFAT) to any fixed position for exponential jump and reset waiting time distributions (WTDs), power-law jump and exponential reset WTDs, as well as exponential jump and power-law reset WTDs. However, the MFAT diverges for power-law jump and reset WTDs. The intermittent CTRW model, which is based on the competition mechanism, can be applied to many physical scenarios, such as the foraging strategy of animals that return to their nests after an unsuccessful foraging attempt, or the work planning of intelligent robots that return to energy replenishment points after prolonged operation.

</details>


### [138] [Quantifying cell shape and density fluctuations in epithelial tissue in vivo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09874)
*Jake Turley,Helen Weavers,Paul Martin,Isaac V. Chenchiah,Tanniemola B. Liverpool*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个描述上皮细胞形状动力学的理论框架，将细胞建模为可相互作用的软椭球体（soft ellipsoids），通过粗粒化（coarse-graining）得到连续随机微分方程并推导时空关联函数，用于从细胞形状涨落中提取组织有效"刚度"等特征参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织通常具有异质性（heterogeneous），细胞形状与尺寸存在多种变化；其中相对平均值的局部偏离（涨落）可能在组织内传播，从而反映组织有效"刚度"（stiffness）或刚性等特性。因此需要能同时刻画细胞形状平均行为与涨落模式的理论框架。

Method: 将细胞视为尺寸与长宽比（aspect ratio）各异的相互作用软椭球体（soft ellipsoids）；对模型进行粗粒化（coarse-graining），得到一组连续随机微分方程（continuum stochastic differential equations），并由此推导时空关联函数（spatial-temporal correlation functions）。

Result: 所推导的关联函数与来自果蝇（Drosophila）蛹翅发育过程的实验数据相吻合。

Conclusion: 基于这些关联函数，可以确定表征主动细胞形状变化（active cell shape changes）与组织有效"刚度"的关键参数。

Abstract: Controlling changes in cell shape are crucial for many biological processes, such as tissue development and wound healing. Tissues typically are heterogeneous with a variety of cell shapes and sizes. Of particular interest are local deviations from an average cell shape and size. These fluctuations may extend and transmit across tissues, potentially offering valuable insights into tissue characteristics such as variations in effective "stiffness" or rigidity. In this study, we present a theoretical framework that captures the dynamics of epithelial cell shapes within tissue, incorporating both their average behaviour and fluctuation patterns. We model cells as interacting soft ellipsoids of varying size and aspect ratio. Coarse-graining our model, we obtain a set of continuum stochastic differential equations from which we derive spatial-temporal correlation functions. These correlation functions fit with our experimental data from the developmental process of the \textit{Drosophila} pupal wing. From the correlation functions, critical parameters representing active cell shape changes and effective tissue "stiffness" can be determined.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient protocol for the Markovian Mpemba effect in $N$-level systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09988)
*David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文针对马尔可夫 Mpemba 效应（Markovian Mpemba effect）参数难寻的问题，在三级系统中给出其充要条件并阐明物理机制，进而提出可在 N 级系统中高效确定效应参数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba 效应表现为热化时间（time to thermalize）与偏离平衡程度的非单调关系，即初始温差更大的系统可能先于温差更小的系统热化。该效应较为罕见，导致难以找到能产生它的参数，从而给实验设计与应用开发带来困难。

Method: 论文在三级系统（three-level systems）中推导马尔可夫 Mpemba 效应的充要条件（necessary and sufficient conditions），并解释其背后的物理机制；在此基础上，基于对三级情形的理解，发展出一种高效算法（efficient algorithm），用于确定 N 级系统（N-level systems）中产生该效应所需的参数。

Result: 论文给出了三级系统中马尔可夫 Mpemba 效应的充要条件与物理机制解释，并提出了一个可高效确定 N 级系统对应参数的方法。

Conclusion: 该方案有望为在大系统中实现 Mpemba 实验及相关应用打开路径。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect is an anomalous phenomenon in which the time to thermalize does not depend monotonically on the distance from equilibrium, allowing systems with an initially larger temperature difference to thermalize before those with a smaller one. The rarity of the effect makes it hard to find parameters that produce it, complicating the design of experiments and the development of applications. Here, we find necessary and sufficient conditions for the Markovian Mpemba effect in three-level systems and explain its underlying physical mechanisms. Based on our understanding of the three-level case, we develop an efficient algorithm to determine the parameters for the effect in $N$-level systems. This protocol could open the door for the realization of Mpemba experiments and related applications in large systems.

</details>


### [140] [Infinite ergodic theory and functional statistics of non-confined Feller process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10106)
*Vicenç Méndez*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究一类非约束 Feller 过程（non-confined Feller process）的局部时场（local-time field）空间结构，证明长时间极限下局部时可因子化为由非归一化平稳密度（non-normalizable stationary density）控制的空间剖面与单一 Mittag-Leffler 随机振幅控制的时间涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象具有非归一化平稳概率密度与无限均值回归时间（return times with infinite mean），属于无限遍历理论（infinite ergodic theory）范畴；已有研究多关注加性可观测量，而本文关注局部时场的空间结构这一未被充分刻画的问题。

Method: 该过程经确定性重标度（deterministic rescaling）后等价于平方 Bessel 过程（squared Bessel process）。作者在长时间极限下分析局部时的因子化结构，并考察其空间关联与占据时间（occupation times），同时对比非可积幂次可观测量的极限行为。

Result: 局部时在长时间极限下可因子化：空间剖面由非归一化平稳密度决定，时间涨落由单一 Mittag-Leffler 随机振幅控制。归一化空间关联收敛至与两个观测水平均无关的普适常数；有限区间的占据时间呈现 Darling-Kac 涨落。非可积幂次可观测量则表现出自相似平方 Bessel 型极限，而非 Mittag-Leffler 统计。

Conclusion: 该空间场视角为状态依赖乘性噪声（state-dependent multiplicative noise）下的无限遍历理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study additive observables of a non-confined Feller process characterized by a non- normalizable stationary probability density and return times with infinite mean. The process is equivalent, after a deterministic rescaling, to a squared Bessel process, but we focus here on a question: the spatial structure of its local-time field. We show that the local time admits a fac- torization in the long time limit. Its spatial profile is governed by the non-normalizable stationary density, whereas its temporal fluctuations are controlled by a single Mittag-Leffler random ampli- tude. As a consequence, normalized spatial correlations of the local time converge to a universal constant independent of the two observation levels. Occupation times of finite intervals follow as corollaries and display Darling-Kac fluctuations. In contrast, non-integrable power observables exhibit self-similar squared-Bessel-type limits rather than Mittag-Leffler statistics. This spatial- field perspective provides a unifying framework for infinite ergodic theory under state-dependent multiplicative noise.

</details>


### [141] [Anizotropic Ising Model on 2D Kagomé Lattice as an Inhomogeneous XYZ Integrable Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10163)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究 kagomé 格子上具有两组交替耦合的非均匀二维 Ising 模型，将其局域 Boltzmann 权重映射为满足自由费米子条件的非对称八顶点 R-矩阵，并分析其 Yang–Baxter 结构及对应一维量子自旋链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非均匀二维 Ising 模型的可积性与统计力学性质展开研究，聚焦 kagomé 格子上交替耦合 $J_i$ 与 $J'_i$ 的广义模型，以揭示其可积结构并建立与量子自旋链的对应。

Method: 沿三个格子方向引入交替耦合 $J_i$、$J'_i$（$i=1,2,3$），将局域 Boltzmann 权重映射为对任意六个耦合值均满足自由费米子（free-fermion）条件的非对称八顶点 R-矩阵（eight-vertex R-matrix）；在 $J'_i=J_i$ 与 $J'_i=-J_i$ 两种情形下分析 Yang–Baxter 结构，并在各向异性极限下导出相应一维量子自旋链。

Result: $J'_i=J_i$ 情形给出横场 Ising 型哈密顿量（transverse-field Ising-type Hamiltonian）；$J'_i=-J_i$ 情形产生带有分级置换结构（graded permutation structure）且配分函数零点发生移动的修正链。对部分各向异性模型，得到自由能（free energy）与比热（specific heat）。

Conclusion: 该非对称八顶点 R-矩阵框架统一处理了 kagomé 格子上交替耦合 Ising 模型，两种耦合关系分别对应不同的量子自旋链结构，其中第二种情形改变配分函数零点分布；部分各向异性情形下可获得自由能与比热等热力学量。

Abstract: We investigate a generalized inhomogeneous two-dimensional Ising model on the kagomé lattice with two alternating couplings $J_i$ and $J'_i$, $i=1,2,3$, along the three lattice directions. The local Boltzmann weights are mapped onto a non-symmetric eight-vertex $R$-matrix satisfying the free-fermion condition for arbitrary values of the six couplings. We analyze the Yang--Baxter structure for the cases $J'_i=J_i$ and $J'_i=-J_i$ and derive the corresponding one-dimensional quantum spin chains in the anisotropic limit. The first case yields a transverse-field Ising-type Hamiltonian, while the second leads to a modified chain with a graded permutation structure and shifted partition-function zeros. For a partially anisotropic model, we also obtain the free energy and specific heat.

</details>


### [142] [Italian Business-to-Business Invoicing Data: A Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10227)
*Valerio Astuti,Daniele Piras*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于意大利税务局（Italian Tax Office）电子发票全量数据，首次刻画意大利企业间（firm-to-firm）生产网络，涵盖买卖双方数量分布、中心性（centrality）与平均距离，并给出地理与行业分解。结果显示买方与卖方数量分布均为重尾（heavy-tailed），网络呈无标度（scale-free）结构，中心性亦高度集中，且下游（买方侧）尾部指数普遍低于上游（卖方侧）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在利用电子发票这一覆盖全体的数据源，对意大利企业间商业关系网络进行完整刻画，弥补以往缺乏全国范围 firm-to-firm 生产网络实证描述的空缺，并首次呈现该网络的地理与行业结构。

Method: 依托意大利税务局收集的电子发票（electronic invoices）全量数据，利用其中的企业对企业明细信息，统计每家企业买方（buyers/customers）与卖方（sellers/suppliers）数量的分布、各企业在生产网络中的中心性（centrality）以及企业间平均距离（average distance），并对上述分布进行尾部指数（tail exponents）估计，同时按地理与行业维度分解。

Result: 每家企业买方数与卖方数均呈重尾分布（heavy-tailed distributions），表明网络具有无标度（scale-free）结构；中心性取值分布同样呈重尾，说明重要性高度集中于少数企业。所有分布的尾部指数估计结果显示，下游（买方侧）指数均低于上游（卖方侧）。

Conclusion: 研究首次刻画了描述意大利生产体系的企业间网络，并确认其无标度与重要性高度集中的特征，同时揭示买方侧与卖方侧在尾部行为上的系统性差异（下游指数更低）。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive description of the network of Italian Business-to-Business commercial relationships, based on the universe of electronic invoices collected by the Italian Tax Office. The firm-to-firm detail of the data is exploited to describe the distribution of the numbers of buyers (customers) and sellers (suppliers) per firm, the centrality of each firm in the production network, and the average distance between firms. We characterize for the first time the firm-to-firm network describing the Italian production system, and present its geographic and sectoral breakdown. The analysis reveals heavy tailed distributions for the numbers of buyers and sellers per firm, implying a scale-free structure of the network. The distributions of centrality values display heavy tails as well, indicating a strong concentration of importance in a relatively small number of firms. We estimate the tail exponents for all these distributions, finding in all cases lower exponents for downstream (buyer-side) distributions than for the upstream (seller-side) ones.

</details>


### [143] [Quantifying translational and bond-orientational order metrics in hyperuniform and nonhyperuniform many-particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10359)
*Anirban Mukherjee,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出平移序度量 τ_T 与键取向序度量 τ_O 的统一两点统计框架，并系统计算平衡硬粒子、RSA 堆积与二维 SHU 基态三类模型，揭示取向序在三者中普遍处于次主导地位但随有序化进程相对增强的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化多粒子体系的有序/无序程度（order/disorder）是物理学、材料科学与数学中的未解难题，且平移序与取向序此前缺乏统一的统计比较基准。

Method: 定义平移序度量 τ_T 为总关联函数（total correlation function）h(r) 的 L² 范数平方，并引入其键取向类似物 τ_O（基于加权总关联函数 h_f(r) 与局域取向权重），构成 (τ_T, τ_O) 二者统一的两点统计表述；对二维与三维的非超均匀（nonhyperuniform）球堆积（平衡硬粒子、非平衡随机序列吸附 RSA 堆积）计算随堆积分数 φ 的变化，并考察 2D 无序隐形超均匀（stealthy hyperuniform, SHU）基态（0<χ<1/2）。

Result: 非超均匀体系中，键取向序沿平衡流体与 RSA 构型始终为次主导；但在平衡流体支上端附近，τ_O 相对 τ_T 的量级显著上升（二维强于三维），并在所采样的晶体支上二者趋于可比。相同 φ 下，平衡流体与 RSA 堆积的 (τ_T, τ_O) 轨迹互异，显示结构有序性依赖制备历史。无序 SHU 相中取向序同样次主导，但随接近无序-有序阈值而相对增强。三类模型中，超出泊松参考区后 τ_T 与 τ_O 正相关，τ_O 随 τ_T 单调增加。

Conclusion: (τ_T, τ_O) 为平移序与键取向序提供了共同的两点统计基础，可区分不同制备路径的结构有序差异，并表明取向序在本文所考察体系中普遍为次主导但随有序度提升而相对增强，且与平移序在非泊松区呈单调正相关。

Abstract: Quantifying the degree of order/disorder in many-particle systems remains an outstanding problem in physics, materials science, and mathematics. To this end, we consider the translational order metric $τ_T$, defined as the squared $L^2$ norm of the total correlation function $h(\mathbf{r})$, and introduce its bond-orientational analogue $τ_O$, defined from the weighted total correlation function $h_{\mathbf{f}}(\mathbf{r})$ using local orientational weights (S. Torquato et al., Phys. Rev. X 16, 011042 (2026)). The pair $(τ_T,τ_O)$ places both forms of order on a common two-point statistical footing. We compute both metrics for two nonhyperuniform sphere-packing models in 2D and 3D as functions of packing fraction $φ$: 1) equilibrium hard particles and 2) nonequilibrium random sequential addition (RSA) packings. For the nonhyperuniform systems, bond-orientational order remains subdominant along the equilibrium-fluid and RSA configurations; however, its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases near the upper end of the equilibrium-fluid branches, much more strongly in 2D than in 3D, and the two metrics become comparable along the sampled crystal branches. At common packing fractions, equilibrium fluids and RSA packings trace distinct $(τ_T,τ_O)$ trajectories, revealing preparation-dependent differences in structural order. As a representative hyperuniform family, we study 2D disordered stealthy hyperuniform (SHU) ground states for $0<χ<1/2$, where $χ$ is the stealthiness parameter. Within the disordered SHU phase, bond-orientational order remains subdominant, but its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases toward the disorder-to-order threshold. In all three models, $τ_T$ and $τ_O$ are positively correlated beyond the Poisson-reference regime: $τ_O$ increases monotonically with $τ_T$ across the sampled state points.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal Intermediate Hamiltonians for Non-Equilibrium Free Energy Calculations: A Numerical Study of Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10519)
*David Beyer,Helmut Grubmüller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作通过在离散时间马尔可夫模型中构建均方误差（mean squared error, MSE）的倾斜主方程（tilted-master-equation）精确表示并结合自动微分（automatic differentiation），求解使 Jarzynski 估计量收敛最优的中间哈密顿量序列，发现最优中间态在始末时刻发生跳变，可将 MSE 降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jarzynski 关系（Jarzynski relation）虽能从非平衡有限时间切换模拟估计平衡自由能差，但指数功平均受稀有轨迹主导，导致估计量收敛性差。作者旨在寻找连接初末态的中间哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）序列，以最小化估计量的 MSE 从而改善收敛。

Method: 针对离散时间马尔可夫模型（discrete-time Markov model），采用大样本极限下 MSE 的精确倾斜主方程表示，并结合自动微分，对所有中间能量进行基于梯度的有效最小化；随后在双态模型、双势阱势（double-well potential）与平移势阱三个复杂度递增的模型中验证。

Result: 在三个体系中，最优中间哈密顿量均在初始与最终时刻发生跳变。大量蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟表明，相较于线性与对数插值，最优中间态可将 MSE 降低一个数量级以上，且能量景观变化越大效果越显著；同时，最优中间态未必比误差更大的中间态耗散更少功。

Conclusion: 研究提示了面向真实分子体系的高效非平衡自由能计算启发式规则：最优中间哈密顿量在始末时刻跳变；对跨垒问题应快速降低势垒之后再升高；最小化耗散并不能保证更快收敛。

Abstract: The Jarzynski relation enables the estimation of equilibrium free energy differences from non-equilibrium, finite-time switching simulations. These estimates usually converge poorly because rare trajectories dominate the exponential work average. Here, we numerically determined and explored the sequence of intermediate Hamiltonians connecting initial and final states that minimize the mean squared error (MSE) of the Jarzynski estimator and thereby enhance convergence. For discrete-time Markov models, an exact tilted-master-equation representation of the MSE in the large-sample limit, combined with automatic differentiation, enables efficient gradient-based minimization over all intermediate energies. We applied our approach to three model systems of increasing complexity: a two-state model, a double-well potential, and a shifted potential well. In all three systems, the optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations show that optimal intermediates can reduce the MSE by more than an order of magnitude compared with linear and logarithmic interpolation, most strongly for large changes in the energy landscape. Remarkably, they need not dissipate less work than intermediates yielding larger errors. Our results suggest heuristics for more efficient non-equilibrium free energy calculations of realistic molecular systems: optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times; for barrier-crossing problems, the barrier should be lowered rapidly and raised again later; and minimizing dissipation does not guarantee faster convergence.

</details>
