# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Decoding the Imprints of Energy-Momentum Squared Gravity in Neutron Stars with Machine Learning Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09248)
*Sayantan Ghosh,Premachand Mahapatra,Dipti Deb*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究能量-动量平方引力（EMSG）对中子星结构的影响，并利用监督机器学习（supervised ML）从可观测星体性质中识别EMSG特征。基于约10^4个核物态方程（EOS）求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，计算质量、半径、潮汐形变率与f模频率，并用多种分类器区分不同EMSG耦合区间，随机森林（RF）准确率约99.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星（neutron stars）为强场引力检验及寻找广义相对论（General Relativity）偏离提供了独特实验场所。该工作旨在判断EMSG的效应能否在观测过滤后仍通过可观测星体性质被识别，并检验机器学习辅助方法的可行性。

Method: 对约10^4个核物态方程（nuclear EOSs）在EMSG耦合参数 α={-5.01,-2.50,0,+2.50,+5.01}×10^-38 erg^-1 cm^3 下求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，计算引力质量M、半径R、无量纲潮汐形变率Λ与基本f模振荡频率。施加对M、R、Λ的观测约束后划分训练集与测试集，采用随机森林（RF）、K近邻（KNN）、支持向量机（SVM）、逻辑回归（LR）与高斯朴素贝叶斯（GNB）对α={-5.01,0,+5.01}×10^-38 erg^-1 cm^3三个代表区间进行基于(M,R,Λ,f)的分类。

Result: 随机森林（RF）分类器表现最优，准确率约99.85%，精确率、召回率与F1分数均超过99.8%；K近邻（KNN）准确率亦超过99%。近似对角的混淆矩阵（confusion matrices）表明，不同EMSG区间对应的观测可行中子星构型在多维可观测空间中高度可分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使经过观测筛选，中子星可观测性质仍保留EMSG的稳健特征；机器学习辅助的中子星观测可作为探测修改引力（modified gravity）的有前景的互补途径，适用于当前与未来多信使观测（multi-messenger observations）。

Abstract: Neutron stars (NSs) provide a unique laboratory for testing gravity in the strong-field regime and for searching for deviations from General Relativity (GR). In this work, we investigate the effects of Energy-Momentum Squared Gravity (EMSG) on NS structure and examine whether its signatures can be identified from observable stellar properties using supervised machine learning (ML). We solve the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations for approximately $10^{4}$ nuclear equations of state (EOSs) for EMSG coupling parameters $α=\{-5.01,-2.50,0,+2.50,+5.01\}\times10^{-38}\,\mathrm{erg}^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{3}$, and calculate the gravitational mass $M$, radius $R$, dimensionless tidal deformability $Λ$, and fundamental $f$-mode oscillation frequency for each stellar configuration. Imposing observational constraints on $M$, $R$, and $Λ$, we split our datasets into train and test sets, and we employ Random Forest (RF), K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Logistic Regression (LR), and Gaussian Naive Bayes (GNB) to classify the representative sectors $α=\{-5.01,0,+5.01\}\times10^{-38}\,\mathrm{erg}^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{3}$ using $(M, R,Λ,f)$. The RF classifier performs best, achieving an accuracy of approximately $99.85\%$ with precision, recall, and F1-scores exceeding $99.8\%$, while KNN also achieves accuracy above $99\%$. The nearly diagonal confusion matrices demonstrate that the observationally viable NS configurations associated with different EMSG sectors remain highly separable in the multidimensional observable space. Our results show that NS observables retain robust signatures of EMSG even after observational filtering, establishing ML-assisted NS observations as a promising complementary approach for probing modified gravity with current and future multi-messenger observations.

</details>

### [146] [Primordial turbulence from inflation: a new inflaton-driven turbulent regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10326)
*Oksana Iarygina,Axel Brandenburg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 首次将暴胀动力学与后续磁流体动力学演化直接衔接：自洽演化耦合的暴胀子-等离子体系统，跨越暴胀、重加热至辐射主导时期，发现暴胀子驱动的逆级联及自由衰减湍流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究尚未在暴胀动力学与后续磁流体动力学（magnetohydrodynamics, MHD）演化之间建立直接联系，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从真空涨落（vacuum fluctuations）出发，对耦合的暴胀子-等离子体（inflaton-plasma）系统进行自洽演化，历经暴胀（inflation）、重加热（reheating）直至辐射主导时期（radiation-dominated era）。

Result: 发现暴胀子驱动的逆级联（inverse cascade），随后进入自由衰减湍流；动能超过磁能并驱动发电机放大（dynamo amplification）。暴胀子驱动湍流导致的磁能衰减比螺旋湍流（helical turbulence）更陡。

Conclusion: 暴胀子驱动湍流效应应在原初磁场（primordial fields）的后续演化及其当代可观测量的研究中予以考虑。

Abstract: For the first time, we establish a direct connection between inflationary dynamics and the subsequent magnetohydrodynamic evolution. Starting from vacuum fluctuations, we self-consistently evolve the coupled inflaton-plasma system through inflation, reheating, and into the radiation-dominated era. We find an inflaton-driven inverse cascade followed by freely decaying turbulence, with kinetic energy dominating over magnetic energy and driving dynamo amplification. The inflaton-driven turbulence causes steeper magnetic energy decay than helical turbulence and should therefore be accounted for subsequent evolution of primordial fields and their present-day observables.

</details>


### [147] [Safe Phantom Divide Crossing from Unscreened Non-Minimal Coupling to Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10133)
*Joel Argudo-Panes,Alex González-Fuentes,Adrià Gómez-Valent*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探索非最小耦合标量场模型中此前少被研究的 α>0 参数区域，该区域可实现幽灵分界线穿越且与局域引力约束相容。作者结合最新宇宙学数据（含 DES-Dovekie 超新星样本）首次对 α>0 进行完整蒙特卡洛分析，发现其在约 2σ 置信水平上优于 ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）迹象，包括幽灵分界线（phantom divide）可能被穿越，促使研究者寻找可行的理论解释。非最小耦合引力模型 F(φ)=1+αφ² 对数据拟合优于 ΛCDM 及 w₀wₐCDM 参数化，但此前充分考察的 α<0 区域缺乏屏蔽机制，违反局域引力约束并加剧低红移物质增长，损害了其可行性。

Method: 研究此前基本未探索的 α>0 参数区域，该设置可实现幽灵分界线穿越并满足局域约束。回顾模型的背景与线性微扰动力学，推导解析结果以提供物理洞察，并采用最新宇宙学数据（包括 DES-Dovekie 超新星样本）更新约束。首次在 α>0 下进行完整蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）分析，研究 G 变化对超新星绝对光度的影响，并与 α<0 结果对比（两者均采用线性势）。

Result: α>0 设置在需一定精细调节（fine-tuning）的前提下满足大爆炸核合成（BBN）约束，且过去有效引力耦合偏离牛顿常数（Newton's constant）的幅度低于 0.3%。标准模型在 α<0 情形下以 2.50σ 置信水平被排除；而在 α>0 情形下排除显著性降至约 2σ，表明这些模型相对 ΛCDM 具有适度偏好。

Conclusion: α>0 的非最小耦合标量场方案为解释动力学暗能量与幽灵分界线穿越提供了与局域引力约束相容的可行途径，并在约 2σ 水平上相对 ΛCDM 显示出适度偏好，尽管仍需精细调节。

Abstract: Recent hints of dynamical dark energy, including a possible crossing of the phantom divide, have motivated the search for viable theoretical explanations. Scalar fields non-minimally coupled to gravity with a coupling of the form $F(\varphi)=1+α\varphi^2$ have emerged as promising candidates, providing an improved fit to the data compared to $Λ$CDM and even the $w_0w_a$CDM parametrization. However, in the absence of a screening mechanism, the regions of parameter space examined exhaustively so far (with $α<0$) violate stringent local constraints on the strength and time variation of gravity and enhance the matter growth rate at low redshifts, thereby compromising the viability of these solutions. In this work, we investigate a largely unexplored region of parameter space (with $α>0$) that realizes the crossing while remaining consistent with local constraints. This setup, which is subject to some fine-tuning, respects the BBN constraints and leads only to deviations of the effective gravitational coupling from Newton's constant below $0.3%$ in the past. We review the background and linear perturbation dynamics of the model, deriving analytical results that provide physical insight into its behavior, and update the constraints on the model using the latest cosmological data, including the DES-Dovekie supernova sample. We perform for the first time a full-fledged Monte Carlo analysis with $α>0$, studying the impact of the variation of $G$ on the supernovae absolute luminosity, and compare the results with those obtained for $α<0$, using in both cases a linear potential. In the context of the model with non-minimal coupling and $α<0$, the standard model is excluded at the $2.50σ$ CL, whereas for $α>0$, the exclusion significance decreases to $\sim 2σ$ CL, indicating a moderate preference for these models over $Λ$CDM. [abridged]

</details>


### [148] [Observational constraints on a damped harmonic oscillator model of dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10138)
*Saddam Hussain,Muhammad Ahmad,Ming Zhang,Ayodeji Ibitoye,Lang Cui*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用完整CMB似然结合DESI BAO与三类Ia型超新星数据，约束阻尼谐振子（damped harmonic oscillator, DHO）暗能量状态方程，其晚期动力学由频率$f$、阻尼率$b$与平衡值$w_{\rm m}$完全确定。模型仅在低红移呈振荡行为且被不同超新星数据集区分为欠阻尼或过阻尼解；在高红移近似$Λ$CDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于用阻尼谐振子形式的暗能量状态方程刻画晚期宇宙动力学，并检验不同超新星汇编对模型振荡特征与参数约束的影响。

Method: 状态方程服从以$e$-fold数为自变量的二阶阻尼振子方程；采用完整CMB似然，结合DESI BAO以及Pantheon+、DES-Dovekie、Union3三套超新星汇编进行参数约束，并处理扰动方程的数值刚性问题。

Result: 在低红移，DES-Dovekie与Union3给出欠阻尼解，Pantheon+给出过阻尼解；模型在$z<0.6$显著偏离$Λ$CDM而在更高红移接近之。$(f,b)$空间中存在状态方程快速变化的区域使扰动方程数值刚硬、无法以$c_s^2=1$积分，降低静止系声速可消除该障碍且可观测量在$10^{-3}$水平不变。给出的$H_0$分别为$67.53^{+1.22}_{-1.18}$、$69.08^{+1.23}_{-1.16}$、$70.67^{+1.89}_{-1.87}$ km/s/Mpc，$w_0$分别为$-0.521^{+0.891}_{-0.414}$、$-3.01^{+1.14}_{-1.21}$、$-3.16^{+1.11}_{-1.16}$。

Conclusion: 模型高红移行为与$Λ$CDM一致，偏离仅出现在低红移，且振荡性质与参数约束依赖于所用超新星汇编；降低声速的处理被视为数值手段而非物理修改。

Abstract: We constrain a damped harmonic oscillator (DHO) dark-energy equation of state using the full cosmic microwave background (CMB) likelihoods in combination with DESI BAO and three distinct Type Ia supernova compilations: Pantheon+, DES-Dovekie, and Union3. The equation of state obeys a second-order damped oscillator equation in number of $e$-folds, so that its frequency $f$, damping rate $b$, and equilibrium value $w_{\rm m}$ fully specify the late-time dynamics. The model exhibits oscillatory behavior only at low redshifts, around the equilibrium value $w=-1$, with distinct characteristics for the different supernova compilations: an underdamped solution for DES-Dovekie and Union3, and an overdamped solution for Pantheon+. At higher redshifts, the model closely mimics $Λ$CDM and deviates significantly only at $z<0.6$, with the magnitude of the deviation depending on the supernova compilation. We further identify a region of the $(f,b)$ parameter space, corresponding to rapid variation of the equation of state at low redshift, in which the perturbation equations become numerically stiff and cannot be integrated with a canonical dark-energy sound speed i.e., $c_s^2 =1$. We show that reducing the rest-frame sound speed removes this obstruction while leaving the observables unchanged at the $10^{-3}$ level, and therefore treat it as a numerical prescription rather than a physical modification of the model. The model yields $H_0 = 67.53^{+1.22}_{-1.18}$ km/s/Mpc for Pantheon+, $H_0 = 69.08^{+1.23}_{-1.16}$ km/s/Mpc for DES-Dovekie, and $H_0 = 70.67^{+1.89}_{-1.87}$ km/s/Mpc for Union3. The present-day equation-of-state parameter is constrained to $w_0 = -0.521^{+0.891}_{-0.414}$, $-3.01^{+1.14}_{-1.21}$, and $-3.16^{+1.11}_{-1.16}$ for Pantheon+, DES-Dovekie, and Union3, respectively.

</details>

### [149] [Fermionic quantum cellular automata in 2d are trivial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09317)
*Jeffrey Kwan,David M. Long,Jeongwan Haah*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在局部有限维代数上，任意二维费米子量子元胞自动机（fermionic quantum cellular automaton, QCA）均可分解为局部自同构与费米子平移的复合；其核心依据是一维格点上局部有限维费米子可逆子代数满足 Brauer 平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子 QCA 是局部费米子算子代数（\(\mathbb Z_2\)-分次超代数）的自同构，具有有界传播性质，刻画其一般结构（尤其是高维情形下的分类）是该领域的基础问题。摘要指出该工作旨在给出二维费米子 QCA 的结构定理。

Method: 研究以局部有限维代数上的费米子 QCA 为对象，通过考察一维格点上局部有限维费米子可逆子代数的性质进行论证，证明此类子代数具有 Brauer 平凡性（Brauer trivial），即稳定有界传播同构于某个张量积费米子代数。

Result: 证明了一维格点中每个局部有限维费米子可逆子代数均为 Brauer 平凡；由此推出，二维局部有限维代数上的每个费米子 QCA 都是局部自同构与一个费米子平移（fermionic shift）的复合。

Conclusion: 二维费米子 QCA 在局部有限维情形下具有完全的结构刻画，其本质为局部自同构与费米子平移的组合；该结论依托一维子代数的 Brauer 平凡性这一代数不变量结果。

Abstract: Fermionic quantum cellular automata (QCA) are automorphisms of local fermionic operator algebras ($\mathbb Z_2$-graded superalgebras) that have bounded spread: they map local operators to nearby operators. We prove that every 2-dimensional fermionic QCA on a locally finite-dimensional algebra is a composition of local automorphisms and a fermionic shift. This is implied by our result that every locally finite-dimensional fermionic invertible subalgebra in a one-dimensional lattice is Brauer trivial, \textit{i.e.}, it is stably bounded-spread isomorphic to a tensor product fermionic algebra.

</details>


### [150] [Bargmann-Fock Representation and Global Estimates for the Linearized Hard-Sphere Boltzmann Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09548)
*Ilya Karlin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在未经 Sonine 截断（Sonine truncation）的前提下，对三维硬球（hard spheres）线性化 Boltzmann 碰撞算子（linearized Boltzmann collision operator）进行重新组织，显式分离角向与径向结构，并借助 Hermite/Fock 与 Bargmann 表示将其约化为具 su(1,1) 结构的径向算符，从而给出全局谱几何的构造性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理依赖 Sonine 截断以获得 Burnett 矩阵与应力/热流修正，但截断会掩盖算子的完整角向与径向结构。本文旨在截断之前先厘清完整算子的谱结构，以理解其全局谱几何而非仅关注矩近似。

Method: 利用 Carleman 表示（Carleman representation）将碰撞积分化为正交平移的叠加；在 Hermite/Fock 与 Bargmann 形式下，为所有 Hermite 碰撞括号构造单一解析相干态核（coherent-state kernel）。通过旋转协方差分离固定角动量扇区，每扇区约化为带 su(1,1) 结构的单变量径向 Bargmann 空间，径向算符为 A_ℓ=κπG(J_ℓ)−C_ℓ，并借酉 intertwiner 与经典 Burnett/Sonine 坐标等同。

Result: 未截断算子的紧增益（compact gain）仅给出紧修正，损失项决定公共本质谱（essential spectrum）[ν_min,∞)。径向压缩的 su(1,1) 相干波包在每个固定角向扇区产生阈值 Weyl 序列（Weyl sequences）；当 ℓ→∞ 且压缩按 ℓ^{-1} 标度时，在整个本质谱带内部给出全空间 Weyl 序列，且 ⟨j⟩∼ℓ^2，揭示 (ℓ,j) 平面上的二次连续走廊。固定 ℓ 且 j→∞ 时，矩阵尾部趋于普适 Toeplitz 算子，其 θ=π 处的零点即同一阈值逃逸的局部特征。

Conclusion: Fock 重构提供了精确径向化（exact radialization）与对全局谱几何的构造性控制，同时亦重现 Burnett 矩阵及标准的应力与热流修正。

Abstract: We study the linearized Boltzmann collision operator for three-dimensional hard spheres and reorganize the complete operator so that its angular and radial structures are explicit before any Sonine truncation. From the Carleman representation, the collision integral becomes a superposition of orthogonal translations; in Hermite/Fock and Bargmann form these generate a single analytic coherent-state kernel for all Hermite collision brackets. Rotational covariance separates fixed angular-momentum sectors, each reducing exactly to a one-variable radial Bargmann space with an $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ structure. The radial operator is $\mathfrak A_\ell=κπG(\mathcal J_\ell)-\mathfrak C_\ell$, where $\mathcal J_\ell$ is an explicit Laguerre--Jacobi operator and $\mathfrak C_\ell$ is compact. A unitary intertwiner identifies this realization with the classical Burnett/Sonine coordinate, recovering the Burnett matrix and standard stress and heat-flux corrections.
  For the untruncated operator, the compact gain gives only compact corrections, while the loss sets the common essential spectrum $[ν_{\min},\infty)$. Radially squeezed $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ coherent packets yield threshold Weyl sequences in every fixed angular sector. With $\ell\to\infty$ and squeezing scaled as $\ell^{-1}$, they furnish full-space Weyl sequences throughout the essential band interior, with $\langle j\rangle\sim\ell^2$, revealing quadratic continuum corridors in the $(\ell,j)$ plane. At fixed $\ell$ and $j\to\infty$, the matrix tail approaches a universal Toeplitz operator whose zero at $θ=π$ is the local signature of the same threshold escape. Thus the Fock reformulation provides exact radialization and constructive control of the global spectral geometry.

</details>


### [151] [Hadamard--Tetrahedral Symmetric Spacetime and Clifford-Like Factorizations of Dirac and Maxwell Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09617)
*Farhad Aghili*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过将归一化四阶Hadamard变换与虚时间坐标结合，构造Minkowski时空的对称复坐标表示，使时间与空间四分量均分、度规化为欧几里得型复双线性形式，并将该框架从Dirac与Maxwell系统推广至弱场Einstein--Dirac--Maxwell耦合系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅从内容推断其目标在于为Dirac、Maxwell及弱场引力系统寻求统一的对称坐标表示与共同主算子结构。

Method: 构造复坐标q^a，将归一化四阶Hadamard变换与虚时间坐标结合；利用构成正四面体的Hadamard空间符号向量改写Dirac与Maxwell算子，并在Fourier空间分析其不变量与因子化结构。

Result: Minkowski度规化为欧几里得型复双线性度规；Dirac矩阵满足欧几里得型Clifford代数并含Pauli矩阵的四面体组合，Riemann--Silberstein Maxwell矩阵实现相应自旋1结构；Dirac与Maxwell系统在Fourier空间由共同不变量κ_aκ^a支配，完整Maxwell算子可精确矩形Clifford式因子化。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至弱场Einstein--Dirac--Maxwell系统：在调和规范下线性化引力场由同一标量波算子□_q支配，引力修正通过扰动四足标架、自旋联络与曲率进入Dirac和Maxwell方程，共同主算子结构暗示可用于耦合场求解器的共享谱核与标量波预处理。

Abstract: A symmetric representation of Minkowski spacetime is constructed by combining a normalized fourth-order Hadamard transformation with an imaginary temporal coordinate. The resulting complex coordinates $q^a$ distribute time and space equally among four components and transform the Minkowski metric into a Euclidean-form complex bilinear metric. The four Hadamard spatial sign vectors form a regular tetrahedron and appear simultaneously in the transformed Dirac and Maxwell operators. The Dirac matrices satisfy a Euclidean-form Clifford algebra and contain tetrahedral combinations of Pauli matrices, whereas the Riemann--Silberstein Maxwell matrices realize the corresponding spin-1 structure. A gauge-invariant QED Lagrangian is formulated in the same coordinates. In Fourier space, the Dirac and Maxwell systems are governed by the common invariant $κ_aκ^a$, while the complete Maxwell operator admits an exact rectangular Clifford-like factorization. The formulation is further extended to the weak-field Einstein--Dirac--Maxwell system. In harmonic gauge, the linearized gravitational field is governed by the same scalar wave operator $\Box_q$, while gravitational corrections to the Dirac and Maxwell equations enter through the perturbed tetrad, spin connection, and curvature. This common principal-operator structure suggests shared spectral kernels and scalar-wave preconditioning for coupled field solvers.

</details>


### [152] [Counterexamples to GNS Conjecture 1.8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09637)
*Xiuqing Duan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定（disprove）Gabrielov、Novikov 与 Shapiro（GNS）猜想 1.8 的两个部分：对库仑势（Coulomb potential），24 个电荷的通用有理构型至少具有 18 个 Morse 指标为一的非退化临界点，但仅有 14 个有效 Voronoi 单胞；并给出另一构造及乘积悬置（product suspension）形式的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验 GNS 猜想 1.8 中关于临界点数量与有效 Voronoi 胞（effective Voronoi cells）数量之间关系的论断，该猜想涉及库仑势与 Voronoi 划分的对应问题，作者通过构造反例来判定其两个部分是否成立。

Method: 方法为构造性反例：对库仑势考察 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中 24 个单位电荷的通用有理构型（generic rational configuration），并与有效 Voronoi 单胞计数进行对比；另采用适当的直线（proper-line）构造，以及对每个正子空间维数使用乘积悬置（product suspension）得到反例。

Result: 结果显示：对库仑势，该构型至少存在 18 个 Morse 指标为 1 的非退化临界点，但恰好只有 14 个有效 Voronoi 一维胞；另一 proper-line 构造至少有两个非退化极小点，却仅有一个相对有效（relatively effective）的零维胞交集；对每个正子空间维数，乘积悬置均给出 proper-subspace 反例。

Conclusion: 结论是 GNS 猜想 1.8 的两个部分均被否定，说明临界点数目与有效 Voronoi 胞数目之间并不满足该猜想所预期的关系。

Abstract: We disprove both parts of Conjecture 1.8 of Gabrielov, Novikov, and Shapiro (GNS). For the Coulomb potential, a generic rational configuration of 24 unit charges in $\mathbb{R}^3$ has at least 18 nondegenerate critical points of Morse index one but exactly 14 effective Voronoi one-cells. A separate proper-line construction has at least two nondegenerate minima but only one relatively effective zero-dimensional cell intersection. For each positive subspace dimension, product suspension gives a proper-subspace counterexample.

</details>


### [153] [Wasserstein Stability, Couplings Across Volumes, and the $1+1/d$ Moment Thresholds in the Edwards--Anderson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09680)
*Mauris Chueng,Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最近邻 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型（Edwards–Anderson Ising model）在自由与周期边界条件下退火压力（quenched pressure）的极限性质，证明其无限体积压力对耦合分布具 Lipschitz 连续性，并在周期体积下刻画几乎必然收敛与完全收敛的等价性及最优矩条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解空间非均匀无序（spatially inhomogeneous disorder）下热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）的定量稳定性，以及有限体积无序阵列在不同耦合方式（joint coupling）下压力收敛性的结构条件。

Method: 采用退火压力分析框架：证明无限体积压力关于耦合律（coupling law）在 1-Wasserstein 距离下为 βd-Lipschitz；在周期体积下，比较有限体积无序阵列的几乎必然收敛与单体积压力律的完全收敛（complete convergence）；并分析矩条件 E|J|^(1+1/d) < ∞ 及一维情形的首矩条件。

Result: 得到：压力对耦合律具 βd-Lipschitz 连续性并给出定量热力学极限；周期体积下几乎必然收敛（在任意联合耦合下）等价于压力律的完全收敛；E|J|^(1+1/d) < ∞ 保证该普适耦合结论；该指数在一致幂矩假设下最优——对每个 1 ≤ q < 1+1/d 存在有限 q 阶矩的中心对称分布，使规范嵌套体积几乎必然收敛，而独立重采样体积依概率收敛但不几乎必然收敛；一维中首矩条件亦为有限极限压力的必要条件。

Conclusion: 结论表明 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型退火压力的热力学极限具有对耦合律的定量稳定性，且其普遍收敛性的矩阈值 1+1/d 在一致幂矩假设下达到最优；一维情形下有限极限压力的存在性由首矩条件完全刻画。

Abstract: We study the quenched pressure of the nearest-neighbor Edwards--Anderson Ising model with free and periodic boundary conditions. First, we prove that the infinite-volume pressure is $βd$-Lipschitz in the coupling law for the $1$-Wasserstein distance, yielding quantitative thermodynamic limits for spatially inhomogeneous disorder. Second, for periodic volumes, we prove that almost-sure convergence under every joint coupling of the finite-volume disorder arrays is equivalent to complete convergence of the one-volume pressure laws. Third, we prove that $\mathbb{E}\vert{}J\vert{}^{1+1/d}<\infty$ guarantees this universal-coupling conclusion. We further show that this exponent is optimal among uniform power-moment assumptions: for every $1\leq q<1+1/d$, there is a centered symmetric law with finite $q$-th moment for which canonical nested volumes converge almost surely, whereas independently resampled volumes with the same fixed-volume marginals converge in probability but not almost surely. Finally, in dimension one, we prove that the first-moment condition is also necessary for a finite limiting pressure.

</details>


### [154] [Euler-Poincare Reduction: A Geometric Treatment of the Relativistic Spinning Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09747)
*Burak Gul,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在拉格朗日（Lagrangian）框架下，通过欧拉-庞加莱约化（Euler-Poincaré reduction）论证：庞加莱群（Poincaré group）上的自由相对论自旋粒子是重陀螺（heavy top）系统的精确洛伦兹类比（Lorentzian analog），时空位置充当被携带量（advected quantity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重陀螺是半直积群（semidirect product group）上欧拉-庞加莱约化的经典范例，而相对论自旋粒子的李群（Lie group）描述多建立在哈密顿（Hamiltonian/symplectic）力学框架内。作者意在提供一个显式拉格朗日约化视角，以揭示两个系统共有的对称结构，并解释外电磁场引入与坐标选择所带来的几何效应。

Method: 采用显式的拉格朗日欧拉-庞加莱约化（Lagrangian Euler-Poincaré reduction）方法，将庞加莱群上自由相对论自旋粒子的描述与半直积群上的重陀螺系统并置对照，并把时空位置识别为被携带量；同时考察外电磁场引入时约化的变化以及向左平凡化坐标（left-trivialized coordinates）的过渡。

Result: 庞加莱群上的自由相对论自旋粒子被确认为重陀螺系统的精确洛伦兹类比；引入外电磁场会导致部分约化（partial reduction）；转向左平凡化坐标构成一种几何选择机制（geometric selection mechanism），自然偏向某一类相互作用拉格朗日量。

Conclusion: 该几何统一不仅阐明了两个系统潜在的对称性，也解释了电磁场导致部分约化的原因，并表明在特定坐标选择下，自旋-场耦合（spin-field coupling）表现为粒子质量的动力学重定义（dynamical redefinition of mass）。

Abstract: The heavy top is a classic example of Euler-Poincare reduction on a semidirect product group, demonstrating how an advected quantity-the direction of gravity-couples to rotational motion. While the description of relativistic spinning particles via Lie group configurations is well established in Hamiltonian (symplectic) mechanics, we demonstrate that an explicit Lagrangian Euler-Poincare reduction provides a direct, transparent parallel to the heavy top. Specifically, we show that the free relativistic spinning particle on the Poincare group is the precise Lorentzian analog of this system, with spacetime position being the advected quantity. This geometric unification not only illuminates the underlying symmetry of both systems but also clarifies why the introduction of external electromagnetic fields leads to a partial reduction. Finally, we establish that the transition to left-trivialized coordinates acts as a geometric selection mechanism, naturally favoring a class of interaction Lagrangians where spin-field coupling manifests as a dynamical redefinition of the particle's mass.

</details>


### [155] [Universal correlations in the Abelian sandpile model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10352)
*Qiyu Liu,Jan-Niklas Herre,Prajit Baruah,Sebastian Dreizler,Christoph Karrasch,Wioletta Ruszel,Dirk Schuricht*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作用数值方法研究二维 Abelian 沙堆模型（Abelian sandpile model）的体相关函数，借助 Wilson 算法生成均匀生成树并经 Majumdar–Dhar 燃烧双射映射为独立递归构型，从而消除样本自相关并加速收敛。方形与蜂窝格子的结果与解析预言相符；对 Kagome 格子则给出首个系统性数值研究，并用推导出的格格林函数闭式表达式交叉验证体高度-1 概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标有二：一是将数值结果与对数共形场论（logarithmic conformal field theory）的预言进行比对，二是将分析推广到解析方法难以处理的格子。Kagome 等复杂格子缺乏系统的体相关函数数值研究，构成该工作的主要空白。

Method: 采用 Wilson 算法并行、高效地生成大尺度均匀生成树（uniform spanning tree），再通过 Majumdar–Dhar 燃烧双射（burning bijection）映射为相互独立的递归构型（recurrent configurations），从而消除样本自相关（autocorrelation）并获得快速收敛。此外，通过格格林函数（lattice Green function）推导出体高度-1 概率的闭式解析表达式。

Result: 在方形（单子格）与蜂窝（双子格）格子上，数值结果与解析预言吻合良好。对 Kagome 格子，首次给出体相关函数的系统性数值研究，并以所推导的闭式解析表达式对体高度-1 概率进行交叉验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于均匀生成树的数值方案可有效处理体相关函数计算，兼具与解析预言的一致性及向解析困难格子的可扩展性；Kagome 格子的结果与自推导的闭式表达式相互印证。

Abstract: We numerically study the bulk correlation functions in the two-dimensional Abelian sandpile model, aiming both to compare with the predictions of logarithmic conformal field theory and to extend the analysis to lattices where analytical methods are difficult to apply. Wilson's algorithm efficiently generates large-scale uniform spanning trees in parallel, which can be mapped to independent recurrent configurations via the Majumdar--Dhar burning bijection, eliminating sample autocorrelations and yielding fast convergence. On the square (single-sublattice) and honeycomb (two-sublattice) lattices our results agree well with the analytical predictions. For the kagome lattice we provide the first systematic numerical study of the bulk correlation functions, and cross-check the bulk height-1 probability against a closed-form analytical expression that we also derive here via the lattice Green function.

</details>

### [156] [Curvature-Induced Geometric Universality in Non-Hermitian Anderson Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09562)
*Chen Wang,Run-Qiu Yang,X. R. Wang,Hechen Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出在双曲型晶格上存在由几何控制的非厄米安德森转变（non-Hermitian Anderson transition）普适类：负曲率与指数体积增长改变了临界行为，使不同{p,q}镶嵌呈现统一临界指数ν≈1的标度塌缩，并表现为类平均场标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，欧几里得空间中安德森转变的普适类主要由对称性与空间维度决定。作者希望检验在具有负曲率和指数体积增长的非欧几里得几何中，空间曲率本身是否可作为超越维度—对称性分类的额外组织原则。

Method: 在双曲型晶格（hyperbolic-like lattices）上研究非厄米安德森转变，对多个不同{p,q}镶嵌计算参与比（participation ratios）并进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析；同时辅以唯象的粗粒化朗道—金兹堡（Landau-Ginzburg）分析。

Result: 多个不同{p,q}镶嵌的有限尺寸标度显示单参数标度塌缩（one-parameter scaling collapse），在数值精度内具有共同临界指数ν≈1。粗粒化朗道—金兹堡分析表明，指数形式的关联体积增长抑制了临界涨落（critical fluctuations），为观测到的类平均场标度（mean-field-like scaling）提供了合理解释。

Conclusion: 结果表明，空间曲率可作为安德森转变普适性的额外组织原则，突破传统的基于维度与对称性的分类框架，提示几何控制的普适类（geometry-controlled universality class）的存在。

Abstract: In Euclidean space, universality classes of Anderson transitions are primarily determined by symmetry and spatial dimensionality. Here, we present evidence for a geometry-controlled universality class of non-Hermitian Anderson transitions on hyperbolic-like lattices. In this setting, critical behavior is influenced by the large-scale hyperbolic geometry, characterized by negative curvature, exponential volume growth, and a non-Euclidean notion of spatial scaling. Finite-size scaling of participation ratios across several distinct \( \{p,q\} \) tilings reveals one-parameter scaling collapses with a common critical exponent \( ν\simeq1 \) within numerical accuracy. A complementary phenomenological coarse-grained Landau-Ginzburg analysis shows how exponential correlation-volume growth suppresses critical fluctuations, offering a rationale for the observed mean-field-like scaling. Our results suggest that spatial curvature can act as an additional organizing principle for Anderson-transition universality beyond the conventional dimensionality- and symmetry-based classification.

</details>

### [157] [Origin of Flat Bands and Role of Electron Correlation in Lutetium Hydrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09446)
*Anmol Lamichhane,Adam Denchfield,Hyeondeok Shin,Panchapakesan Ganesh,Russell J. Hemley,Hyowon Park*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论、动力学平均场理论与约束随机相近似，揭示氢化镥（lutetium hydrides）中平带（flat band）的微观起源与关联性质，指出其本质为弱关联金属，并确定氢轨道占据（hydrogen orbital filling）是主导关联与低能平带物理的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢化镥（LuH$_x$，1.75≤x≤3）系列相在压力下可超导，但因氢缺陷（hydrogen defect）易形成而电子性质难以表征；近期角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）观测到令人困惑的平带区域，使该体系成为超导与平带物理可能交汇的候选材料，其微观机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）、动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）与约束随机相近似（constrained random-phase approximation, cRPA）相结合的第一性原理方法，系统计算各组分下的屏蔽在位库仑相互作用及关联强度。

Result: 所有组分中，由于氢轨道紧凑，H-s 态的屏蔽在位库仑相互作用大于 Lu-d 态；但体系仍为弱关联金属（weakly correlated metal），因近乎填满的 H-s 壳层电荷涨落很小，其大相互作用仅表现为静态能级移动而非关联来源。x=2 时氢主要占据四面体位点，而反位缺陷（anti-site defect，氢占据略不利的八面体位点）同时产生 ARPES 平带特征与低能光吸收峰，印证实验样品中常见的缺陷性质。关联强度主要由这些位点的氢轨道占据决定，而非相互作用大小本身。

Conclusion: 氢轨道占据被确定为决定氢化镥中关联效应与低能平带物理的基本组织原则，为理解该类材料的电子行为及超导-平带交叉提供微观框架。

Abstract: Lutetium hydrides (LuH$_x$, $1.75 \leq x \leq 3$) form a diverse series of phases, several of which superconduct under pressure. Characterizing their electronic properties has remained challenging owing to a high propensity for hydrogen defect formation, and recent angle-resolved photoemission (ARPES) measurements reveal puzzling flat-band regions that position these materials as candidates where superconductivity and flat-band physics may intersect. Here, by combining density functional theory, dynamical mean-field theory, and the constrained random-phase approximation, we uncover the microscopic origin and correlation nature of these flat bands. Across all compositions, the screened on-site Coulomb interaction is larger for H-s states than for Lu-$d$ states due to compact hydrogen orbitals. Nevertheless, these systems remain weakly correlated metals: the nearly filled H-$s$ shell admits little charge fluctuation, so its large interaction acts as a static level shift rather than a source of correlation. Although hydrogen primarily occupies tetrahedral sites at $x=2$, we discover that anti-site defects--where hydrogens occupy slightly unfavorable octahedral sites--generate both the ARPES flat-band features and the low-energy optical absorption peak, attesting to the usual defective nature of such materials in experimental samples. We further find that correlation strength is governed primarily by hydrogen orbital filling at these sites rather than the interaction magnitude itself. Consequently, we identify hydrogen orbital filling as the fundamental organizing principle dictating correlation and low-energy flat-band physics in lutetium hydrides.

</details>


### [158] [Eight-unit-cell electronic modulations in cuprates originating from local molecular orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09615)
*Zhiheng Yao,Sixuan Chen,Jianfa Zhao,Shusen Ye,Weixiang Qu,Ning Xia,Yuling Dai,Luchuan Shi,Hongrui Zhang,Zhenqi Hao,Changqing Jin,Shuo Yang,Yayu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用谱学成像扫描隧道显微镜（spectroscopic imaging scanning tunneling microscopy, SI-STM）在空穴掺杂的 Ca2CuO2Cl2 铜氧化物（cuprate）中观测到约 8a0 周期的电子态空间调制，并提出其源于掺杂空穴形成的分子轨道（molecular orbital）而非对密度波（pair density wave, PDW）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有八倍晶胞（8a0）周期的对密度波（PDW）态被广泛视为铜氧化物的主要序参量，但其存在性与起源仍存在激烈争论，因此需要直接的实空间电子态证据加以检验。

Method: 采用谱学成像扫描隧道显微镜（spectroscopic imaging scanning tunneling microscopy, SI-STM），在空穴掺杂 Ca2CuO2Cl2 铜氧化物（cuprate）的超导区与绝缘区中测量电子态的空间调制。

Result: 在超导与绝缘两种区间内均观测到约 8a0 周期的电子态空间调制；该 8a0 空间图案由掺杂空穴形成的分子轨道（molecular orbital）所产生，这些轨道以 4a0*4a0 网格（plaquette）为基本单元组织。

Conclusion: 4a0 分子轨道被认定为铜氧化物中基本的电子构筑单元（electronic building block），而 8a0 的 PDW 则是该结构在足够高掺杂下出现的空间次谐波（spatial subharmonic）。

Abstract: The pair density wave (PDW) state with eight-unit-cell (8a0) periodicity has been widely regarded as the primary order in cuprates, yet its existence and origin remain subjects of intense debate. Using spectroscopic imaging scanning tunneling microscopy, we observe spatial modulations of the electronic states with approximately 8a0 periodicity in both the superconducting and insulating regimes of hole-doped Ca2CuO2Cl2 cuprate. We find that the 8a0 spatial patterns are generated by the formation of molecular orbitals by doped holes, which organize into 4a0*4a0 plaquettes as the basic unit. Our results identify the 4a0 molecular orbital as the fundamental electronic building block in cuprates, while the 8a0 PDW represents a spatial subharmonic that emerges at sufficiently high doping.

</details>


### [159] [Record-Breaking Elemental Superconductivity in Tetralayer Kagome Borophene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09730)
*Yingnan Liu,Yan Liu,Renyu Duan,Menghui Wang,Meiling Xu,Hanyu Liu,Shoutao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过第一性原理计算与各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，预测由ABAB共价堆垛稳定的四层笼目硼烯（TKB）为临界温度达102 K的液氮温区元素超导体，创已报道元素超导体临界温度最高值，并提出面外s-pz键介导的配对机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维元素晶体中强共价键通常带来高声子频率但电子-声子耦合不足，导致液氮温区超导罕见，故需探索新的高临界温度机制与材料。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与完全各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，研究ABAB共价堆垛稳定的四层笼目硼烯（tetralayer kagome borophene, TKB）的超导性质。

Result: 预测TKB临界温度为102 K，为已报道元素超导体中的最高值；其配对由费米能级处层间s-pz成键态与硼原子低频面外振动强耦合实现，区别于以面内sigma键态和高频面内B-B伸缩模主导的已知硼基高临界温度超导体。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了多层硼烯中独特的“面外配对通道”，并表明共价堆垛工程（covalent stacking engineering）可作为二维材料实现高临界温度超导的潜在路径。

Abstract: Superconductivity above the liquid-nitrogen temperature remains rare in two-dimensional elemental crystals, where strong covalent bonding often yields high phonon frequencies but insufficient electron-phonon coupling. Here, using first-principles calculations and fully anisotropic Migdal-Eliashberg theory, we predict tetralayer kagome borophene (TKB) stabilized by ABAB covalent stacking, as a liquid-nitrogen-temperature elemental superconductor. With a predicted critical temperature of 102 K, TKB sets a record-high value among previously reported elemental superconductors. Unlike known high-Tc boron-based superconductors dominated by in-plane sigma-bonding states and high-frequency in-plane B-B stretching modes, TKB realizes an out-of-plane s-pz-bonding-mediated pairing mechanism, in which interlayer s-pz bonding states at the Fermi level are strongly coupled to low-frequency out-of-plane vibrations of boron atoms. These results reveal a distinct out-of-plane pairing channel in multilayer borophene and establish covalent stacking engineering as a potential route for high-Tc superconductivity in two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [160] [Nonlinear Edelstein effect in Rashba superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10212)
*Hikaru Ueki,Youichi Yanase*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含能带内（intraband）与能带间（interband）贡献的超导体 Edelstein 效应准经典理论，并用于研究 s 波 Rashba 超导体中的超电流诱导表面自旋磁化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准经典理论仅给出领头阶的能带内贡献，无法描述源自量子几何（quantum geometry）的能带间贡献，因此需要扩展理论框架以完整刻画超导体中的 Edelstein 效应。

Method: 在反对称自旋轨道耦合（antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling）下推导扩展的 Eilenberger 方程以描述能带间贡献，并以多带 Eilenberger 方程计算能带内贡献；将其应用于 s 波 Rashba 超导体。

Result: 能带内贡献源于相反动量与自旋极化准粒子的超电流诱导不对称性，随自旋轨道耦合强度线性增长；能带间贡献源于 Rashba 自旋轨道势动量导数产生的反常速度项（可用 Berry 联络表示），呈非单调依赖，在 Rashba 自旋劈裂与超导能隙相当时达到最大。

Conclusion: 在洁净 s 波 Rashba 模型中，磁化对超电流的非线性依赖仅来自能带间贡献；因此线性 Edelstein 效应由能带内贡献主导，而非线性 Edelstein 效应可作为探测量子几何相关能带间贡献的有效手段。

Abstract: We formulate a quasiclassical theory of the Edelstein effect in superconductors that incorporates both intraband and interband contributions. To describe the interband contribution, which is absent from the conventional leading-order quasiclassical formulation, we derive augmented Eilenberger equations in the presence of antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling. The intraband contribution is evaluated using multiband Eilenberger equations. We apply these formulations to supercurrent-induced surface spin magnetization in $s$-wave Rashba superconductors and investigate its dependence on temperature, distance from the surface, spin-orbit coupling strength, and supercurrent. The intraband contribution originates from a supercurrent-induced asymmetry of quasiparticles with opposite momenta and spin polarizations, whereas the interband contribution arises from the anomalous-velocity term generated by the momentum derivative of the Rashba spin-orbit potential. In the helicity basis, this anomalous-velocity term is expressed in terms of the Berry connection associated with the momentum dependence of the Rashba eigenstates. The intraband contribution increases linearly with the spin-orbit coupling strength, whereas the interband contribution exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence and is maximized when the Rashba spin splitting is comparable to the superconducting gap. Moreover, within the clean $s$-wave Rashba model considered here, we find that the nonlinear dependence of magnetization on the supercurrent arises solely from the interband contribution. Thus, although the intraband contribution dominates the linear Edelstein effect, the nonlinear Edelstein effect can serve as a useful probe of the interband contribution originating from quantum geometry.

</details>


### [161] [A First-Principles Multiscale Framework for Topological Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10381)
*Christopher L. Jacobs,Baishali Mandal,Taehwan Jung,Purna P. Paudel,Jason Kawasaki,Tudor D. Stanescu,Subhasish Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作建立了一个整合第一性原理电子结构、超导与拓扑的统一计算框架，用于研究铁基材料中的拓扑超导（topological superconductivity, TSC），并预测与验证了FeSe/GaAs异质结等候选马约拉纳材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实材料中拓扑超导的微观理解需要兼顾材料信息的计算方法，即将第一性原理电子结构、超导性与拓扑统一纳入同一计算框架，而非依赖简化模型。该研究旨在为拓扑超导的材料设计与筛选提供定量路径。

Method: 方法上结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）、基于Wannier函数的低能哈密顿量（Wannier-based low-energy Hamiltonians）、Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论，以及基于Matsubara格林函数（Matsubara Green's-function）并直接在真实多轨道超导哈密顿量上执行的陈数（Chern number）计算。该框架被应用于块体状与单层FeTeSe以及FeSe/GaAs异质结，以统一研究本征与近邻诱导的拓扑超导。

Result: 研究识别出促进稳健拓扑超导的关键电子结构要素，包括费米能级附近的Rashba活性态、强诱导超导配对，以及自旋轨道活性部门与超导部门之间显著的轨道杂化。据此在铁基材料中预测了多个拓扑超导相，并表明FeSe/GaAs异质结尤为有前景，可在实验可达的化学势与低塞曼场（Zeeman field）下呈现拓扑相变。同时完成了FeSe/GaAs异质结的生长与结构表征，确立了该材料平台的实验可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为直接从真实电子结构出发工程化与筛选马约拉纳（Majorana）候选材料及异质结提供了定量途径，并为拓扑超导的未来“材料按需设计（materials-by-design）”方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: A microscopic understanding of topological superconductivity (TSC) in real materials requires a materials-informed approach that integrates first-principles electronic structure, superconductivity, and topology within a unified computational framework. Here, we develop such an approach by combining density functional theory, Wannier-based low-energy Hamiltonians, Bogoliubov-de Gennes theory, and Matsubara Green's-function-based Chern number calculations performed directly on realistic multiorbital superconducting Hamiltonians. We apply this framework to bulk-like and monolayer FeTeSe and to FeSe/GaAs heterostructures, enabling a unified investigation of both intrinsic and proximity-induced topological superconductivity. We identify key electronic-structure ingredients that promote robust TSC, including Rashba-active states near the Fermi level, strong induced superconducting pairing, and substantial orbital hybridization between spin-orbit-active and superconducting sectors. Guided by these design principles, we predict multiple topological superconducting phases in Fe-based materials and demonstrate that FeSe/GaAs heterostructures are particularly promising, exhibiting topological transitions at experimentally accessible chemical potentials and low Zeeman fields. Complementing the theoretical predictions, we demonstrate the growth and structural characterization of FeSe/GaAs heterostructures, establishing the experimental feasibility of the proposed materials platform. Our results provide a quantitative route for engineering and screening candidate Majorana materials and heterostructures directly from realistic electronic structures and establish a foundation for future materials-by-design approaches to topological superconductivity.

</details>

### [162] [Spacetime closedness theorem for homogeneous Lorentzian foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09181)
*Robert Monjo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在洛伦兹（Lorentzian）层面直接建立了一个时空闭合性定理（spacetime closedness theorem），通过从大爆炸与时间控制推出有限切片体积、再结合齐性与完备性升级为紧致性，并在任意时空维度下以 Lean 4 形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间形式分类定理（space-form classification theorem）只能刻画齐性各向同性的空间几何，本身并不给出关于环境时空的洛伦兹陈述。受空间齐性宇宙模型的几何解释启发，作者希望在洛伦兹层面直接建立时空闭合性结论。

Method: 核心技术步骤是从分层（foliation）上的纯洛伦兹控制假设推导有限切片体积。论证分两步：解析步骤由大爆炸（Big Bang）与时间控制推出有限切片体积；几何步骤借助齐性（homogeneity）与完备性（completeness）将有限体积升级为紧致性。强版本针对极大正则的全局双曲（globally hyperbolic）(n+1) 时空，弱版本以累积膨胀率（accumulated expansion rate）的时间可积性替代极大正则性。

Result: 定理在任意时空维度下成立，并同时给出强、弱两个抽象命题的 Lean 4 形式化。强版本覆盖具有限时间大爆炸与齐性完备类空切片的极大正则全局双曲 (n+1) 时空；弱版本在较弱的正则性假设下仍得到相应结论。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，时空闭合性可在洛伦兹层面通过“有限体积—紧致性”的两步论证获得，且不依赖经典空间形式分类定理本身，并具备机器可验证的形式化支撑。

Abstract: Motivated by the geometric interpretation of spatially homogeneous cosmological models, we formulate a spacetime closedness theorem directly at the Lorentzian level. A classical space-form classification theorem organizes homogeneous and isotropic spatial geometries, but by itself it does not yield a Lorentzian statement about the ambient spacetime. The main technical step is to derive finite slice-volume from genuinely Lorentzian control hypotheses on the foliation. This is achieved in a strong version, for maximally regular globally hyperbolic $(n+1)$--spacetimes with a finite-time Big Bang and homogeneous complete spacelike slices, and in a weaker version in which maximal regularity is replaced by time-integrability of the accumulated expansion rate. In both cases, the argument separates an analytic step, deriving finite slice-volume from the Big Bang and temporal control, from a geometric step, upgrading finite volume to compactness by homogeneity and completeness. The theorem is stated in arbitrary spacetime dimension and is accompanied by a Lean~4 formalization of the strong and weak abstract statements.

</details>


### [163] [Conformal boundary rigidity for simple Finsler metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09527)
*Kelvin Lam,Hanming Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：单简单芬斯勒流形（simple Finsler manifold）具有共形稳定性（conformally stable），并建立了关于共形因子类（class of conformal factors）的稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将芬斯勒几何（Finsler geometry）中简单流形的共形稳定性问题加以刻画，为此需要处理共形因子所诱导的边界距离函数（boundary distance functions）的奇异性，以便引入合适的 Sobolev 范数进行分析。

Method: 方法上，作者对给定的简单芬斯勒流形与一类共形因子，刻画其诱导边界距离函数的奇异性，从而定义合适的 H^2 范数；并借鉴 Mukhometov 的经典分部积分技术（integration by parts technique）并将其推广至芬斯勒情形。

Result: 作者在上述 Sobolev 范数与共形因子类上的 L^2 范数（L^2 norm）下，得到了一个稳定性估计（stability estimate）。

Conclusion: 结论是简单芬斯勒流形是共形稳定的，且所采用的 Mukhometov 型分部积分方法可有效推广到芬斯勒框架（Finsler setting）中。

Abstract: In this paper we prove that simple Finsler manifolds are conformally stable. Given a simple Finsler manifold and a class of conformal factors, we characterize the singularity of their induced boundary distance functions which allows us to define an appropiate $H^2$ norm. We then obtain a stability estimate with respect to this Sobolev norm and the $L^2$ norm on the class of the conformal factors. To prove the main theorems, we adapt the classical integration by parts technique employed by Mukhometov \cite{Muhometov} to the Finsler setting.

</details>


### [164] [On the Asymptotics of the Volume of Hitchin Moduli Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09795)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究亏格 $g\geq2$ 紧黎曼面上固定奇次行列式的秩二无迹 Higgs 丛模空间，在特定归一化下给出了 Hitchin 度量的测地球体积精确渐近公式，并确定了哈密顿子水平集体积与指数加权体积的首项渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Hitchin 模空间（Hitchin moduli space）的几何测度性质（如测地球体积的渐近增长）缺乏显式刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明结合超凯勒体积形式（hyperkähler volume form）的齐次性与正则 Hitchin 轨迹（regular Hitchin locus）上的度量渐近；系数通过辛约化（symplectic reduction）与 Prym 极化（Prym polarization）计算，并用等变局部化（equivariant localization）独立验证。

Result: 得到对任意 $p\in\M$ 均成立的体积公式 $\Vol_{g_{L^2}}B_{L^2}(p,R)=\frac{2^{4g-3}\pi^{9g-9}}{(3g-3)!}R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6})$，并确定哈密顿子水平集体积与指数加权体积的首项渐近。

Conclusion: Hitchin 模空间的 $L^2$ 测地球体积以 $R^{6g-6}$ 为主阶增长，其系数可由两条独立途径（辛约化/Prym 极化与等变局部化）相互印证。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a compact Riemann surface of genus $g\geq2$, and let $\M$ be the moduli space of rank-two trace-free Higgs bundles with fixed determinant of odd degree. For the normalization of the Hitchin metric used in this paper, we prove that the volume of geodesic ball in the Hitchin moduli space is given by $$ \Vol_{g_{L^2}}B_{L^2}(p,R) =\frac{2^{4g-3}π^{9g-9}}{(3g-3)!}\,R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6}) $$ for every $p\in\M$. We also determine the leading asymptotics of Hamiltonian sublevel volumes and exponentially weighted volumes. The proof combines homogeneity of the hyperkähler volume form with metric asymptotics on the regular Hitchin locus. Symplectic reduction and the Prym polarization evaluate the coefficient, which is independently recovered by equivariant localization.

</details>


### [165] [Area-constrained Willmore surfaces in Lorentzian manifolds: curvature inequalities and the Hawking quasi-local energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09416)
*Alejandro Peñuela Diaz*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在四维洛伦兹流形中建立面积约束的 Willmore 理论，针对闭类空曲面引入沿指定法方向的“方向临界性（directional criticality）”，并在连通相容方向与爱因斯坦张量符号条件下证明尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫|H|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)，进而导出 Hawking 准局域能量的正性与刚性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洛伦兹流形中，面积约束 Willmore 泛函的 Euler–Lagrange 方程取值于法丛（normal bundle），无法直接给出整体临界性的标量刻画，因此需要引入方向临界性以处理这一结构性问题，并将结果与 Hawking 准局域能量（Hawking quasi-local energy）的正性、刚性及单调性问题相联系。

Method: 引入沿给定法方向的面积约束 Willmore 临界性概念，借助法联络（normal connection）刻画一类“连通相容法方向（connection-compatible directions）”；在该方向临界性、爱因斯坦张量符号条件（Einstein-tensor sign condition）下证明尖锐不等式，并给出超曲面表述以将既有 Hawking 曲面问题还原为沿空间法方向的临界性。

Result: 在连通相容类空方向下建立尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫_Σ|H|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)；在主能量条件（dominant energy condition）与 λ ≥ 0 下推出 ∫_Σ|H|²dμ ≤ 16π；等号情形具刚性：Σ 为圆球型并满足闵可夫斯基因果菱形刚性（Minkowski causal-diamond rigidity）。对类光临界方向亦证明类似尖锐不等式，并在球对称、Kerr 与 FLRW 时空中给出算例，区分方向临界性与整体临界性，说明刚性理论中符号假设的必要性。

Conclusion: 该框架为 Hawking 准局域能量提供了正性、刚性、稳定性（stationarity）与无穷小单调性（infinitesimal monotonicity）的陈述；超曲面表述将先前研究的 Hawking 曲面问题纳入其中，而算例表明方向临界性严格弱于整体临界性，且符号条件不可省略。

Abstract: We develop an area-constrained Willmore theory for closed spacelike surfaces in four-dimensional Lorentzian manifolds. Since the Euler--Lagrange equation is normal-bundle-valued, we introduce directional criticality along a prescribed normal direction and identify a class of connection-compatible directions determined by the normal connection. Our main result is the sharp inequality $2λ|Σ|+\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq 8πχ(Σ)$ for criticality in a connection-compatible spacelike direction under an Einstein-tensor sign condition. Under the dominant energy condition and $λ\geq0$, this yields $\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq16π$. Equality is rigid: $Σ$ is round and satisfies Minkowski causal-diamond rigidity. We also prove an analogous sharp inequality for null critical directions.
  These results give positivity, rigidity, stationarity, and infinitesimal monotonicity statements for the Hawking quasi-local energy. A hypersurface formulation recovers the previously studied Hawking-surface problem as criticality in the spatial normal direction. Explicit examples in spherical symmetry, Kerr, and FLRW spacetimes distinguish directional from full criticality and show the necessity of the sign assumptions in the rigidity theory.

</details>

### [166] [Temperature-induced effective topology in many-body ultracold atomic quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09463)
*Nitya Cuzzuol,Michele Miotto,Arianna Montorsi,Giacomo Valtolina,Luca Barbiero*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出将超冷原子体系中对温度的可控调节视为一种资源，用以构造零温下不存在的有效拓扑物态（effective topological phases）。通过有限温度矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）计算，在相互作用玻色 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 链、自旋-1 XXZ 链以及基态无拓扑序的费米偶极梯子模型中，识别出由不同激发能隙尺度控制的中间温度窗口，从而实现或定性改变拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷原子量子体系为研究相互作用对称保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phases）提供了强大平台，而其中对温度的精细控制尚未被作为调控拓扑的独立资源加以利用。作者旨在证明有限温度本身可被用于工程化零温情形下没有对应物的有效拓扑区间。

Method: 采用有限温度矩阵乘积态（finite-temperature matrix-product-state）计算方法，考察三类实验可及哈密顿量：在零温具有拓扑相的相互作用玻色 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 链与自旋-1 XXZ 链，以及基态不表现拓扑序的费米偶极梯子（fermionic dipolar ladder）模型。通过追踪多种互补的拓扑标记（topological markers）来判定各温度区间中的拓扑性质。

Result: 识别出一种根源于零温相图结构的机制：在有限尺寸体系中，不同大小的激发能隙界定出一个中间温度窗口，其中与较小能隙相关的关联被抑制，而由较大能隙保护的关联得以保留。由此出现具有拓扑特征的有效有限温度区间，其拓扑性质在零温下或完全缺失、或定性不同。该现象在三类模型中均可被观测。

Conclusion: 研究确立了一条探索超冷原子量子体系中有效拓扑相（effective topological phases）的新途径，表明温度可作为工程化拓扑物态的资源，而不仅是被抑制拓扑序的退相干因素。

Abstract: Ultracold atomic quantum systems offer an unprecedentedly powerful platform for exploring interacting symmetry-protected topological phases. Here, we show that their characteristic control over temperature provides a resource for engineering effective topological regimes with no zero-temperature counterpart. Employing finite-temperature matrix-product-state calculations, we investigate experimentally accessible Hamiltonians - the interacting bosonic Su-Schrieffer-Heeger and spin-1 XXZ chains - that host a topological phase at zero temperature, as well as a fermionic dipolar ladder model whose ground state exhibits no topological order. By tracking complementary topological markers, we identify an intriguing mechanism rooted in the structure of the zero-temperature phase diagrams: in finite-size systems, excitation gaps with different magnitudes define an intermediate-temperature window where the correlations associated with the lower gap are suppressed, while those protected by the larger gap persist. This enables the emergence of effective finite-temperature regimes exhibiting topological features that are either absent or qualitatively different from those at zero temperature. Our results establish a novel route toward exploring effective topological phases in ultracold atomic quantum systems.

</details>


### [167] [Prethermal ripplons in quenched binary Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09464)
*Yanda Geng,Stephen Eckel,Gretchen K. Campbell,Ian B. Spielman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在一维界面（1D interface）上的二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensates, BECs）体系中，淬火后界面毛细波（capillary waves, ripplons）快速弛豫进入长寿命的预热态（prethermal state），其显著特征是能量持续非均分。高动量模式与体声子模式迅速热化，而低动量模式保持较高温度，作者将其归因于运动学隔离（kinematic isolation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预热态（prethermal states）广泛存在于经历快速退相位（fast dephasing）或重构后缓慢弛豫至热平衡的物理系统中，但其能量非均分机制尚待实验检验。该研究以互不相溶二维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BECs）间的一维界面为平台，类比经典 Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou 问题，探究预热态的成因。

Method: 对二维 BECs 间的一维界面进行淬火（quench）实验，直接测量界面高度轮廓随时间的演化，并识别各毛细波（ripplon）模式的贡献，从而分析其能量分布与温度特征。

Result: 淬火后界面毛细波迅速弛豫进入长寿命预热态，表现为持久的能量非均分。各 ripplon 模式的振幅虽呈热分布，但整体并不处于热平衡：高动量模式与 2D BEC 的体声子模式快速平衡，低动量模式则维持较高温度，构成全系统的长寿命预热构型。

Conclusion: 该预热态源于运动学隔离（kinematic isolation）：在超冷温度下，系统缺乏同时守恒能量与动量的弛豫过程，因而低动量模式无法有效与体声子模式热化。

Abstract: Prethermal states--long-lived quasi-equilibrium configurations--occur in a wide range of physical systems that exhibit fast dephasing or reconfiguration followed by slow relaxation towards thermal equilibrium. We experimentally studied the 1D interface between immiscible 2D Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs); following a quench, the interfacial capillary waves (ripplons) quickly relaxed into a long-lived prethermal state characterized by a persistent non-equipartition of energy, evoking the classic Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou problem. We directly measured the interface's height profile as it evolved in time, identified the contribution of individual ripplon modes, and find that, though their individual amplitudes are thermally distributed, they are not in thermal equilibrium--high-momentum modes rapidly equilibrated with the bulk phonon modes of the 2D BEC, while lower momentum modes remained at elevated temperatures, forming a long-lived prethermal configuration for the whole system. We attribute this to kinematic isolation: the absence of energy- and momentum-conserving relaxation processes in our system at ultracold temperature.

</details>


### [168] [Fractional Chern Insulators in Twisted Bilayer Optical Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09733)
*Yan-Bin Yang,Jiong-Hao Wang,Shicheng Ma,Yong Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在扭转双层光晶格中，理论研究表明原子相互作用可将拓扑平庸的近平坦莫尔能带转变为平坦陈能带，并在分数量填充下产生分数量子陈绝缘体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期在扭转双层光晶格中实现原子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）实验的启发，本文旨在探索该体系中冷原子的拓扑态，以寻求高度可调控平台上强关联拓扑相的新实现途径。

Method: 采用单粒子能带分析判断莫尔能带（moiré bands）的平坦性与拓扑性质；基于自洽Hartree-Fock计算研究相互作用诱导的能带效应；并运用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）方法识别分数量填充下的拓扑相。

Result: 在适当的实验参数下，单粒子层面出现近平坦莫尔能带，但在Altland-Zirnbauer分类中为拓扑平庸；引入原子相互作用后，自洽Hartree-Fock计算给出平坦陈能带（flat Chern band）；精确对角化进一步在分数量填充处确认了分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）相。

Conclusion: 相互作用可在扭转双层光晶格中诱导非平庸拓扑能带与分数量子陈绝缘体相，为在该高度可调体系中探索强关联拓扑相（strongly correlated topological phases）开辟了道路。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer materials provide a versatile platform for realizing novel topological states. Motivated by recent experimental realization of atomic Bose-Einstein condensates in twisted bilayer optical lattices, we theoretically investigate topological states for cold atoms trapped in such a system. At single-particle level, the system hosts nearly flat moiré bands under appropriate experimental parameters. Although these noninteracting bands are topologically trivial in the Altland-Zirnbauer classification, we find that atomic interactions can induce a flat Chern band based on self-consistent Hartree-Fock calculations. Furthermore, exact diagonalization identifies a fractional Chern insulator phase at fractional filling. Our work thus paves the way for exploring strongly correlated topological phases in highly tunable twisted bilayer optical lattices.

</details>


### [169] [A machine-learned dynamical phase diagram of the one-dimensional nonlinear Schrödinger equation with quasiperiodic disorder and a static field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10045)
*Marcos Pérez*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作以短时模拟与无监督聚类相结合的方式，绘制一维离散非线性薛定谔（Gross-Pitaevskii）方程在准周期 Aubry-André 势与静态 Stark 场下的输运相图，识别出五种输运区域，并由短时特征预测长时渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对参数空间逐点进行长时间渐近模拟（每点 $t\gtrsim10^6$）在计算上不可行，因此需要一种低成本方法在自相互作用、场强与准周期强度构成的三维参数空间中系统地划分输运区域。

Method: 用 3600 次短时模拟替代昂贵的长时间模拟，将每次模拟压缩为八个动力学特征（dynamical features），再用高斯混合模型（Gaussian mixture model）对参数空间聚类；聚类边界由无监督方式确定，簇名由手工标定规则赋予，并通过消融实验（ablation）验证聚类确实平滑了边界而非仅重新标注。

Result: 得到五种输运区域：弹道（ballistic）、局域（localized）、振荡局域（oscillatory-localized）、亚扩散（subdiffusive）与自囚禁（self-trapped）。短时聚类可预测长时渐近扩展指数 $\alpha_\infty$（弹道 $2.01\pm0.07$，亚扩散 $0.28\pm0.16$，与弱混沌预测 $\alpha=1/3$ 一致），并独立预测自囚禁簇的长时保持率 $\Pi_0=0.72\pm0.32$。初始态差异显著：delta 态在 $\lambda=0$ 处自囚禁起始于 $g=3.907\pm0.017$ 并随 $g$ 扩大形成楔形区域；Gaussian 态无自囚禁，而是在 Aubry-André 临界线附近形成随 $g$ 变宽的亚扩散通道。方案可在每切片数分钟内生成完整的 $15\times15$ 相图。

Conclusion: 短时模拟加无监督聚类可高效替代长时间渐近模拟，生成完整相图，并验证于比训练窗口更长的、尺寸大八倍的格点上的 100 次长时运行，具有较强的预测与诊断能力。

Abstract: We map the transport regimes of a wave packet evolving under the one-dimensional discrete nonlinear Schrödinger (Gross-Pitaevskii) equation with a quasiperiodic Aubry-André potential and a static (Stark) field, as a function of self-interaction $g$, field $F$, and quasiperiodic strength $λ$, for two initial states (delta and Gaussian). Instead of one expensive long-time simulation per grid point, we run 3600 short simulations, reduce each to eight dynamical features, and cluster parameter space with a Gaussian mixture model. Clustering finds the boundaries between regimes unsupervised; each cluster's name is then assigned by a hand-calibrated rule, and an ablation shows clustering measurably smooths those boundaries rather than merely relabeling them. The five resulting regimes (ballistic, localized, oscillatory-localized, subdiffusive, and self-trapped) are validated against 100 long runs on a lattice eight times larger than the short sweep, so the validation window is longer than the training window even for the fastest-spreading regime. The short-time clusters predict the long-time asymptotic spreading exponent ($α_\infty = 2.01\pm0.07$ ballistic, $0.28\pm0.16$ subdiffusive, consistent with the weak-chaos prediction $α=1/3$) and, independently, a long-time retention $Π_0=0.72\pm0.32$ for the self-trapped cluster, whose exponent alone is not diagnostic. The initial-state contrast is striking: the delta state develops a broad self-trapping wedge (onset at $g=3.907\pm0.017$ at $λ=0$) that invades the ballistic and localized regions as $g$ grows, while the Gaussian state shows no self-trapping, instead opening a subdiffusive corridor along the Aubry-André critical line that widens with $g$. The scheme yields a full $15\times15$ phase map in minutes per slice, where direct asymptotic simulation ($t\gtrsim10^6$ per point) is prohibitive.

</details>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Microscopic Understanding of Thermal-magnon Transport in a Low-damping Ferrimagnetic Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09674)
*Lerato Takana,Katya Mikhailova,Junwei Tong,Xiangcheng Liu,Kwangyul Hu,Juan Hofer,Guanxiong Qu,Clare Yu,Ivan Schuller,Michael Flatté,Xiaoqin Li,Yuri Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究利用非局域自旋塞贝克（spin Seebeck）几何结构，在低阻尼Li$_{0.5}$Al$_{1.0}$Fe$_{1.5}$O$_4$/Pt纳米器件中探究热激发磁振子（magnon）输运，揭示磁振子群速度与交换刚度是调控铁磁薄膜热磁振子输运的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热生成磁振子可在磁性绝缘体中实现热驱动自旋输运，但其传播的微观机制仍缺乏清晰理解。本研究旨在分离磁振子输运与局域热电势效应，以厘清其本征输运行为及调控机制。

Method: 采用非局域自旋塞贝克几何结构，探测区域位于热愈合长度（thermal healing length）之外；结合热成像、布里渊光散射（Brillouin light scattering）与微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations），研究磁场与温度对磁振子输运的影响。

Result: 远高于饱和磁场的磁场强烈抑制热磁振子输运；布里渊光散射显示磁场增大使背向体磁振子（backward volume magnons）群速度下降，为磁振子自旋扩散长度减小提供微观解释。温度升高时，尽管磁振子数量增加，热磁振子输运反而减弱，微磁模拟仅在引入温度依赖交换刚度（exchange stiffness）时才能再现该现象。

Conclusion: 磁振子群速度与交换刚度被确定为铁磁薄膜中热磁振子输运的关键控制参数，为理解磁场与温度依赖的自旋输运提供微观机制依据。

Abstract: Thermally generated magnons enable heat-driven spin transport in magnetic insulators, yet the microscopic mechanisms governing their propagation remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate thermal magnon transport in low-damping Li$_{0.5}$Al$_{1.0}$Fe$_{1.5}$O$_4$/Pt nanodevices using a nonlocal spin Seebeck geometry that separates magnon transport from local thermoelectric effects. Thermal imaging establishes a detector region outside the thermal healing length, enabling intrinsic nonlocal measurements. We find that thermal magnon transport is strongly suppressed by magnetic fields far above saturation. Brillouin light scattering reveals that increasing field reduces the group velocity of backward volume magnons, providing a microscopic origin for the observed reduction in magnon spin diffusion length. We further find that thermal magnon transport decreases with increasing temperature despite an increasing magnon population. Micromagnetic simulations reproduce this behavior only when a temperature-dependent exchange stiffness is included. These results identify magnon group velocity and exchange stiffness as key parameters governing thermal magnon transport in ferrimagnetic thin films.

</details>
