# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Feasibility of a collider-based detector search for upward-going fermions produced from gravitationally-bound dark matter within the Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09194)
*Tamas Almos Vami,Sanjit Masanam,Matt Bellis,Josephine Swann,Philip Tanedo,Adam Green*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文评估了利用 LHC 级别通用探测器（general-purpose detector）观测源自地球内部俘获暗物质（dark matter）湮灭所产生的向上飞行 μ 子（upward-going muons）的可行性与灵敏度，发现预期限制显著优于现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质被理论预言可被地球引力俘获并湮灭为暗光子（dark photon），暗光子通过动力学混合（kinetic mixing）与标准模型光子耦合，进而在近地表衰变为可观测费米子对，这为间接探测提供了新途径。

Method: 在假设的通用探测器上计算预期信号率（signal rates），考察显著本底（backgrounds）对灵敏度的影响，并纳入该探测器的实验效率（experimental efficiencies）以确定预期限制。

Result: 所得预期限制相较现有约束构成显著改进，表明该探测方案具有较高的物理潜力。

Conclusion: 结果强烈支持开展针对源自地球束缚暗物质的向上飞行 μ 子的搜寻，作为暗物质探测的新方向。

Abstract: Dark matter is theorized to be gravitationally captured within the Earth and to subsequently annihilate into dark photons. The dark photons kinetically mix with the Standard Model photons which would allow the decay of the dark photon to pairs of observable fermions near Earth's surface. We examine the feasibility and sensitivity of an LHC-class general-purpose detector to observe muons originating from these dark matter models. We calculate the expected signal rates at a hypothetical general-purpose detector and examine the effect of significant backgrounds on sensitivity. We also consider experimental efficiencies of such a detector to determine expected limits for the proposed analysis. We find these expected limits constitute a significant improvement over existing ones, strongly motivating a search for upward-going muons stemming from dark matter bound within the Earth.

</details>


### [172] [On the analytic continuation and matching of threshold singularities from Vacuum Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09202)
*Selomit Ramírez-Uribe,Prasanna K. Dhani,German F. R. Sborlini,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在圈树对偶（Loop-Tree Duality）框架下，系统讨论将初态在壳能量解析延拓至负值以从真空振幅生成散射振幅干涉项的全部运动学构型，并扩展阈值奇点匹配的讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从真空振幅生成散射振幅干涉项时，需将初态在壳能量解析延拓至负值，其各种运动学构型尚缺乏系统讨论；同时当阈值奇点匹配受味道（flavour）约束时，实现局域抵消存在困难。

Method: 对用于从真空振幅生成散射振幅干涉项的初态在壳能量负值解析延拓的全部运动学构型进行详细讨论；扩展此前关于阈值奇点匹配的分析；引入角平均（angular averaging）作为实现局域抵消的策略。

Result: 给出了负能量解析延拓所有运动学构型的完整讨论，并说明角平均可在阈值奇点匹配受味道约束时有效实现局域抵消。

Conclusion: 角平均被提出为一种高效策略，用于在味道约束下真空振幅阈值奇点匹配情形中达成局域抵消，从而完善圈树对偶中干涉项生成的处理。

Abstract: We provide a detailed discussion for all kinematical configurations of the analytic continuation to negative values of initial-state on-shell energies used to generate interferences of scattering amplitudes from vacuum amplitudes in the Loop-Tree Duality. We also extend previous discussions on the matching of threshold singularities and introduce angular averaging as an efficient strategy to achieve a local cancellation when the matching of threshold singularities from vacuum amplitudes is constrained by flavour.

</details>


### [173] [Level-Four Modular Symmetry Selected by the Harmonic Pattern of Quark and Lepton Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09262)
*Vernon Barger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出夸克与带电轻子质量比遵循以四为模的层级模式（modular level four / 有限群 S4），据此用 theta 常数拟合轻子质量并预言夸克质量、CKM 矩阵与中微子谱，且无连续自由参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量比的规律显示对数层级步长呈 2:1 比例（近似八度关系），且上下型夸克与下型夸克-轻子比较之间存在镜像排序，这一结构亟需一个统一的对称性框架加以解释。

Method: 将模式解读为 Z_N 时钟上的电荷计数，发现仅在第四层级（或其倍数）实现，从而引入模形式第四层级与有限群 S4，三代位于 T 荷 (3,1,0) 的三重态、镜像置于符号单态；数据确定基参数 ε=14/75 与调和模 τ=1.0685i，并用五个 weight one-half theta 常数公设加两条公设与一条经验关系。

Result: 带电轻子质量比以 0.02% 精度落在 theta 修饰格点上；由三个轻子质量与电弱标度重现六个夸克质量，给出 |V_us|=0.2255、|V_cb|=0.04197、|V_ub|=0.003721、δ=1.138（实测 1.139±0.023）；幺正三角顶点 (0.161,0.348) 与 J=3.11×10⁻⁵ 均与实测一致；中微子方面 sinθ13=θ2(τ)m2/m3 在 JUNO 207 天数据上符合 0.7σ，给出 m1=0.25 meV、Σmν=0.0589 eV、δCP=π 及 1.3–3.9 meV 的等效 Majorana 质量。

Conclusion: 该结构性模式在带电轻子、夸克与中微子中一致成立，其紫外完成属于磁化环面（magnetized torus）类，Pati–Salam 为自然规范嵌入，且不引入任何连续参数。

Abstract: The mass ratios of the quarks and charged leptons follow a simple pattern. The logarithmic generation steps come in a two to one ratio, a note to its octave, and the ordering is mirrored between the up-down and down-lepton comparisons. The pattern, read as charge counting on a $Z_N$ clock, is realized at level four and at no level that is not a multiple of four, which motivates modular level four and the finite group $S_4$, with the generations in the triplet at $T$ charges $(3,1,0)$ and the mirror in the sign singlet. The charged-lepton mass ratios lie on a theta-dressed lattice at $0.02\%$, which sets the base $\varepsilon=0.18664$, taken as $14/75$, and the harmonic modulus $τ=1.0685i$. Five postulates in the weight one-half theta constants, identified on the same data, reproduce the six quark masses from three lepton masses and the electroweak scale. Two more and one empirical relation for $|V_{td}|$ close the CKM matrix, giving $|V_{us}|=\sqrt{m_d/m_s}=0.2255$, $|V_{cb}|=0.04197$, $|V_{ub}|=0.003721$, and $δ=1.138$ against the measured $1.139\pm0.023$, with no continuous parameter. The unitarity triangle follows, with apex $(0.161,0.348)$ against the measured $(0.161\pm0.010,0.347\pm0.010)$, and $J=3.11\times10^{-5}$ against $(3.09\pm0.07)\times10^{-5}$. The same structure carries the neutrinos. A single theta insertion gives $\sinθ_{13}=θ_2(τ)m_2/m_3$, which holds at $0.7σ$ on the 207-day JUNO data, and the harmonic condition completes a normal-ordered spectrum with $m_1=0.25$~meV, $Σm_ν=0.0589$~eV, a CP-conserving phase $δ_{CP}=π$, and effective Majorana masses quantized at $1.3$ to $3.9$~meV. The ultraviolet completion belongs to the magnetized torus class, with Pati--Salam the natural gauge embedding.

</details>


### [174] [More QCD Masterclass Lectures on Jet Physics and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09785)
*Andrew J. Larkoski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 这些讲义源自 2026 年法国 Saint-Jacut-de-la-mer 的 QCD Masterclass，在 2024 年学校已发表笔记基础上补充若干近年热门新主题，并推导了部分新结果（尤其与异常检测相关）。全部结果均基于 QCD 基本原理得出，不涉及机器学习细节讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲义的写作动机是补充 2024 年学校已发表笔记，纳入近年来流行起来的新主题，包括事件度量、异常检测、标度律、分类性能极限以及尊重时空对称性的回归。作者同时指出，这些问题虽由应用于 QCD 的机器学习所激发，但讨论不涉及机器学习本身细节。

Method: 方法上，所有结果通过应用量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）的基本原理推导得到，而非依赖机器学习技术细节。文中除综述已知结果外，还针对部分主题（特别是异常检测）给出新推导结果；讲义开篇以"为人文主义物理学的致歉"起始。

Result: 讲义呈现了事件度量（event metrics）、异常检测（anomaly detection）、标度律（scaling laws）、分类性能极限（limits of classification performance）以及尊重时空对称性的回归（regression respecting spacetime symmetries）等主题的结果，其中许多为已知结果，部分为新推导，尤其是异常检测相关的新结果。

Conclusion: 结论是：这些问题虽然源自机器学习在 QCD 中的应用，但其全部结果均可由 QCD 基本原理推出，无需涉及机器学习细节；讲义在既有笔记基础上拓展了新主题并补充了新结果。

Abstract: These lectures were presented at the 2026 QCD Masterclass in Saint-Jacut-de-la-mer, France. They supplement the published notes from the 2024 school with presentation of a few new topics that have become popular in recent years. These include event metrics, anomaly detection, scaling laws, the limits of classification performance, and regression that respects spacetime symmetries. Many of the presented results are known, but some new results are derived, especially related to anomaly detection. As was the case in those original notes, while all these problems are motivated from machine learning applied to QCD, no discussion of details of machine learning is presented in these notes. All results follow from application of fundamental principles of QCD. I begin these notes with an apology for humanist physics.

</details>


### [175] [Charmed baryon semileptonic decays in a relativistic three-quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09802)
*Ru-Hui Ni,Zhen-Yang Wang,Jia-Jun Wu,Bing-Song Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在相对论三夸克模型（relativistic three-quark model）框架下计算单粲重子（singly charmed baryon）半轻子衰变，无需引入可调参数；Λc 衰变结果与实验及格点 QCD（Lattice QCD）吻合，Ξc0 分支比高于实验平均值，Ωc0 末态 Ξ- 呈现正纵向极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粲重子半轻子衰变是检验重夸克与轻味 SU(3) 对称性破缺的重要场所，且实验与格点 QCD 在部分道存在分歧。该研究旨在以无自由参数的模型统一描述多个衰变道并给出可供未来测量检验的预言。

Method: 采用相对论三夸克模型，由重子质量谱确定组分夸克质量与空间波函数。物理 Ξc 态中轻味 SU(3) 破缺（m_s > m_{u,d}）自然导致反三重态与六重态构型的相干混合，由质量本征态完全确定，故弱跃迁振幅无待定参数。使用 Bakamjian–Thomas 推进（Bakamjian–Thomas boost）计算 c→s,d 螺旋度振幅（helicity amplitudes），包含空间雅可比（spatial Jacobian）与组分自旋的 Wigner 转动。

Result: 由振幅得到分支比、q^2 分布、纵向极化与形状因子（form factors）。Λc+→Λℓ+νℓ 与 Λc+→nℓ+νℓ 的分支比和形状因子与实验数据及格点 QCD 一致；Ξc0→Ξ-e+νe 得分支比约 4.0%，与近期格点 QCD 相符但明显高于当前实验平均值；Ωc0→Ξ-ℓ+νℓ 中自旋为 1 的 ss 旁观夸克导致末态 Ξ- 正纵向极化，与以反三重态为主衰变道中的负极化相反。

Conclusion: 模型在无自由参数条件下可同时再现与 Λc 相关的实验与格点结果；Ξc0 分支比差异的来源尚需实验与理论的进一步专门研究。所给出的 q^2 谱、角分布不对称性（angular asymmetries）与末态重子极化预言可为未来单粲重子半轻子衰变测量提供参考。

Abstract: We study the spin-$1/2\to1/2$ semileptonic decays of singly charmed baryons ($Λ_c^+$, $Ξ_c^{0,+}$, and $Ω_c^0$) into the light baryon octet within a relativistic three-quark model. The constituent quark masses and spatial wave functions are determined by the baryon mass spectrum. For the physical $Ξ_c$ states, the light flavor $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ breaking ($m_s > m_{u,d}$) naturally induces a coherent mixing between the flavor antitriplet and flavor sextet configurations, which is completely fixed by the mass eigenstates. Consequently, no adjustable parameters are introduced in calculating the weak transition amplitudes. Using these wave functions, we calculate the $c\to s,d$ helicity amplitudes with the Bakamjian--Thomas boost, including the spatial Jacobian and the Wigner rotations of the constituent spins. The branching fractions, $q^2$ distributions, longitudinal polarizations, and form factors are then obtained from these amplitudes. For the $Λ_c^+\toΛ\ell^+ν_\ell$ and $Λ_c^+\to n\ell^+ν_\ell$ modes, our branching fractions and form factors are in good agreement with the experimental data and Lattice QCD results. For $Ξ_c^0\toΞ^-e^+ν_e$, we obtain branch ratio $\mathcal B_{\ell} \simeq 4.0~\%$, which is consistent with recent Lattice QCD calculations but lies well above the current experimental average. Clarifying the origin of this discrepancy calls for further dedicated efforts from both experimental and theoretical sides. For the $Ω_c^0\toΞ^-\ell^+ν_\ell$ decay, the spin-$1$ $ss$ spectator yields a positive longitudinal polarization of the final $Ξ^-$, in contrast to the negative polarizations in the predominantly antitriplet decay modes. Our predictions for the $q^2$ spectra, angular asymmetries, and final baryon polarizations may provide useful references for future measurements of singly charmed baryon semileptonic decays.

</details>


### [176] [Probing the Electromagnetic Structure of Heavy-Light Hybrid Mesons with Vector and Axial-Vector Quantum Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10070)
*A. Amiri,K. Azizi,P. Eslami*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在光锥QCD求和规则框架下研究矢量与轴矢量重轻杂化介子的电磁性质，通过外场光子关联函数推导磁偶极与电四极形状因子，并在Q²=0处提取电磁矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量与轴矢量重轻杂化介子含有显式胶子自由度，其电磁性质（electromagnetic properties）尚需系统性研究；本文旨在为J^{P(C)}=1^{-(-)}、1^{+(-)}、1^{+(+)}、1^{-(+)}等杂化组态提供电磁矩的理论预言。

Method: 采用光锥QCD求和规则（light cone QCD sum rules），引入外背景光子并构建相应插值流，将关联函数（correlation function）的强子表示与QCD表示匹配；QCD侧包含微扰光子发射贡献及由光子分布振幅（photon distribution amplitudes, DAs）描述的非微扰效应。

Result: 得到磁偶极形状因子与电四极形状因子，并在Q²=0处提取电磁矩；对\bar b g u、b g\bar u、\bar c g d、c g\bar d、\bar c g s、c g\bar s等带电组态给出数值预言。电磁矩随组态变化；电四极矩（EQMs）呈现较系统的规律：来自\mathcal J_μ^1与\mathcal J_μ^3的结果接近，\mathcal J_μ^2与\mathcal J_μ^4的结果接近，且前者量级一般略大；磁偶极矩（MDMs）则未呈现同样的系统性分组。

Conclusion: 杂化介子的电磁矩依赖具体组态与所用插值流；电四极矩在不同流之间表现出规律性配对，而磁偶极矩不具备相同的系统分组特征。含底夸克态的相关结果在摘要中未完整给出。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic (EM) properties of vector and axial-vector heavy-light hybrid mesons within the light cone QCD sum rules framework. These states are characterized by an explicit gluonic degree of freedom and are studied through interpolating currents corresponding to the $J^{P(C)}=1^{-(-)}$, $1^{+(-)}$, $1^{+(+)}$, and $1^{-(+)}$ hybrid configurations. By analyzing the correlation function in the presence of an external background photon and matching its hadronic and QCD representations, we derive the light cone sum rules for the magnetic dipole and electric quadrupole form factors and extract the corresponding electromagnetic moments at $Q^2=0$. The QCD side of the sum rules incorporates the perturbative photon emission contributions as well as the relevant non-perturbative effects described by photon distribution amplitudes (DAs). Numerical predictions are obtained for the charged $\bar b g u$, $b g\bar u$, $\bar c g d$, $c g\bar d$, $\bar c g s$, and $c g\bar s$ hybrid configurations. The calculated electromagnetic moments show variations among the different hybrid configurations. A more systematic pattern is observed for the electric quadrupole moments (EQMs): the results obtained from the $\mathcal J_μ^1$ and $\mathcal J_μ^3$ currents are generally close to each other, as are those obtained from $\mathcal J_μ^2$ and $\mathcal J_μ^4$, with the former pair generally yielding somewhat larger magnitudes. The magnetic dipole moments (MDMs), in contrast, do not necessarily exhibit the same systematic grouping, although they also show variations among the different currents and configurations. For the bottom-containing states...

</details>


### [177] [Quantifying Information Hierarchy for Neutrino Oscillation Parameters at JUNO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10383)
*Yu-han Shu,Neetu Raj Singh Chundawat,Luis A. Delgadillo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用量子与经典费舍尔信息（Fisher information）量化反应堆反中微子态中振荡参数信息在测量过程中的传递，为JUNO建立"信息阶梯"，揭示精度损失的参数依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子本质上是量子系统，振荡参数的估计精度最终取决于中微子态中编码的信息量以及测量提取信息的效率。因此需要量化信息从量子态经测量过程流向探测器事件的具体路径与损耗。

Method: 采用量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information）与经典费舍尔信息（classical Fisher information），追踪反应堆反中微子态中编码的信息如何流经测量过程，直至探测器观测到的事件，并据此为JUNO建立"信息阶梯"（information ladder）。

Result: 不同信息层级间的精度损失强烈依赖于具体参数。JUNO的配置在太阳扇区（solar sector）振荡参数上接近最优统计极限，但$θ_{13}$与$Δm_{31}^{2}$的信息因测量策略与探测器效应而显著退化。尽管存在信息损失，剩余信息仍足以使JUNO在六年内对$Δm_{31}^{2}$达到亚百分比（sub-percent）精度。

Conclusion: 信息从量子态到探测器事件的传递存在层级性损耗，且损耗程度随参数而异；JUNO对太阳扇区参数接近统计最优，对$θ_{13}$与$Δm_{31}^{2}$的测量策略与探测器效应导致明显信息退化，但残余信息仍可支撑$Δm_{31}^{2}$的亚百分比精度测量。

Abstract: Since neutrinos are quantum systems inherently, the precision with which oscillation parameters can be estimated ultimately depends on how much information about these parameters is encoded in the neutrino state and how efficiently that information can be extracted through measurement. In this work, we quantify how information encoded in reactor antineutrino states flows through the measurement process to the events observed at the detector, using quantum and classical Fisher information. We establish the information ladder for JUNO, revealing that the loss of precision across different information levels is strongly parameter dependent. We demonstrate that the JUNO configuration approaches the optimal statistical limit for the oscillation parameters of the solar sector, while information on $θ_{13}$ and $Δm_{31}^{2}$ is significantly degraded by the measurement strategy and detector effects. Despite this information loss, the remaining information is sufficient for JUNO to achieve sub-percent precision on $Δm_{31}^{2}$ within six years.

</details>


### [178] [Can Elastic Neutrino Scattering Account for the LZ230616 Event?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10504)
*Ayan Chattaraj,Anirban Majumdar,Dimitrios K. Papoulias,Rahul Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ 合作组报告的孤立核反冲事件 LZ230616 无法用弹性中微子-原子核散射（含标准模型内外及多种奇异中微子通量）解释，所有被考察情形均被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 报告了能量 248±23stat±23syst keV_nr 的孤立核反冲事件，其背景预期极低，且常规弹性暗物质（dark matter, DM）-原子核散射难以解释这一局域特征，故需检验其他物理来源的可能性。

Method: 考察相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, CEνNS）作为替代解释，涵盖标准模型内外的中微子-原子核相互作用，并纳入来自 DM 湮灭（χχ→νν̄）、DM 衰变（χ→νν̄）成对中微子及原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）蒸发的奇异中微子通量。

Result: 运动学考量、伴随的低能反冲谱以及现有约束三方面分析共同表明，所考察的所有情形均无法给出 LZ230616 的可行弹性中微子-原子核散射解释。

Conclusion: LZ230616 不能归因于弹性中微子-原子核散射，涉及标准模型内外相互作用及多种奇异中微子源的情形均被排除。

Abstract: Recently, the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration reported an isolated nuclear-recoil event at $248\pm 23_\mathrm{stat}\pm 23_\mathrm{syst}~\mathrm{keV_{nr}}$, in a region where the expected background is very small and conventional elastic dark matter (DM)-nucleus scattering cannot readily account for such a localized feature. We investigate whether LZ230616 could instead originate from coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS). We consider neutrino-nucleus interactions within and beyond the Standard Model, together with exotic neutrino fluxes from DM annihilation ($χχ\to ν\barν$) or decay ($χ\to ν\barν$) into neutrino pairs and from primordial black hole (PBH) evaporation. We show that kinematic considerations, the accompanying low energy recoil spectrum, and existing constraints exclude a viable interpretation of LZ230616 in terms of elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering for all scenarios considered.

</details>


### [179] [On the universality of soft emission beyond the next-to-soft order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10069)
*Martin Beneke,Maria Santana,Michel Stillger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于在壳有效理论（on-shell effective theory）重构非阿贝尔规范理论（non-abelian gauge theory）中的低-伯内特-克罗尔定理（Low-Burnett-Kroll theorem），给出任意软展开阶数下的外部与内部辐射的规范不变分解，并导出全阶外部发射振幅的紧凑表达式及局部发射算符形式，最后在标量量子色动力学（scalar QCD）中比较辐射与非辐射硬过程的独立振幅数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软定理（soft theorem）虽可将软规范玻色子（soft gauge boson）的树级发射振幅用非辐射振幅（non-radiative amplitude）表示，但在更高阶软展开中缺乏自然的规范不变（gauge-invariant）结构区分。动机在于借助在壳有效理论框架，把外部发射（external emission）与内部发射（internal emission）系统分离，从而推广至软展开的任意阶。

Method: 将在壳有效理论用于描述软规范玻色子的发射，实现任意软展开阶数下的规范不变分解，区分外部与内部发射过程；由此推导以非辐射振幅表示的全阶外部发射振幅，并将局部发射振幅表示为单一规范不变算符，对超过次软阶（next-to-soft）的每一阶均成立。

Result: 得到全阶外部发射振幅的紧凑表达式，其完全由非辐射振幅给出；同时证明在次软阶以上的每一阶，局部发射振幅可由单个规范不变算符表述。在标量量子色动力学中，对含任意数目标量夸克与反夸克的辐射与非辐射硬过程，比较了洛伦兹（Lorentz）与 SU(3) 不变振幅的数目。

Conclusion: 在壳有效理论为软定理提供了自然的规范不变组织方式，使外部与内部发射可逐阶分离，并将高阶局部发射统一为单一规范不变算符；标量 QCD 中的振幅计数比较为该结构提供了具体检验途径。

Abstract: The Low-Burnett-Kroll theorem (extended to non-abelian gauge theory) states that the tree-level emission amplitude of a soft gauge boson from an arbitrary hard process can be expressed in terms of the non-radiative amplitude including the next-to-soft term. Reformulating the soft theorem in the framework of an on-shell effective theory leads to a natural gauge-invariant split into an external and internal emission process to any order in the soft expansion. In this framework, we derive a compact expression for the all-order external emission amplitude expressed in terms of the non-radiative amplitude, and express the local emission amplitude through a single gauge-invariant operator at every order beyond the next-to-soft one. For scalar QCD, we compare the number of Lorentz- and $SU(3)$-invariant amplitudes for the radiative and non-radiative hard process with an arbitrary number of scalar quarks and antiquarks.

</details>


### [180] [An improved partial-wave projection of the one-particle exchange in relativistic three-body scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10306)
*Nicholas C. Chambers,Andrew W. Jackura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出了相对论三体散射振幅中单粒子交换（OPE）分波的改进有限求和表达式，适用于三个有质量无自旋粒子及任意角动量配置，并通过解析与数值检验验证其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子交换（one-particle exchange, OPE）是相对论三体散射振幅（relativistic three-body scattering amplitudes）的关键组成部分，此前工作在处理任意总角动量 J 所涉分波（partial waves）快速增多时效率不足。

Method: 给出针对三个有质量、无自旋粒子（massive, spinless particles）在任意角动量配置下有效的分波 OPE 有限求和（finite-sum）表达式，并进行解析与数值检验。

Result: 有限求和表达式再现了分波 OPE 的已知性质，包括阈行为标度（threshold scaling behavior）与奇异性结构（singularity structure）；相较以往工作，更高效地处理了贡献于任意总角动量 J 的分波快速增殖问题。

Conclusion: 该结果可支持构建稳健的波集（wavesets），用于三体振幅分析（three-body amplitude analyses）。

Abstract: A critical component of relativistic three-body scattering amplitudes is the one-particle exchange (OPE) process, wherein a single particle is exchanged between incoming and outgoing two-body subsystems. We present an improved, finite-sum expression for the partial-wave OPE that is valid for three massive, spinless particles in arbitrary angular momentum configurations. A collection of analytic and numerical checks demonstrate that our finite-sum expression reproduces the known properties of the partial-wave OPE, including its threshold scaling behavior and singularity structure. Compared with previous work, this result more efficiently handles the rapid proliferation of partial waves contributing to any total angular momentum $J$, enabling the construction of robust wavesets for three-body amplitude analyses.

</details>


### [181] [Anomalies at the End of the Universe?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10459)
*Jay Hubisz,Hanieh Moradipasha,Prakriti Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 AdS 切片（slice of AdS）上的额外维理论中，反常（anomaly）的空间分布在不同几何下不一致，而这些几何对偶于同一共形场论（CFT）的不同状态，由此产生一个疑难。作者主张，要实现上述对偶解释，必须通过引入拓扑项（topological terms）或其他体费米子（bulk fermions），使局域于红外（IR）的自洽反常流到几何的“紫外”（UV）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究针对 AdS 切片上额外维理论中反常空间分布的疑难：对偶于同一 CFT 不同状态的多种几何之间，反常可能无法匹配。此不一致性威胁到这些几何能否被一致地解释为同一 CFT 的对偶描述，因此需要加以解决。

Method: 论证方式基于反常一致性（consistency of the anomaly）的要求：考察反常在不同几何间的匹配条件，并由此论证需在理论中引入拓扑项（topological terms）或其他体费米子（bulk fermions）。其作用是把红外（IR）局域的自洽反常流入这些几何的“紫外”（UV）区域。

Result: 分析表明，反常在不同几何间的一致性无法自动满足，需借助额外成分才能成立。为此必须添加拓扑项或其他体费米子，使 IR 局域的自洽反常被流（flow）至几何的 UV 区域，从而实现对偶解释所需的一致性。

Conclusion: 若希望在 AdS 切片额外维理论中保留对偶解释，则反常跨几何的一致性要求理论包含拓扑项或其他体费米子，以将 IR 局域反常转移至 UV 区域；否则不同几何间的反常无法匹配。

Abstract: The spatial distribution of anomalies in extra dimensional theories on a slice of AdS leads to a puzzle where anomalies may not match across different geometries that are dual to various states of the same CFT. We argue that consistency of the anomaly across such geometries necessitates the addition of topological terms or other bulk fermions to the theory that flow any IR localized consistent anomaly into the ``UV" region of these geometries if one wishes to have such a dual interpretation.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [The anisotropic local law for sample covariance matrices under quadratic-form concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09440)
*Renyuan Ma,Theodor Misiakiewicz*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在比例渐近区间 n ≍ N 下研究样本协方差矩阵 K，仅假设列向量二次型以最优速率一致集中，即证明了各向异性局部律（anisotropic local law），从而移除了 Fan、Ma、Paquette 与 Wang（2026）工作中对高阶累积量张量（higher-cumulant tensor）的假设，回答了其提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于样本协方差矩阵局部律的结论通常依赖高阶累积量张量假设，该假设对若干重要分布（如高温球面 4-自旋模型）并不成立，因此需要探明仅靠二次型集中即可否得到最优局部律。作者旨在回答 Fan 等（2026）所提出的这一问题。

Method: 在 n ≍ N 的比例区间（proportional regime）下，设列向量 x_1,…,x_N 独立、中心化且具有共同协方差 Σ，但坐标间可有强非线性相依。仅假设列向量的二次型（quadratic forms）以最优速率一致集中，即 |x_i^* A x_i − Tr ΣA| ≺ ‖A‖_F，并附加多项式范数矩（polynomial norm moments）与 Σ 的标准非退化条件（nondegeneracy condition），在此框架下证明各向异性局部律。

Result: 在正则谱域（regular spectral domains）上，对任意确定性单位向量 u, v ∈ C^n，一致下至谱尺度 η := Im z ≥ N^{−1+τ}，得到最优各向异性局部律估计 |⟨u,((K−z)^{-1} − (−zI_n − z m̃_0(z)Σ)^{−1})v⟩| ≺ sqrt(Im m̃_0(z)/(Nη)) + 1/(Nη)，其中 m̃_0(z) 为变形 Marchenko-Pastur 律（deformed Marchenko-Pastur law）的 Stieltjes 变换。

Conclusion: 该结果在仅依赖二次型集中等温和假设下达成最优局部律，去除了高阶累积量张量假设。其适用范围涵盖具有有界非退化协方差的中心化对数凹（log-concave）列分布、高斯向量的非线性倾斜（nonlinear tilts）、深度随机特征（deep random features）以及累积量假设已知失效的高温球面 4-自旋模型（spherical 4-spin model）等例子。

Abstract: We study sample covariance matrices $K = \frac{1}{N} \sum_{i=1}^N \x_i \x_i^* \in \R^{n \times n}$ in the proportional regime $n \asymp N$. The columns $\x_1, \ldots, \x_N \in \R^n$ are independent and centered, with common covariance $\E \x_i \x_i^* = Σ$, but may otherwise have strongly and nonlinearly dependent coordinates. Assuming only that quadratic forms of the columns concentrate uniformly at the optimal rate $| \x_i^* A \x_i - \Tr ΣA | \prec \| A \|_F$, together with polynomial norm moments and a standard nondegeneracy condition on $Σ$, we prove the optimal anisotropic local law: on regular spectral domains, uniformly down to spectral scales $η:= \Im z \geq N^{-1 + τ}$,
  \[
  \big| \< \u , \big( (K-z)^{-1} - (-zI_n-z\widetilde m_0(z)Σ\big)^{-1} \bv \> \big| \prec \sqrt{\frac{\Im \widetilde m_0 (z)}{Nη}} + \frac{1}{Nη}
  \]
  for all deterministic unit vectors $\u,\bv \in \C^n$, where $\widetilde m_0(z)$ is the Stieltjes transform of the deformed Marchenko-Pastur law. This removes the higher-cumulant tensor assumption of Fan, Ma, Paquette, and Wang (2026), thereby answering the question raised in their work. The result applies, among other examples, to every centered log-concave column distribution with bounded, nondegenerate covariance, nonlinear tilts of Gaussian vectors, deep random features, and a high-temperature spherical 4-spin model for which the cumulant assumption is known to fail.

</details>


### [183] [Sharp Typical Distance and Exponential Small-Ball Bounds in One-Step-Cliff Nielsen Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09642)
*Honghuai Fang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究装备一步悬崖 Nielsen 度量（one-step-cliff Nielsen metric）的 $\operatorname{PU}(D)$（$D=2^n$）上 Haar 随机元素的距离渐近行为，证明归一化距离依概率与 $L^p$ 收敛到 $\pi/\sqrt{3}$，并给出精确的集中不等式与小球测度速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中量子门的复杂性度量通常由 Nielsen 度量刻画，但其在随机量子门上的典型尺度缺乏精确渐近刻画，因此需要确定 Haar 随机元素到单位元的距离极限及测度集中性质。

Method: 主要工具包括：Jacob 行列式（Jacobi determinant）比较以获得小球上界，通过线性化测地线方程的重标度与 Kato 输运（Kato transport）实现；Weyl 积分公式（Weyl integration formula）将积分约化为圆上的 Abel 正则化对数能量估计；中心主对数（centered principal logarithm）与圆酉系综（circular unitary ensemble）特征角二阶矩的集中性用于距离上界。

Result: Haar 随机元素的距离除以 $D$ 依概率与 $L^p$（$1\le p<\infty$）收敛至 $\pi/\sqrt{3}$，偏差为 $O(D^{-1/8}(\log D)^{1/2})$，例外集 Haar 测度不超过 $\exp\{-\Omega(D^{7/4}\log D)\}$。固定 $0<x<\pi/\sqrt{3}$ 时，半径 $xD$ 的球测度为 $\exp\{-\Theta_x(D^2)\}$；$x>\pi/\sqrt{3}$ 时补集测度不超过 $e^{-c_xD^2}$。当 $x\uparrow\pi/\sqrt{3}$，上下对数速率均渐近于 $(\pi^2/3-x^2)^2/(16\zeta(3))$。

Conclusion: 结果表明一步悬崖 Nielsen 度量下 Haar 随机量子门到单位元的距离具有尖锐的典型值 $\pi D/\sqrt{3}$ 与指数级小球/补集测度衰减，并揭示临界指数由 $\zeta(3)$ 决定的普适速率。

Abstract: Let $D=2^n$ and equip $\operatorname{PU}(D)$ with the one-step-cliff Nielsen metric, with quadratic metric coefficients one in Pauli directions of weights one and two and $D^2$ in all higher weights. We prove that the distance from the identity of a Haar-random element, normalized by $D$, converges to $π/\sqrt3$ in probability and in $L^p$ for every $1\le p<\infty$. Quantitatively, it lies within $O(D^{-1/8}(\log D)^{1/2})$ of this limit outside a set of Haar measure at most $\exp\{-Ω(D^{7/4}\log D)\}$. For each fixed $0<x<π/\sqrt3$, the ball of radius $xD$ has Haar measure $\exp\{-Θ_x(D^2)\}$; for $x>π/\sqrt3$, its complement has measure at most $e^{-c_xD^2}$ for some $c_x>0$. As $x\uparrowπ/\sqrt3$, the lower and upper logarithmic rates are both asymptotic to $(π^2/3-x^2)^2/(16ζ(3))$.
  The small-ball upper bound follows from a comparison of Jacobi determinants, obtained by rescaling the linearized geodesic equations and applying Kato transport. Weyl integration reduces the remaining integral to an Abel-regularized logarithmic-energy estimate on the circle. A centered principal logarithm and concentration of the circular unitary ensemble eigenangle second moment give the distance upper bound.

</details>


### [184] [A central limit theorem for Bessel and Dunkl processes with drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10040)
*Michael Voit*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对带漂移的多元（Dunkl-）Bessel 过程（multivariate Dunkl-Bessel processes），在正则漂移向量、任意根系（root systems）与重数（multiplicities）条件下，证明了其中心极限定理（central limit theorem），改进了作者此前的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元（Dunkl-）Bessel 过程可视为欧氏空间上布朗运动在 Weyl 腔（Weyl chambers）上的投影，典型例子为 Dyson 布朗运动；带漂移情形对应带漂移的 Bessel 过程，其转移密度可用多元 Bessel 函数刻画。作者希望在此框架下建立更一般的中心极限定理，以推广并改进已有的近期结果。

Method: 论文通过研究 Weyl 腔上带漂移的 Bessel 过程与 Dunkl 过程的投影结构，并在正则漂移向量（regular drift vectors）、任意根系与任意重数的一般设定下，建立相应的中心极限定理。

Result: 在正则漂移向量、任意根系与重数条件下，所研究的带漂移 Bessel 过程与 Dunkl 过程均满足中心极限定理。

Conclusion: 本文的中心极限定理结论具有一般性，涵盖任意根系与重数，改进（improve）了作者近期的相关结果，深化了对带漂移（Dunkl-）Bessel 过程渐近行为的理解。

Abstract: For some discrete parameters, multivariate (Dunkl-)Bessel processes on Weyl chambers $C$ are projections of Brownian motions on Euclidean spaces $V$; the most prominent examples are Dyson Brownian motions. More generally, the projections of Brownian motions on $V$ with drift are Bessel processes with drifts on $C$, where again the associated transition densities can be described in terms of multivariate Bessel functions. Moreover, Dunkl processes with drift can be defined in an analogous way. In this paper we prove central limit theorems for these Bessel and Dunkl processes with drift for regular drift vectors, and arbitrary root systems and multiplicities. These results improve recent results of the author.

</details>


### [185] [Canonical Local Equilibrium and Cutoff Profiles for the Symmetric Exclusion Process on Discrete Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10304)
*Joe P. Chen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在离散环面（discrete torus）$\mathbb{T}_N^D$（$D\ge2$）上，对对称简单排它过程（symmetric simple exclusion process）的规范（固定粒子数）局部平衡定理给出严格证明，表明在特定时间尺度上相对平衡态的 Radon–Nikodym 密度依 $L^2$ 收敛到规范指数倾斜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于刻画在固定粒子数（canonical/fixed-population）约束下，排它过程偏离平衡态后的局部平衡行为与到达平稳分布的定量速率。为此需在粒子密度远离 $0$ 与 $1$、且对所有给定粒子数的确定性初始构型一致成立的条件下，建立规范版本的局部平衡定理。

Method: 证明结合三个要素：校准的规范比较（calibrated canonical comparison）、独立过程与排它过程动力学之间的固定阶比较（fixed-degree comparison），以及由对能量估计（pair energy estimates）与强瑞利性质（Strong–Rayleigh property）保持性得到的全阶控制（all-degree control）。关键技术对象是唯一的均值为零的小校准场（small mean-zero calibration field），其一格边缘分布与演化中的单粒子热剖面（one-particle heat profile）匹配。

Result: 在时间 $t_N(s)=(\log(N^D)+s)/(2\gamma_N)$（其中 $\gamma_N=2-2\cos(2\pi/N)$）上，过程相对平衡态的 Radon–Nikodym 密度依 $L^2$ 收敛到由该校准场生成的规范指数倾斜。由此，只要剖面坐标收敛，相应的总变差剖面即为高斯平移；更精确地，若协方差归一化平方振幅 $\mathfrak q_N^{S_N}(s)$ 在固定 $s$ 处收敛到标量 $\mathfrak q$，则到平稳分布的距离收敛到 $2\Phi(\sqrt{\mathfrak q}/2)-1$。剖面坐标渐近上由最小非零特征值对应的单粒子特征空间决定。

Conclusion: 论文给出了对称简单排它过程在固定粒子数设定下的规范局部平衡定理，并提供了到平稳分布的定量极限刻画；其结果的一致性覆盖所有满足给定粒子数的确定性初始构型，且剖面坐标由最小非零特征值对应的单粒子特征空间渐近确定。

Abstract: We prove a canonical (or fixed-population) local equilibrium theorem for the symmetric simple exclusion process on the discrete torus $\mathbb T_N^D$, $D\ge2$, at particle densities bounded away from $0$ and $1$, uniformly over all deterministic initial configurations with the prescribed particle number. At times \[
  t_N(s)=\frac{\log(N^D)+s}{2γ_N},
  \qquad γ_N=2-2\cos\left(\frac{2π}{N}\right), \] the Radon--Nikodym density of the process relative to equilibrium converges in $L^2$ to a canonical exponential tilt generated by the unique small mean-zero calibration field whose one-site marginals match the evolving one-particle heat profile. Consequently, whenever the profile coordinate converges, the corresponding total variation profile is a Gaussian shift. More precisely, for a deterministic initial sequence $(S_N)$, if the covariance-normalized squared amplitude $\mathfrak q_N^{S_N}(s)$ converges to a scalar $\mathfrak q$ at a fixed $s$, then the distance to stationarity converges to $
  2Φ\!\left({\sqrt{\mathfrak q}}/2\right)-1 $, where $Φ$ is the standard normal distribution function. The profile coordinate is asymptotically determined by the one-particle eigenspace corresponding to the smallest nonzero eigenvalue.
  The proof combines a calibrated canonical comparison, a fixed-degree comparison between independent and exclusion dynamics, and all-degree control obtained from pair energy estimates and preservation of the Strong--Rayleigh property.

</details>


### [186] [Aldous' spectral gap phenomena in stochastic exchange models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10450)
*Pietro Caputo,Matteo Quattropani,Federico Sau*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究一类广义交换动力学（exchange dynamics），包括 Kipnis–Marchioro–Presutti（KMP）模型与球面上 Kac's walk 的能量，其可逆测度为 Dirichlet 分布。作者证明谱隙（spectral gap）总由能量变量的至多二次多项式实现，即主导模式至多对应双粒子可观测量，并刻画了一粒子与双粒子主导的几何条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类保守连续自旋系统（conservative continuous-spin systems）的可逆测度为 Dirichlet 分布，其谱隙结构此前缺乏统一刻画；同时 Alon 与 Puder 近期提出的猜想尚待解决。论文旨在统一理解交换动力学中主导模式为何总可由低阶（至多二次）能量多项式刻画。

Method: 方法上，通过分析原过程的对偶表示“隐藏模型（hidden model）”以及对相关离散粒子系统的 intertwining 技术来建立证明。该二次降阶（degree-two reduction）机制被推广到更广泛的随机交换模型及其粒子系统，并进一步分析带边界驱动（boundary-driven）的非可逆版本。

Result: 主要结果为：谱隙总由能量变量的至多二次多项式达到，等价地主导模式为一粒子或双粒子可观测量；由此得到的双粒子谱隙恒等式解决了 Alon–Puder 猜想。作者精确刻画了一粒子足够与双粒子主导的界限：在一致重标度下，segment-like 几何总是一粒子型，mean-field 几何总是双粒子型，其余几何出现非平凡转变。对边界驱动 KMP 模型，其非可逆谱隙总为一粒子型。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Aldous 型谱隙现象推广至加权图或超图（hypergraph）更新结构的一般交换动力学，并显示二次降阶机制具有稳健性，可延伸至 harmonic process、immediate exchange model、averaging-type processes 及非可逆变体。其结论为保守与边界驱动两类情形提供了统一的谱隙类型分类。

Abstract: We consider a broad class of exchange dynamics with arbitrary weighted graph or hypergraph update structures, which includes the Kipnis--Marchioro--Presutti (KMP) model and the energies of Kac's walk on the sphere. These are conservative continuous-spin systems whose reversible measures are Dirichlet distributions. We prove that their spectral gap is always attained by a polynomial of degree at most two in the energy variables. Equivalently, through an intertwining with the associated discrete particle systems, the dominant mode is always represented by either a one-particle or a two-particle observable. We interpret this as a manifestation of an Aldous-type spectral gap phenomenon. In particular, the resulting two-particle spectral gap identity settles a recent conjecture of Alon and Puder. We also characterize sharply when one particle suffices and when a genuinely two-particle mode dominates. Under a common rescaling of the Dirichlet parameters, segment-like geometries are precisely those for which the gap is always of one-particle type, mean-field geometries are precisely those for which it is always of two-particle type, and all other geometries exhibit a nontrivial transition. The proof relies on the analysis of the so-called hidden model, a dual representation of the original process. The mechanism underlying the degree-two reduction is remarkably robust and extends to a much broader class of stochastic exchange models and their associated particle systems, including the harmonic process, the immediate exchange model, averaging-type processes, and nonreversible variants. Finally, we analyze boundary-driven versions of the KMP model, in which the bulk exchange dynamics interacts with reservoirs, generally resulting in nonreversible processes. In contrast to the conservative setting, we prove that the spectral gap of a boundary-driven model is always of one-particle type.

</details>


### [187] [Fluctuations of additive martingale limits of branching Brownian motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10530)
*Xinxin Chen,Michel Pain*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文强化并细化了一维分支布朗运动（branching Brownian motion）中加性鞅（additive martingale）临界极限定理：将 Madaule 的概率收敛升级为几乎必然收敛（almost sure convergence），并刻画了收敛过程中的涨落（fluctuations），给出谱负 1-稳定分布（spectrally negative 1-stable distribution）极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Madaule (2016) 已建立次临界区间 |β| < β_c 下加性鞅极限 W_∞(β) 与临界导数鞅（derivative martingale）极限 Z_∞ 之间的概率收敛关系，但该结果的收敛模式与二阶涨落结构尚未刻画，本文旨在补足这两方面。

Method: 研究对象为一维分支布朗运动，考察次临界加性鞅极限 W_∞(β)、临界导数鞅极限 Z_∞，分析 β ↗ β_c 时 (W_∞(β)/(β_c−β) − 2Z_∞) 的渐近行为并引入对数修正项，进而推导其标准化后的分布极限；方法延伸至复加性鞅（complex additive martingales）情形及多维收敛。

Result: 首先将 Madaule 的概率收敛强化为几乎必然收敛。其次证明标准化涨落依分布收敛（convergence in distribution）到随机变量 S：在给定 Z_∞ 的条件下，S 服从谱负 1-稳定分布，其尺度参数（scale parameter）与位移参数（shift parameter）均与 Z_∞ 成正比。上述结果进一步推广至复加性鞅及多维收敛框架。

Conclusion: 本文在收敛模式与涨落结构两个层面深化了对分支布朗运动加性鞅临界行为的理解，所得几乎必然收敛与稳定分布涨落结果可自然推广到复值设定与多维情形。

Abstract: Consider a one-dimensional branching Brownian motion. Let $W_\infty(β)$ denote the limit of the additive martingale in the subcritical regime $\lvert β\rvert < β_c$ and $Z_\infty$ be the limit of the derivative martingale at criticality. Madaule (Stochastic Process. Appl. 126 (2016), no. 2, 470--502) established the following convergence \[ \frac{W_\infty(β)}{β_c-β}\xrightarrow[β\nearrow β_c]{\mathbb{P}} 2Z_\infty. \] The goal of this paper is twofold: firstly, we strengthen this result into an almost sure convergence; secondly, we describe the fluctuations occurring in this convergence by proving \[ \frac{1}{β_c-β}\left( \frac{W_\infty(β)}{β_c-β} - 2 Z_\infty +2(β_c-β)\log(β_c-β) Z_\infty\right) \xrightarrow[β\nearrow β_c]{(d)} S, \] where, conditionally on $Z_\infty$, $S$ follows a spectrally negative 1-stable distribution with scale and shift parameters proportional to $Z_\infty$. Furthermore, these results are extended to the setting of complex additive martingales and the fluctuations to a multi-dimensional convergence.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [The hyperbolic class and vanishing laws in a TMF-valued four-manifold invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09819)
*Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 在谱 Looijenga 构造（spectral Looijenga construction）的显式假设下，直接计算了 Gukov–Krushkal–Meier–Pei 零截面不变量（zero-section invariant）在周期拓扑模形式（periodic topological modular forms）中的双曲平面取值，结果为 Hopf 元素 eta。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作需额外的配边对偶（cobordism-duality）假设才能得到双曲取值，本文旨在去掉该假设，直接从 Looijenga 限制映射（Looijenga restriction maps）推导，从而获得更一般、更完整的取值结论。

Method: 在关于谱 Looijenga 构造的显式假设下，通过 Looijenga 限制映射直接推导双曲平面的取值，而非依赖配边对偶假设。

Result: 双曲平面的取值为 Hopf 元素 eta，且加入一个双曲平面（hyperbolic plane）的作用等价于乘以该元素；结合定格（definite lattices）的消灭与消没结果，得到光滑闭单连通 spin 四维流形上的完整取值公式。

Conclusion: 非零符号差（signature）迫使该不变量为零，因此 K3 曲面的两个定向（orientations）取值均为零。

Abstract: Under explicit hypotheses on the spectral Looijenga construction, we compute the hyperbolic-plane value of the zero-section invariant of Gukov, Krushkal, Meier, and Pei in periodic topological modular forms. The value is the Hopf element eta, and adjoining a hyperbolic plane acts by multiplication by this element. Earlier work obtains the hyperbolic value under an additional cobordism-duality assumption; here we derive it directly from the Looijenga restriction maps without that assumption. Together with annihilation and vanishing results for definite lattices, this gives a complete value formula on smooth closed simply connected spin four-manifolds. In particular, nonzero signature forces the invariant to vanish, so both orientations of a K3 surface have value zero.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Constant Maps and Exponentially Small Sectors in ABJM Bethe Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09273)
*Seyed Morteza Hosseini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在姊妹篇重构 universal twist 下有限秩扭超势（twisted superpotential）的基础上，利用高精度 Bethe-vacuum 数据、物理启发的符号回归（symbolic regression）与整数关系，确定了 ABJM 理论的有限秩拓扑扭指标（topologically twisted index）。结果给出可证明的积分表示、Euler 和求值，以及在 k=1,2,4 时指数抑制扇区的算术结构，为量子引力块与黑洞量子熵提供有限 N 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABJM 理论的拓扑扭指标可刻画磁荷超对称 AdS₄ 黑洞的微观熵，但有限秩情形下该指标的重构尚未完成。姊妹篇已重构 universal twist 处的有限秩扭超势，然而 Hessian、有效膨胀子（effective dilaton）与单圈因子所携带的信息超出在壳扭超势，因此需要独立确定有限秩指标。

Method: 采用高精度 Bethe-vacuum 数据、物理启发的符号回归与整数关系方法。在所声明的有限类内，将指标常映射（constant map）由 ABJM S³ 在能级 k 与 k/2 的常映射、扭超势常映射及初等项重构；并借助解析证明建立与一核积分、Euler 和及绝对收敛积分的等价关系。

Result: 证明该指标常映射对实 k>0 等于一个收敛的一核积分，从而重求和 large-k 展开、确定此前仅数值给出的常数，并给出任意亏格（genus）的 type-IIA 系数。证明 arXiv:2608.04107 中的 Clausen 表达式等于交错 Euler 和（alternating Euler sum）与绝对收敛积分，给出两类有理自变量 Euler 和族的闭式求值。k=1,2,4 时指数抑制扇区呈现算术结构：扭超势重构系数服从除数和（divisor-sum）公式，并伴随与权四 Eisenstein 形式相关的全阶 Eichler 积分级数猜想；指标系数约化为两个有理生成元，其中之一可由模积（modular-product）公式复现全部可得系数。

Conclusion: 在该模积延拓成立的条件下，模变换（modular transformations）可确定最近对数奇点、收敛半径与首项系数增长。两观测量共享同一平移秩。上述结果构成量子引力块（quantum gravitational blocks）与体黑洞量子熵的有限 N 基准。

Abstract: The topologically twisted index of ABJM theory captures the microscopic entropy of magnetically charged supersymmetric $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ black holes. Building on the companion Letter arXiv:2608.04107, which reconstructed the finite-rank twisted superpotential at the universal twist, we determine the finite-rank index using high-precision Bethe-vacuum data, physics-informed symbolic regression, and integer relations. The Hessian, effective dilaton, and one-loop factors encode information beyond the on-shell twisted superpotential.
  The two observables share the same shifted rank. Within a declared finite class, we reconstruct the index constant map from the ABJM $S^3$ constant maps at levels $k$ and $k/2$, the constant map of the twisted superpotential, and elementary terms. We prove that it equals a convergent one-kernel integral for real $k>0$, resumming the large-$k$ expansion, fixing a previously numerical constant, and yielding type-IIA coefficients at arbitrary genus. We also prove that the Clausen expression in arXiv:2608.04107 equals an alternating Euler sum and an absolutely convergent integral, giving closed evaluations for two rational-argument Euler-sum families.
  At $k=1,2,4$, the exponentially suppressed sectors exhibit arithmetic structure. The reconstructed coefficients of the twisted superpotential obey a divisor-sum formula with a conjectural all-order Eichler-integral series associated with a weight-four Eisenstein form. The index coefficients reduce to two rational generators; one admits modular-product formulas reproducing every available coefficient and suggesting an all-order completion. Conditional on this continuation, modular transformations determine the nearest logarithmic singularity, radius of convergence, and leading coefficient growth. These results provide a finite-$N$ benchmark for quantum gravitational blocks and bulk black hole quantum entropy.

</details>


### [190] [Towards quantum elliptic cohomology from GLSMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09277)
*Z. Cao,E. Sharpe,H. Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出将数学界正在发展的量子椭圆上同调环（quantum elliptic cohomology rings）解释为 GLSM 的库仑分支（Coulomb branch）计算，并指出该解释在部分基本例子中受四维规范反常阻碍，进而给出一个经典超势替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在为 Bouaziz-Huq-Kuruvilla-Lee 正在构建的量子椭圆上同调环提供物理（GLSM）诠释，延续 Fano 环面簇（Fano toric varieties）的量子上同调与量子 K 理论（quantum K theory）中成熟的处理范式。

Method: 方法上采用 GLSM 框架，以扭曲有效超势（twisted effective superpotentials）的临界轨迹（critical loci）给出环关系；对量子椭圆上同调，则尝试通过四维理论经 KK 约化（KK reduction）得到的二维理论中的库仑分支计算，来刻画平行面缺陷（parallel surface defects）的算符乘积展开（OPE）。

Result: 在若干基本例子中，上述库仑分支解释受到四维规范反常（gauge anomalies）的阻碍；对此作者提供一个经典超势，其临界轨迹可生成预期的环关系，但该超势并非来自四维 KK 约化，尽管具备许多类似特征。文中给出了若干简单例子，并对 gerbe 情形作出预测。

Conclusion: 量子椭圆上同调环的 GLSM 库仑分支诠释在原则上可行，但受四维反常限制而需借助非 KK 来源的经典超势实现，该方案对 gerbe 等情形给出可检验的预测。

Abstract: This paper briefly outlines a proposal for a GLSM Coulomb-branch interpretation of the quantum elliptic cohomology rings currently being developed in mathematics by Bouaziz-Huq-Kuruvilla-Lee. The proposal is modeled on the established GLSM computations of quantum cohomology and quantum K theory of Fano toric varieties, where ring relations arise as critical loci of twisted effective superpotentials. In the case of quantum elliptic cohomology, the natural generalization would be to encode ring relations in Coulomb branch computations in two-dimensional theories obtained by KK reductions from four-dimensional theories, describing OPEs of parallel surface defects, which we review. However, in some basic examples, this interpretation is obstructed by four-dimensional gauge anomalies. In such cases, we provide a classical superpotential whose critical loci generate the expected ring relations, but which is not obtained by KK reduction from four dimensions, though it shares many characteristics with such KK reductions. We describe some simple examples, and make some predictions for the case of gerbes.

</details>


### [191] [Bounds on scattering amplitudes of non-identical scalar particles in 4d](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09282)
*Gabriele Ferretti,Denis Karateev,Alessandro Piazza,Marco Serone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文运用原初 S-矩阵自举（primal S-matrix bootstrap）方法，在四维时空中首次研究涉及两种不同标量粒子的二对二散射振幅。通过引入 Z₂×Z₂ 全局对称性并考察不等质量情形，发现"伪物理割"（pseudo-physical cut）导致部分观测量无界，等质量极限下两侧界限得以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 S-矩阵自举研究多集中于单一粒子或等质量情形，涉及两种不同标量粒子的散射振幅尚属空白。本文旨在为 pion-kaon、pion-nucleon 等物理过程的自举研究建立起点。

Method: 采用原初 S-矩阵自举，在 d = 4 时空中施加 Z₂×Z₂ 全局对称性，使两种粒子为携带电荷 (-,+) 与 (+,-) 的最轻粒子，以保证绝对稳定并排除三角反常阈。通过 Omnès 参数化（Omnès parametrisation）引入伪物理割不连续性的信息作为验证手段。

Result: 不等质量情形下，伪物理割的不连续性不受物理幺正性直接约束，导致若干观测量无界，另一些仅服从非平凡的单侧界限，或在固定其他观测量后变为有界。等质量极限下伪物理区域消失，两侧界限恢复。通过 Omnès 参数化提供伪物理不连续性信息亦可恢复有界性。

Conclusion: 伪物理割的存在从根本上改变了自举问题的结构，不等质量与等质量情形下可观测量的有界性表现显著不同。本研究为 pion-kaon、pion-nucleon 等物理过程的自举分析提供了起点。

Abstract: We initiate the study of two-to-two scattering amplitudes involving two distinct scalar particles in $d = 4$ spacetime dimensions using the primal S-matrix bootstrap. We impose a $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$ global symmetry under which the two particles are the lightest ones carrying charges $(-,+)$ and $(+,-)$, guaranteeing their absolute stability and absence of triangular anomalous thresholds. For unequal masses, the presence of a pseudo-physical cut whose discontinuity is not directly constrained by physical unitarity qualitatively changes the bootstrap problem. We find several observables to be unbounded, while others obey non-trivial one-sided bounds or become bounded once another observable is fixed. In the equal-mass limit, where the pseudo-physical region disappears, two-sided bounds are recovered. As a proof of concept, we show that supplying information about the pseudo-physical discontinuity through an Omnès parametrisation also restores boundedness. Our results provide a starting point for future studies of physical processes such as pion-kaon and pion-nucleon scattering.

</details>


### [192] [Cosmological Collider Signals From a Triangle Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09288)
*Zhehan Qin,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文首次给出了暴胀双谱与三谱中由大质量标量三角圈图（triangle loop）产生的前导解析宇宙对撞机信号（cosmological collider signals），并发现这些信号可由带单一有效收缩耦合（pinched coupling）的泡泡图（bubble diagram）重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角圈图信号在宇宙对撞机模型中频繁出现，但其解析形式因多重技术挑战而一直未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合方向切割规则（directional cutting rule）与部分 Mellin-Barnes 表示（partial Mellin-Barnes representations），在软极限（soft limit）下求解双谱与三谱的圈图信号。

Result: 获得了三谱的局域与非局域信号，并导出了挤压双谱（squeezed bispectrum）的前导信号；所有信号均可由对应泡泡图配合单一有效收缩耦合重现。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至超越前导阶的信号及任意内禀质量、自旋与相互作用的情形，并为从一般宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）中系统提取解析圈图信号提供了途径。

Abstract: Triangular loop signals appear frequently in cosmological collider models but their analytical forms remain unknown due to multiple technical challenges. Here, we determine for the first time the leading analytical cosmological collider signals generated by a massive scalar triangle loop in inflationary bispectrum and trispectrum. Combining a directional cutting rule with partial Mellin-Barnes representations, we obtain both the local and nonlocal trispectrum signals in a soft limit and derive the leading signal in the squeezed bispectrum. Remarkably, all these signals are reproduced by the corresponding bubble diagram with a single effective pinched coupling. Signals beyond leading order and cases with arbitrary internal masses, spins, and interactions can be generated within the same framework. Our approach also provides a systematic route to extracting analytical loop signals from general cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum Elliptic Cohomology From Four-Dimensional Minimal Supersymmetric Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09298)
*Ilka Brunner,Peng Cheng,Hans Jockers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 N=1 超对称规范理论在 $T^2 \times D^2$ 时空上的非微扰涡旋配分函数，并提出其构成量子椭圆上同调（quantum elliptic cohomology）的一种实现。通过对 Handsaw quiver 簇的等变局部化计算，得到被差分算子湮灭的涡旋配分函数，并将其解释为 BPS 面缺陷间的 Ward 恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注四维 N=1 超对称规范理论在特定时空几何上的非微扰涡旋扇区，试图为该配分函数寻找数学表述，即以量子椭圆上同调刻画涡旋配分函数。同时强调相对四超荷的低维规范理论，反常（anomaly）在此四维表述中起关键作用。

Method: 针对特定 U(1) 规范理论，采用等变局部化（equivariant localization）方法，作用于实现非微扰涡旋扇区模空间的 Handsaw quiver 簇，显式计算涡旋配分函数。并将所得配分函数与差分算子、N=(0,2) BPS 面缺陷（surface defect）的 Ward 恒等式以及 quasimap 模空间对应的 Thom 层（Thom sheaf）几何性质相联系。

Result: 显式求得的涡旋配分函数被差分算子湮灭，这些算子被解释为 N=(0,2) BPS 面缺陷之间的 Ward 恒等式。反常在该四维配分函数的一致表述中扮演必要角色，并与相关 quasimap 模空间的 Thom 层几何性质相关联。

Conclusion: 论文提出以等变椭圆上同调（equivariant elliptic cohomology）构建涡旋配分函数的数学表述，其中规范理论反常对应 Thom 层的几何性质。基于显式计算，进一步探讨在一般量子椭圆上同调数学理论中，quasimap 模空间上是否存在"虚拟结构层（virtual structure sheaf）"。

Abstract: In this paper, we study non-perturbative vortex partition functions of four-dimensional N=1 supersymmetric gauge theories on the space-time geometry $T^2 \times D^2$, and we propose that these partition functions offer a notion of quantum elliptic cohomology. For particular U(1)-gauge theories we calculate these partition functions explicitly by applying equivariant localization methods to Handsaw quiver varieties that realize for this particular class of U(1)-gauge theories the moduli spaces of the non-perturbative vortex sectors. The determined vortex partition functions are annihilated by difference operators, which are interpreted as Ward identities among N=(0,2) BPS surface defects. Compared to lower dimensional gauge theories with four supercharges, anomalies play an essential role for a consistent formulation of the four-dimensional partition functions. In our proposal towards a mathematical formulation of the vortex partition functions in terms of equivariant elliptic cohomology, the gauge theory anomalies relate to geometric properties of the Thom sheaves corresponding to the relevant quasimap moduli spaces. Motivated by the explicit computations we reflect on the existence of a `virtual structure sheaf' on the moduli space of quasimaps for a general mathematical theory of quantum elliptic cohomology.

</details>


### [194] [One-point functions of the Lagrangian in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM with boundaries, defects and interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09303)
*Dongming He,Christoph F. Uhlemann,Paolo Vallarino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 中环境拉格朗日算符 $\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{L}}$ 在 Gaiotto-Witten 分类的 $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS 边界、缺陷与界面（interface）上的一点函数（one-point function），涵盖极大超对称 Janus 界面、双全息（double holography）相关 BCFT 及降低超对称的 Janus 界面，并给出对 $N$ 与 $λ$ 的精确依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaiotto-Witten 分类的 $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS 边界、缺陷与界面（含 Janus 界面与 BCFT）中，环境拉格朗日算符一点函数此前仅在部分极限下已知，且存在非重整化猜想（non-renormalization conjecture）待验证，含真实缺陷自由度的一般情形缺乏系统结果。

Method: 对 Janus 界面，将此前捕捉特定极限下 $\langle \mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{L}}\rangle$ 的结果推广至对 $N$ 与 $λ$ 的精确依赖；对一般含真实缺陷自由度的 $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Gaiotto-Witten 理论，采用超对称局域化（supersymmetric localization）推导一点函数。

Result: Janus 界面情形得到对 $N$ 和 $λ$ 的精确依赖，并确认了非重整化猜想；同时澄清了保持较少超对称的 Janus 变体（Janus variants）的近期结果。一般 Gaiotto-Witten 理论的一点函数在适当极限下与全息预言（holographic predictions）一致，且一般表现出非平凡的耦合依赖。

Conclusion: $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Gaiotto-Witten 边界、缺陷与界面中环境拉格朗日算符一点函数可被系统计算并确认非重整化猜想；其一般耦合依赖与非平凡，且在适当极限下与全息结果相符。

Abstract: We obtain one-point functions of the ambient Lagrangian $\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{L}}$ in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM with $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS boundaries, defects and interfaces classified by Gaiotto and Witten, which include maximally supersymmetric Janus interfaces and BCFTs relevant for double holography, as well as for Janus interfaces with reduced supersymmetry. For Janus interfaces we extend recent results capturing $\langle \mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{L}}\rangle$ in certain limits to exact dependence on $N$ and $λ$, and confirm a non-renormalization conjecture. We also clarify recent results for Janus variants preserving less supersymmetry. For general $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Gaiotto-Witten theories with genuine defect degrees of freedom we derive one-point functions using supersymmetric localization, confirm that the results agree with holographic predictions in the appropriate limits, and show that the one-point functions in general exhibit non-trivial coupling dependence.

</details>


### [195] [Determinantal representation of colored HOMFLY for double braids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09437)
*Ya. Kononov,A. Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文从已知的微分展开（differential expansions）出发，将扭转结与反平行双辫结（twist and antiparallel double-braid knots）在矩形表示（rectangular representations）[r^s] 下的 HOMFLY-PT 多项式表示为 r×r 与 s×s 矩阵的行列式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KNTZ 公式已给出扭转结与双辫结的演化描述，但缺乏以行列式形式统一表达 HOMFLY-PT 多项式的简洁框架。该工作旨在为结多项式（knot polynomials）向 KP/Toda τ 函数（KP/Toda τ-functions）的可能推广提供起点。

Method: 先对八字结（figure-eight knot）给出初等推导，再对扭转结与双辫结演化的 KNTZ 公式进行重求和（re-sum）。所得矩阵由单钩演化系数（single-hook evolution coefficients）与显式的表示相关权重（representation-dependent weights）构造。

Result: HOMFLY-PT 多项式在矩形表示 [r^s] 下被成功表示为 r×r 和 s×s 矩阵的行列式，矩阵元由单钩演化系数与显式表示相关权重确定。

Conclusion: 该行列式公式为研究结多项式向 KP/Toda τ 函数推广提供了出发点；向非矩形表示（non-rectangular representations）及更一般结族的推广仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Starting from the known differential expansions, we express the HOMFLY-PT polynomials of twist and antiparallel double-braid knots in rectangular representations \([r^s]\) as determinants of \(r\times r\) and \(s\times s\) matrices. We first give an elementary derivation for the figure-eight knot and then re-sum the KNTZ formulas for twist and double-braid evolution. The resulting matrices are constructed from single-hook evolution coefficients and explicit representation-dependent weights. These formulas provide a starting point for investigating possible extensions of knot polynomials to KP/Toda \(τ\)-functions. Extensions to non-rectangular representations and to more general knot families remain open problems.

</details>


### [196] [On general background of quantum non-invertible symmetry in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09550)
*Jin Chen,Qiang Jia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究通过平直规范化（flat gauging）非阿贝尔对称性 $G$ 所得到的二维理论 $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ 中的对偶量子对称性 $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$，建立了在任意非可逆对称性背景下计算配分函数的通用框架及其逆变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画规范化非阿贝尔对称性后出现的对偶量子对称性 $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$ 的理论结构，并解决在该非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）的各种背景下如何计算配分函数的问题。

Method: 发展了一个通用框架（general framework），用原理论 $\mathfrak{T}_G$ 的配分函数来表达 $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ 在任意非可逆对称性背景下的配分函数，其中背景由 $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$ 的拓扑缺陷网络（topological defect networks）表示；同时推导了逆变换（inverse transformation）。对 Lie 群 $G$，规范化理解为平直规范化（flat gauging）。

Result: 推导出以 $\mathfrak{T}_G$ 配分函数表示的 $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ 配分函数计算公式，并给出其逆变换，将 $G$ 背景下 $\mathfrak{T}_G$ 的配分函数表示为 $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ 的配分函数。框架在若干例子中得到检验，包括具有无重数（multiplicity-free）与高重数融合规则（higher-multiplicity fusion rules）的有限群，并讨论了向紧致 Lie 群（compact Lie groups）的形式化推广，重点为 $\mathrm{Rep}(SU(2))$。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为计算非可逆对称性背景下对偶理论 $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ 的配分函数提供了系统方法，并通过有限群与 $\mathrm{Rep}(SU(2))$ 等例子验证了其适用性，表明该框架可形式化推广至紧致 Lie 群情形。

Abstract: We study the dual quantum symmetry $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$ in the two-dimensional theory $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ obtained by gauging a non-abelian symmetry $G$ of $\mathfrak{T}_G$, where for Lie groups $G$ the gauging is understood as flat gauging. We develop a general framework for computing partition functions of $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$ in arbitrary non-invertible symmetry backgrounds, represented by topological defect networks of $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$, in terms of the partition functions of the original theory $\mathfrak{T}_G$. We also derive the inverse transformation, expressing partition functions of $\mathfrak{T}_G$ in $G$ backgrounds in terms of those of $\widetilde{\mathfrak{T}}_{\mathrm{Rep}(G)}$. We test the framework in several examples, including finite groups with multiplicity-free and higher-multiplicity fusion rules, and discuss a formal extension to compact Lie groups, focusing on $\mathrm{Rep}(SU(2))$.

</details>


### [197] [The Geometry of Gravitational Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09954)
*Jelle Hartong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在渐近平坦真空时空的未来零无穷附近引入最一般的 Carroll 几何，证明近边界径向展开可由对共形 Carroll 代数（conformal Carroll algebra）的规范构造（gauging）组织，其唯一非零曲率为与特殊共形生成元相关的 K-曲率（K-curvatures）。K-曲率的消失定义渐近真空时空，其层级变换给出四类时空分类，并与 Weyl 张量分量、Carroll 助推反常及 BMS 流相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在以 Carroll 几何与规范理论的语言统一描述未来零无穷附近的真空引力自由度，特别是将边界的真空（软）剪切（soft shear）与渐近对称性结构联系起来。通过 K-曲率这一几何量，作者试图为渐近真空条件与辐射性分类提供协变表述。

Method: 在四维渐近平坦真空时空的未来零无穷附近赋以最一般允许的 Carroll 几何，并将近边界径向展开（至某阶）用对共形 Carroll 代数做规范（gauging）所得的联络来组织。以 K-曲率的消失定义渐近真空时空，据此构造真空软剪切的边界（Carroll）协变表达式，并分析 K-曲率在 Carroll 助推下的层级变换。

Result: 分析表明，规范构造中唯一非零曲率为 K-曲率；其消失给出渐近真空时空，并可用两个边界 Carroll 标量场（Carroll scalar fields）表示真空软剪切。K-曲率在 Carroll 助推下呈层级式相互变换，由此得到四类时空分类：真空、强非辐射、弱非辐射与辐射时空。K-曲率对应 1/r 展开领头阶 10 个 Weyl 张量分量中的 5 个，且其中之一等于新近发现的 Carroll 助推反常（Carroll boost anomaly）。

Conclusion: 边界能量-动量-新闻复量（energy-momentum-news complex）的 Bondi 损失方程可改写为涉及另一能量-动量张量的形式，该张量能量通量为零、无迹，其非守恒完全由 K-曲率刻画；将其与 Carroll 共形 Killing 矢量（Carroll conformal Killing vector）缩并即可得到 BMS 流（BMS currents）。

Abstract: We consider 4-dimensional asymptotically flat vacuum spacetimes near future null infinity endowed with the most general allowable Carroll geometry. We show that the near-boundary radial expansion (to a certain order) can be organised in terms of connections that can be obtained by gauging the conformal Carroll algebra. The only non-vanishing curvatures in this gauging procedure are those that are associated with the special conformal generators and we will refer to these as the $K$-curvatures. The vanishing of these $K$-curvatures defines an asymptotic vacuum spacetime and we use this to construct a boundary (i.e. Carroll) covariant expression for the vacuum (soft) shear in terms of two boundary Carroll scalar fields. The $K$-curvatures transform in a hierarchical fashion into one another under Carroll boosts. This leads to a classification of 4 types of spacetimes: vacuum, strongly and weakly non-radiative, and radiative spacetimes. It is shown that the $K$-curvatures correspond to 5 of the 10 Weyl tensor components at leading order in their $1/r$ expansion. We furthermore observe that one of the $K$-curvatures is equal to the recently found Carroll boost anomaly. Finally, we show that the Bondi loss equations for the boundary energy-momentum-news complex can be cast into a form involving another energy-momentum tensor with vanishing energy flux that is traceless and whose non-conservation is entirely captured by the $K$-curvatures. The BMS currents can be obtained by contracting this latter energy-momentum tensor with a Carroll conformal Killing vector.

</details>


### [198] [Entanglement Entropy of Interacting Scalar Theories on Fuzzy Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10027)
*A. Allouche,D. Dou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $(2+1)$ 维模糊空间（fuzzy space，即模糊球面与模糊圆盘）中标量场的 Rényi 熵（Rényi entropy）与纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）如何受自相互作用影响，通过格林函数方法计算 $\\lambda\\phi^4$ 相互作用的一阶微扰修正。结果显示相互作用修正具有体相（bulk）广延贡献，与自由理论的面定律（area law）形成对比，并伴随显著的红外（IR）敏感性及可能的 UV/IR 混合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由场理论中纠缠熵由纠缠边界附近的自由度主导并服从面定律，但自相互作用是否会改变这一几何局域性尚不清楚。本文旨在探究自相互作用对标量场纠缠熵的影响，并借助模糊空间这一非对易几何框架考察其与 UV/IR 混合（UV/IR mixing）的可能联系。

Method: 在空间截面为模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）与模糊圆盘（fuzzy disc）的 $(2+1)$ 维时空上，采用格林函数方法（Green's function approach）计算 $\\lambda\\phi^4$ 相互作用所诱导的一阶微扰修正。对模糊球面情形，通过投影去除零模（zero mode）以分离并消解相关的红外发散；同时分析交换连续极限与 Moyal 平面（Moyal plane）极限。

Result: 与自由理论的面定律行为不同，相互作用修正给出广延的体相贡献，来自整个模糊空间内的自由度。模糊球面上修正表现出与零模相关的强红外敏感性，去除零模后可获得物理上有意义的量。在交换连续极限下，相互作用修正与自由熵具有相同程度的紫外（UV）发散，但不服从纯面定律；在 Moyal 平面极限下则呈现独特的红外发散。

Conclusion: 自相互作用引入的体相广延特征源于模糊空间整体自由度，打破了自由理论中纠缠熵的边界局域性，并可能与非对易量子场论（noncommutative quantum field theory）中著名的 UV/IR 混合现象相关联。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of self-interactions on the Rényi and entanglement entropies of a scalar field on $(2+1)$-dimensional spacetimes, whose spatial sections are modeled by fuzzy spaces, specifically the fuzzy sphere and the fuzzy disc. We compute the first-order perturbative correction induced by a $λφ^4$ interaction using the Green's function approach. In contrast to the free theory, where the entanglement entropy is dominated by degrees of freedom near the entangling boundary and obeys an area law, we find that the interaction correction has an extensive bulk contribution, receiving significant contributions from degrees of freedom throughout the fuzzy space. For the fuzzy sphere, the correction exhibits strong infrared sensitivity associated with the zero mode. We isolate and resolve this zero-mode IR divergence by projecting out the zero mode, thereby obtaining a physically meaningful quantity. In the commutative continuum limit, the interaction correction has the same degree of UV divergence as the free entropy but does not obey a pure area law. Furthermore, we analyze the Moyal plane limit, where the interaction correction exhibits a distinct IR divergence. We discuss the physical origin of these extensive bulk features and examine their possible connection to the celebrated UV/IR mixing phenomenon in noncommutative quantum field theories.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum tunneling from perturbation theory revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10134)
*Hiroshi Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新检验了 Suzuki-Yasuta 提出的通过真空泡图（vacuum bubble diagrams）微扰系数提取假真空（false vacuum）衰变率的公式，利用 D=2、N=1 情形下至九圈的七个非平凡微扰系数，发现引入两个可调参数后虚部随微扰阶数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Suzuki 与 Yasuta 在 1990 年代末提出的紧凑公式在 D=1（量子力学）中表现良好，但在 D≥2 时有效性不明，原因在于此前仅有 D=2 的前三个微扰系数且结果无收敛迹象。本文旨在借助更多微扰系数重新检验该方法的适用性。

Method: 采用 Serone、Spada 与 Villadoro 给出的 D=2、N=1 情形下前七个非平凡微扰系数（至九圈），在有限阶截断的 Borel 变换（Borel transform）中引入两个可调参数，并将真空能量密度 Borel 变换的间断性与 Borel 积分虚部相等同。

Result: 随着微扰阶数增加，虚部收敛，最后几阶结果在百分之几范围内一致。在中间耦合区间 3≲g̃≲8，该方法给出的虚部与含单圈行列式（one-loop determinant）的领头阶半经典近似（semi-classical approximation）相当接近，比 Malatesta、Parisi 与 Rizzo 计算的含两圈修正的半经典结果大 10%–20%。

Conclusion: Suzuki-Yasuta 方法在 D=2、N=1 情形下的有效性得到支持，虚部在增加微扰阶数时呈现收敛，且与半经典近似结果量级一致；但两可调参数的引入及与两圈修正结果的偏差仍需进一步审视。

Abstract: In the late 1990s, Suzuki and Yasuta proposed a compact formula that extracts the decay rate per unit volume of a false vacuum in the $D$-dimensional $O(N)$ $λ(φ^2)^2$ theory with an unbounded potential from conventional perturbative coefficients of the vacuum energy density, i.e., vacuum bubble diagrams. The idea was to identify the imaginary part arising from the Borel integral along the discontinuity of the Borel transform with that of the vacuum energy density. While the formula works quite well for~$D=1$, i.e., quantum mechanics, its validity remained unclear for~$D\geq2$ because only the first three perturbative coefficients for~$D=2$ were available and the result showed no sign of convergence. In the present paper, we reexamine this approach using the first seven nontrivial perturbative coefficients (up to nine loops) for~$D=2$ and~$N=1$ obtained by Serone, Spada, and~Villadoro. Introducing two tunable parameters in the finite-order truncated Borel transform following these authors, we find that the imaginary part converges as the perturbative order increases, with the last few orders agreeing to within a few percent. In the intermediate range of the coupling constant, $3\lesssim\widetilde{g}\lesssim8$, this approach yields an imaginary part rather close to the leading-order semi-classical approximation with the one-loop determinant; it is $10$--$20\%$ larger than the semi-classical result with the two-loop correction computed by Malatesta, Parisi, and~Rizzo.

</details>


### [200] [Unorientable supersymmetric compactifications of M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10272)
*Ilarion V. Melnikov,Ruben Minasian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论 II 型弦（type II string）的轨形紧化（orbifold compactification），其中轨形作用结合了基流形（base manifold）上的自由作用与左行 Ramond 扇区的 $(-1)^{F_{\text{L}}}$ 对称性，此类背景可保持最小超对称（minimal supersymmetry）并可提升为光滑无定向流形（smooth unorientable manifold）上的 M 理论紧化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造既能保持最小超对称、又可在 M 理论框架下获得几何解释的 II 型弦紧化背景。摘要未进一步说明具体物理动机或待解决的问题。

Method: 采用轨形紧化方法，将基流形上的自由作用与 $(-1)^{F_{\text{L}}}$ 对称性（对左行 Ramond 扇区作用为 -1）相结合。摘要未涉及具体计算细节或模型构造步骤。

Result: 此类弦背景能够保持最小超对称，并可提升（lift）为光滑无定向流形上的 M 理论紧化。摘要未给出具体维度、模空间或谱结构等结果。

Conclusion: 所讨论的轨形紧化提供了一类具有最小超对称的 II 型弦背景，且与光滑无定向流形上的 M 理论紧化相对应。摘要未展开其唯象含义或后续应用。

Abstract: We discuss orbifold compactifications of the type II string, where the orbifold combines a free action on a base manifold and the $(-1)^{\boldsymbol{F}_{\text{L}}}$ symmetry which acts by -1 on left-moving Ramond sectors. Such backgrounds can preserve minimal supersymmetry and lift to M-theory compactification on smooth unorientable manifolds.

</details>


### [201] [An Effective $S$-Matrix Approach to Low-Frequency Waveforms from Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10329)
*Katsuki Aoki,Feng-Yin Cheng,Andrea Cristofoli,Yu-tin Huang,Hyun Jeong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在领头阶之外，发展了黑洞并合低频引力波形的在壳（on-shell）描述，将强耦合并合过程视为有效硬散射（S-matrix）数据，用软定理（soft theorems）与KMOC形式组织长波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞并合过程处于强耦合区域，难以直接求解，因此需要在其长波极限下端建立一个在壳、有效场论式的描述框架，以超越领头阶地刻画低频引力波形。

Method: 将并合视为硬S-矩阵数据，采用软定理与KMOC形式（KMOC formalism）组织长波响应；在次领头阶（next-to-leading order）计算单圈辐射振幅及相应引力子割（graviton cut），并在完整KMOC in-in可观测量中进行比较。

Result: 量子软定理在次领头阶包含经典软定理所没有的对数项，这些额外项在完整KMOC in-in可观测量中与引力子割相互抵消，仅留下与引力拖曳（gravitational drag）及早时加速对应的经典对数贡献。此外还识别出残留反冲与Christodoulou非线性记忆（non-linear memory）给出的1/ω修正。

Conclusion: 低频可及的并合信息存在层级结构：对数尾项仅依赖渐近硬数据，反冲探测总辐射动量，非线性记忆则探测辐射的角分布。

Abstract: We develop an on-shell description of low-frequency gravitational waveforms from black-hole mergers beyond leading order. Treating the strongly coupled merger as effective hard $S$-matrix data, we organize its long-wavelength response using soft theorems and the KMOC formalism. At next-to-leading order, the quantum soft theorem contains logarithmic terms absent from the classical soft theorem. We show that these extra terms cancel in the full KMOC in-in observable between the one-loop radiative amplitude and the corresponding graviton cut, leaving precisely the classical logarithmic contributions associated with gravitational drag and early-time acceleration. We also identify the $1/ω$ corrections from remnant recoil and Christodoulou non-linear memory. These results reveal a hierarchy of merger information accessible at low frequency: logarithmic tails depend only on asymptotic hard data, recoil probes total radiated momentum, while non-linear memory probes the angular distribution of the emitted radiation.

</details>


### [202] [Null String Holography, Null Strings Probe Projective Boundary of Their Target Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10432)
*M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,H. Yavartanoo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了极小零弦理论（minimal null string theory）在背景流形 $\mathcal{M}$ 上与其投影边界 $\mathcal{B}_{_{\text{P}}}=\partial_{_{\text{P}}}\mathcal{M}$ 上的 ILST 理论经典等价，并猜想该等价在量子层面亦成立，称之为零弦全息（null string holography）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立零弦理论与 ILST 理论之间的对偶关系，类比著名的 AdS/CFT 对应（AdS/CFT correspondence），将全息原理推广至零弦框架，从而为理解不同背景时空（包括黑洞）的投影边界物理提供新视角。

Method: 方法为经典层面的理论证明：将背景流形 $\mathcal{M}$ 上的极小零弦理论与投影边界 $\partial_{_{\text{P}}}\mathcal{M}$ 上的 ILST 理论进行等价性论证，并结合 AdS$_D$ 庞加莱坐标片（Poincaré patch）、dS$_D$ 宇宙学坐标片及 $D$ 维闵可夫斯基空间（Minkowski space）等具体背景展开分析。

Result: 结果显示：AdS$_D$ 庞加莱坐标片上的经典零弦理论由 $D-1$ 维闵可夫斯基背景上的 ILST 描述；dS$_D$ 宇宙学坐标片上的零弦由 $D-1$ 维平坦欧几里得空间（flat Euclidean space）上的 ILST 支配；$D$ 维闵可夫斯基空间中的零弦，其投影边界依坐标片不同可为 dS$_{D-1}$、$D-1$ 维卡罗尔空间（Carrollian space）及 $D-1$ 维双曲面（hyperboloid），分别对应类空渐近边界 $\iota^0$、渐近零边界 $\mathcal{I}^\pm$ 与类时无穷 $\iota^\pm$。

Conclusion: 作者猜想该经典等价在量子层面同样成立（即零弦全息），并讨论了其在黑洞背景中的物理意义——此时投影边界包含视界（horizons），暗示全息对偶可能延伸至包含视界的时空结构。

Abstract: We prove that the \textit{minimal null string theory} on background manifold ${\cal M}$ is classically equivalent to the ILST theory on the projective boundary of $\mathcal M$, ${\mathcal B}_{_{\text{P}}}={\partial_{_{\text{P}}}\mathcal{M}}$. We conjecture that this equivalence also holds as quantum level, which we dub \textit{null string holography}. In particular, we show classical null string theory on Poincaré patch of $D$ dimensional Anti de Sitter (AdS$_D$) background is described by ILST on $D-1$ dimensional Minkowski background, paralleling the celebrated AdS/CFT. Null strings on cosmological patch of dS$_D$ is governed by ILST on $D-1$ dimensional flat Euclidean space. For null strings on $D$ dimensional Minkowski space, depending on the coordinate patch used, the projective boundary where the ``dual'' ILST theory resides, can by dS$_{D-1}$, $D-1$ dimensional Carrollian space and $D-1$ dimensional hyperboloid, respectively including spacelike asymptotic boundary $ι^0$, asymptotic null boundaries ${\cal I}^\pm$ and timelike infinities $ι^\pm$. We comment on physical implications of the null string holography on black hole backgrounds whose projective boundary includes horizons.

</details>


### [203] [For Whom Does Bell Hold?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10535)
*Daniel Green,Kshitij Gupta,Qiya Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出将统计关联（correlations）推广为一种贝尔型检验（Bell-type test），用以区分封闭量子系统与经典哈密顿系统的真空涨落（vacuum fluctuations）所产生的时间演化特征，并指出量子真空关联普遍时间无关且受能隙控制，而经典系统在物理频率处出现极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔不等式（Bell's inequalities）的违背是局域确定性系统中非经典行为的明确信号，但在宇宙学等许多物理情形下，可观测物理量无法构造贝尔型检验。宇宙结构可能源自量子真空涨落（quantum vacuum fluctuations），而所有观测却呈现经典特征，因此需要寻找可区分量子与经典时间演化的替代性统计信号。

Method: 作者探索并推广以关联模式作为判断时间演化本质的贝尔型检验，对比分析经历哈密顿演化（Hamiltonian evolution）的封闭系统量子真空涨落与经典哈密顿系统所产生的统计关联特征，并考察接近共振极点时演化的行为及其退相位（dephasing）过程。

Result: 量子真空涨落产生经典哈密顿系统无法模仿的独特关联：量子真空中的关联通常时间无关，并由基态与激发态之间的能隙（energy gap）控制；经典系统则在物理频率处表现出量子真空中不存在的表观极点（apparent poles）。接近极点时，演化由共振哈密顿量主导，产生时间依赖关联并最终通过退相位衰减。

Conclusion: 该关联行为不同于其他量子演化信号，并可应用于量子光学（quantum optics）、量子行走（quantum walks）与量子搜索（quantum search），表明以关联作为贝尔型检验具有推广价值。

Abstract: Violations of Bell's inequalities offer a definitive signal of non-classical (quantum) behavior in local deterministic systems. Yet, in many physical settings where quantum mechanics is expected to play an important role, one cannot construct a Bell-type test using the available observables. Cosmology offers one concrete example, where cosmic structure may have originated from quantum vacuum fluctuations yet all observations are effectively classical. Nevertheless, recent work suggests that quantum and classical time evolution may still be differentiated by the pattern of statistical correlations of these observables. In this paper, we explore and generalize the use of correlations as Bell-type test of the nature of time evolution. We show that quantum vacuum fluctuations of closed systems undergoing Hamiltonian evolution produce unique correlations that are not mimicked by classical Hamiltonian systems. Correlations in the quantum vacuum are generally time-independent and are controlled by the energy gap between the ground and exited states. Classical systems exhibit apparent poles at physical frequencies that do not arise in the quantum vacuum. As one approaches the poles, the evolution becomes dominated by a resonant Hamiltonian giving rise to time dependent correlations that eventually decay through dephasing. Finally, we show that this behavior is distinct from other signals of quantum evolution, including applications to quantum optics, quantum walks, and quantum search.

</details>
